- Packaged release smoke now builds far enough to produce `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe`, MSI, and NSIS setup artifacts; local `tauri build` still stops at updater signing when `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` is absent. The compiled release exe was verified as Windows GUI subsystem and started successfully from isolated `target/packaged-exe-smoke` without exiting. A fresh latest-vanilla live launch smoke passed after clearing stale repo-owned smoke output that had filled C:. Release verification is now stricter: updater manifest generation and bundle verification reject stale NSIS `.sig` files older than the setup exe, require `latest.json` to be newer than the setup/signature artifacts, and require the manifest signature to match the setup `.sig`, preventing stale local updater artifacts from producing a false green. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `node scripts/run-live-winterpack-smoke.mjs vanillaLaunch --fresh --root target/live-smoke/vanilla`, `npm run build`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher missing_launch_artifact_errors_trigger_automatic_repair -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core java -- --test-threads=1`, and a release exe start/subsystem smoke. `npm run verify:tauri-bundles` and `node scripts/generate-tauri-updater-manifest.mjs` intentionally fail against the current local bundle because the setup exe is newer than the stale `.sig`; rerun a signed `tauri build` with `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` before using those bundle artifacts.
- Discover FTB provider copy no longer uses code-shaped `provider metadata exposes...` wording. The FTB plan now says compatible packs install automatically when the pack provides the files the launcher needs, keeping the normal Discover screen friendlier while preserving the existing native provider behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Import custom-folder onboarding is quieter on first open. The custom path field no longer shows `Paste a profile folder path first.` before the user interacts; the `Review folder` action stays disabled until a non-empty path is entered, and quoted Windows paths still normalize before review planning. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen exposes scan action|import screen can review a custom folder path|library new profile action creates a preview profile|empty launcher guides first profile setup"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Library profile rows are less cluttered for normal users. Optional profile metadata now appears only when configured: window size, default server, and last-played chips are hidden when absent instead of showing placeholder text like `Default window` or `No default server`. Version, loader, and memory remain visible, and advanced Java/JVM details stay out of the row summary. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|import screen plans a preview migration"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Dev/stable release-channel configuration now updates the Tauri window title along with the product name, bundle identifier, version, and updater endpoint. Dev packages get a `TheBoysLauncher Dev` window title and stable packages reset to `TheBoysLauncher`, making side-by-side testing and user-facing channel identity clearer. The updater manifest generator and bundle verifier also reject mismatched manifest/channel pairs such as `latest-dev.json` on the stable channel. The offline release-channel verifier covers both transitions and the mismatch guard. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Discover install-link parsing now accepts more Prism/MultiMC provider aliases around the already-implemented native provider matrix. Modrinth app links may use `pack=`, CurseForge app links may use `modId=`/`pack=`, FTB Legacy and FTB Private accept dashed platform names (`ftb-legacy`, `ftb-private`), and Technic app links preserve `version`/`versionId`/`build` when present. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover pasted launcher"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Import review/execution now distinguishes a launcher instance root from the actual Minecraft data root. When an imported Prism/MultiMC/FTB-style profile stores saves, mods, config, options, resource packs, screenshots, or shader packs under `.minecraft/`, review rows and execution copy from that nested folder while keeping the original instance root for metadata/bounds. This fixes imports that previously detected the profile but reviewed zero files or copied root-level launcher metadata instead of real game data. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core import_plan_ -- --test-threads=1` and `cargo test -p launcher-core scanner_detects_supported_launcher_roots -- --test-threads=1`.
- Import scanning now includes the Prism-style FTB App import path by looking under `.ftba/instances`, exposing detected packs as `FTB App` candidates with the new `ftb_app` import kind. The scanner also reads common FTB `instance.json` fields such as `instanceName`, `synopsis`, `modLoaderType`, and `mcVersion` so imported FTB App packs get friendly names, summaries, loader labels, and Minecraft versions in the review flow. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core scanner_detects_supported_launcher_roots -- --test-threads=1`.
- Release-channel manifest validation now accepts only the two supported updater manifest names, `latest.json` and `latest-dev.json`. Both the manifest generator and bundle verifier reject accidental names such as `latest-beta.json`, preventing unsupported channels from passing CI or publish verification. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Native desktop friend-request failures no longer create fake queued outgoing requests when the friends service rejects or cannot process the request. Hosted sign-in failures still show the Sign in recovery action, real successful requests still add the outbound row, and browser preview keeps its local queue behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "friends screen can send requests|native friend request failure|hosted friend requests require|hosted friends search requires"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Native desktop Import scan/review failures no longer show fake web-preview profiles or fake review plans. Manual `Find profiles` now clears Import candidates and surfaces a real unavailable message when `scan_imports` fails in native mode, and `Review` leaves the modal closed when `plan_profile_import` fails instead of showing preview copy items. Browser preview fallback behavior is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen|native import"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Import review rows now hide raw destination paths for normal copy items and show `Ready to bring over` instead. Matching-file rows still show the friendly choice summary and controls, so users can review import decisions without being dropped into AppData/profile paths by default.
- Dev/stable updater manifests now carry an explicit `channel` field. The release manifest generator writes `stable` or `dev`, the bundle verifier rejects missing or mismatched channels, and the Settings update checker refuses a manifest that declares the wrong channel before it can open an installer. The renderer still tolerates older manifests without a channel for compatibility, but every newly generated release manifest is channel-checked by CI.
- Discover provider-link compatibility now accepts Prism/MultiMC-style install links using the older `flame` platform alias for CurseForge, routing them through the native CurseForge provider installer with exact project/file ids. Plain CurseForge pack pages also route to the native provider installer now, with launcher-core resolving the latest downloadable export when no file id is supplied.
- Hosted friends-service status now hides configuration-shaped native text in normal/advanced UI. A hosted outage such as `Hosted friends service is configured at ... but is not reachable...` renders as `Friends service is unavailable right now. Minecraft still works.`, while the health-check URL remains visible separately for troubleshooting and hosted clients still do not start a local backend. Verified with focused hosted/social Playwright coverage.
- Activity now strips the last visible `profile repair/setup` prefix from cached automatic launch setup failures. Both old `Automatic profile repair before launch failed:` rows and current `Automatic profile setup before launch failed:` rows render as `Automatic setup before launch failed: ...`, keeping Activity/overview copy focused on what the launcher is doing instead of internal repair terminology. Verified with focused Activity Playwright coverage.
- Discover now frames provider support more honestly for production use. CurseForge and Modrinth are labeled `Recommended`, compatible ATLauncher/FTB/FTB Legacy/Technic paths keep their working native install routes but explain that older/provider-specific formats may not prepare automatically, and the side summary now says `Recommended first` instead of implying every visible source has the same reliability. Readiness pills also use distinct Clean Dark status treatments for recommended/search/compatible/private providers. Verified with focused Discover Playwright coverage, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Settings now keeps its save/status strip immediately visible at the 1366x768 desktop baseline by moving `Settings save status` under the page heading and making it sticky inside the Settings scroll area. Web-preview Home also no longer silently probes `/dev/sessions` without a real Minecraft/backend session, removing the 403 console noise seen during rendered Settings QA while preserving explicit Friends/native presence behavior. Browser plugin runtime failed with `failed to write kernel assets`, so rendered QA used Playwright against `http://127.0.0.1:1420/`; the 1366x768 screenshot confirmed the save strip was visible and console errors/warnings were clean. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "home screen renders the social launcher shell|settings render memory|settings save status tracks privacy changes"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Runtime browser-preview mock wording is now gone from the frontend source. Activity preview history/status now says `Preview activity history ready`, `Preview game is running`, and related game-log actions explain when the desktop app is required; generic native-action, pack-install, account-load, and profile-setup browser fallbacks no longer use `mocked in web preview` wording. Verified with a frontend source search, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|settings can manage preview Minecraft session|library profile launch actions hide manual repair|discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Discover All Sources now interleaves provider result sets before truncating instead of appending one full provider at a time. This keeps the already-implemented CurseForge/Modrinth/ATLauncher/FTB/FTB Legacy/Technic discovery methods visible as a mixed feed when users search all sources together. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core all_discover_search_interleaves_provider_results_before_truncating -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Library duplicate preview messages now name the copied profile. Preview duplicate creates the copy, focuses Library on it, and reports `<copy name> duplicated in preview` instead of `Profile duplicated in web preview`; native duplicate behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused native/preview duplicate Playwright coverage, a source search for the old string, and `npm run build`.
- Library hide/delete preview messages now name the profile and avoid generic web-preview wording. Preview `Hide from Library` reports `<name> hidden from Library in preview`, preview delete reports `<name> deleted in preview`, and the profile list updates as before; native archive/delete behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused Library removal Playwright coverage, a source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Profile-folder browser-preview fallback copy now explains the desktop boundary directly. Pack Details / More `Open folder` now reports `Opening profile folders requires the desktop app` in preview instead of mock wording; native `open_profile_folder` behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused Pack Details Playwright coverage, a source search for the old string, and `npm run build`.
- New Profile browser-preview creation now behaves and reads more like a real local create. The preview fallback adds the profile, focuses the Library search on the new profile name, and reports `<name> created in preview` instead of `Creating profile is mocked in web preview`; native create/prepare behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused profile-creation Playwright coverage, a source search for the old string, and `npm run build`.
- Launch diagnostics browser-preview fallback copy now describes the desktop requirement instead of mock behavior. Unsigned diagnostics says `Launch diagnostics require the desktop app`, and signed-in diagnostics says `Signed-in launch diagnostics require the desktop app`; native launch-command/diagnostics behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused launch-diagnostics Playwright coverage, a source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Settings save preview now behaves like a real local save instead of reporting a mock. Browser preview normalizes and stores the draft settings in the local snapshot, reports `Settings saved in preview`, and the native save fallback is now the generic `Saving settings failed`; real native `save_settings` behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused Settings save Playwright coverage, a source search for the old string, and `npm run build`.
- Settings friends-service preview copy now avoids mock/test wording. Browser-preview status checks say `Preview friends service status ready`, and local service Start/Stop actions say `Local friends service requires the desktop app`; native service start/stop/status behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused Settings service Playwright coverage, a source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Settings Java preview copy now reflects useful preview state instead of test/mock wording. Browser-preview Java discovery says `Preview Java runtime ready`, recommendations say `Preview Java recommendations loaded`, and preview install says `Preview Java runtime added`; the native Java discovery/recommend/install paths are unchanged. Verified with focused Java Settings Playwright coverage, a source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Preview Minecraft account Settings copy now reports the real preview state change instead of mock wording. `Use preview account` shows `Preview account ready for <name>`, preview sign-out shows `Signed out of Minecraft`, and native fallbacks now use generic account failure messages instead of `mocked in web preview`; old account mock strings remain only as negative test assertions. Verified with focused Settings/account Playwright coverage, a source search, and `npm run build`.
- Microsoft sign-in browser-preview fallback copy is now product-facing. The Sign in action says `Microsoft sign-in requires the desktop app`, and manual completion fallback says `Microsoft sign-in completion requires the desktop app`, replacing the remaining sign-in `mocked in web preview` wording while leaving native OAuth behavior unchanged. Verified with focused sign-in Playwright coverage, a source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Friend-join status/fallback copy now matches the Minecraft-account language. Authenticated friend joins keep saying `Joining <friend> with your Minecraft account` after the native launch queues instead of flickering to `Authenticated join queued`, and browser-preview friend joins now say `Friend joins require the desktop app`; the native server target and presence flows are unchanged. Verified with focused friend-join Playwright coverage, a source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Minecraft account launch/session copy is now less internal. Signed-in Play uses `Launching with your Minecraft account`, account refresh success says `Minecraft account ready for <name>`, and expired-account refresh status says `Minecraft account for <name> needs sign-in` instead of `authenticated profile` / `session` wording. Verified with focused signed-in launch Playwright coverage, a renderer source search for the old strings, and `npm run build`.
- Browser-preview Play fallback copy is now product-facing. Unsigned Play says `Game launches require the desktop app`, and signed-in preview Play says `Sign in launches require the desktop app`, replacing the developer-ish `mocked in web preview` wording while leaving real Tauri launch behavior unchanged. Verified with focused Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Authenticated friend-join status copy is now player-facing. When a saved Minecraft account is used for a friend join, the sidebar says `Joining <friend> with your Minecraft account` instead of exposing internal `stored session` wording; the native authenticated launch and presence paths are unchanged. Verified with focused friend-join Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Discover link install copy now matches the implemented provider behavior. The paste panel says `Install from link` instead of `Install from archive`, because it accepts provider pages/app links/private-code routes plus direct `.zip` / `.mrpack` archives; the native install paths are unchanged. Verified with focused Discover Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Settings save controls are less cluttered. The Memory card no longer has a duplicate `Save changes` button; all Settings changes now save through the persistent `Settings save status` bar, which already shows validation guidance and owns the single `Save settings` action. Verified with focused Settings Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Activity failed-install recovery now uses friendlier action copy. Failed pack-install rows say `Try again` and `Trying...` instead of `Retry` / `Retrying...`, matching the rest of the launch/setup recovery language while keeping the same native `install_pack` retry path. Verified with focused failed-install Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Launch setup recovery now catches asset launch-argument mismatch errors, including `asset launch argument --assetIndex requires --assetsDir`. Renderer-facing Activity/sidebar text sanitizes those failures to `Game files are missing. The launcher will set them up automatically.`, and native pre-launch auto-repair treats them like missing launch artifacts instead of surfacing a dead-end Play failure. Verified with focused Activity Playwright coverage, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher missing_launch_artifact_errors_trigger_automatic_repair -- --test-threads=1`, and `npm run build`.
- New Profile and Library Customize advanced loader dropdowns now only show loaders the launcher can prepare for the selected Minecraft version. Incompatible Forge/NeoForge choices are hidden instead of appearing as disabled `not available` options, with a short hint explaining that the list is compatibility-filtered; the existing automatic fallback to Vanilla on version changes is unchanged. Verified with focused profile-creation/customization Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Activity overview task progress now uses plainer status wording. The latest-task breakdown says `done` / `working` / `waiting`, and the file-progress summary says `Now: ...` instead of exposing internal `completed` / `active` / `Current:` labels; operation/event state handling is unchanged. Verified with focused Activity/pack-failure Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Import profile rows now use friendlier file-count copy. Known counts show `1 file to copy` / `N files to copy`, and unknown counts show `Files to copy will be checked during review` instead of `Importable files unknown`; import scan/review behavior is unchanged. Verified with focused Import Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Profile creation and Settings validation copy now uses friendlier guidance instead of form-schema wording. New profile validation says `Pick a Minecraft version`, `Use at least 512 MB of memory`, and related Settings validation says `Maximum memory needs to be at least the minimum memory` / `Enter an offline username`; validation rules and native save/create behavior are unchanged. Verified with focused profile/settings Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- CurseForge pasted pack pages now fail early with useful guidance instead of attempting a native install that may need the keyed CurseForge API. Exact CurseForge file links such as `/files/<fileId>`, project file links, app install links, and direct zip exports remain installable; plain pack pages now ask users to paste a specific Files/download link or zip export. Verified with focused Discover CurseForge Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Manage Accounts removal copy now distinguishes saved-account removal from signing out of Minecraft globally. Saved account rows use `Remove`, `Remove account`, and `Removing...`, while the confirmation still says the account is removed from this launcher only; the existing current-account sign-out path remains separate. Verified with focused Settings account Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Advanced profile launch-check details use friendlier labels. The on-screen preview now shows `Java app`, `Game folder`, `Launch options`, and `Launcher variables` instead of `Executable`, `Working directory`, `Arguments`, and `Environment`; copied troubleshooting text remains unchanged. Verified with focused launch-details/setup-recovery Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Advanced Settings Java recovery copy is clearer for non-technical users. The button that loads managed Java recommendations now says `Recommended Java` instead of the vague `Recommendations`, while Java controls remain hidden under Advanced. Verified with focused Settings Java Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Profile deletion copy now avoids saying shared Minecraft downloads are kept for faster `reinstalls`, which made successful deletion sound suspicious. The Library confirmation and native delete receipt now say shared downloads are kept for faster future installs; the underlying behavior still removes the managed profile and keeps the shared cache. Verified with focused Library delete Playwright coverage, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher delete_profile_success_message_explains_shared_cache_retention -- --test-threads=1`, and `npm run build`.
- Import profile copy is a little friendlier on the normal screen. Candidate rows now say `Version not detected` / `Loader not detected` when metadata is missing, and the collapsed source disclosure says `View folder path` instead of `View source details`; the import logic and reviewed copy plan are unchanged. Verified with focused import Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Discover pasted-link support now covers more Prism/provider shapes around the already-implemented native providers. Prism/TheBoysLauncher app links can route FTB Legacy archives and FTB Private codes, and pasted ATLauncher, FTB, and Technic website links preserve explicit `/version` / `/versions` / `/build` path segments when present. Verified with focused Discover pasted-link Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Activity overview now uses `View steps` for the latest-task expander instead of the generic `View details`, keeping normal task progress language friendlier while preserving detailed step history behind the disclosure. Verified with focused Activity Playwright coverage for overview/history/setup progress and `npm run build`.
- Native Discover unsupported-format errors now avoid roadmap-ish `not supported yet` wording at the source. ATLauncher browser/manual-download entries, ATLauncher legacy file types, Technic legacy jar-mod metadata failures, FTB Legacy jar-mod archives, and unknown native provider requests now report that the launcher cannot install those formats automatically, while the renderer still sanitizes both old historical events and the new native messages into beginner-facing guidance. Verified with focused Discover provider failure/search Playwright coverage, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_legacy -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher completed_modpack_archive_event_uses_archive_operation -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Dev/stable update handoff failures from native trusted-download validation now show player-facing recovery copy. If the renderer accepts a manifest but native `open_external_url` refuses the installer URL, Settings shows `Launcher update download could not be verified. Check again before installing.` instead of raw `Only TheBoysLauncher installer downloads can be opened.` wording. Verified with focused dev-channel Settings coverage, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run test:release-channels`.
- Profile creation and customization setup fallbacks now surface the same sidebar recovery action as failed Play setup. If native `prepare_profile` fails after a profile is created or after version/loader changes are saved, the launcher keeps the saved profile visible, sets the affected profile as the recovery target, and tells the user to press `Try play again` instead of leaving only passive `next time you press Play` guidance. Verified with focused profile-create/profile-edit Playwright coverage, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Failed automatic profile setup now leaves a usable Play recovery path, and that recovery is no longer disabled by unrelated pack installs. The sidebar retry action only blocks on the target profile's own lifecycle/running state, so users can keep installing one pack while retrying setup/launch for another profile. Added coverage for a pending SkyPack install plus WinterPack setup recovery, alongside the existing setup-refresh and hidden-manual-repair flows. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "launch setup recovery stays|pack play setup refreshes|library profile launch actions hide manual repair"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover paste installs now support Prism/TheBoysLauncher app install links for Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, and Technic in addition to the existing CurseForge app-link path. Examples such as `prismlauncher://install?platform=modrinth&projectId=...&versionId=...`, `platform=atlauncher&pack=...`, `platform=ftb&packId=...`, and `platform=technic&slug=...` route through the native provider installer and refresh Library after install. Renderer and native updater handoff validation also now reject trusted-looking GitHub setup URLs with query strings or fragments, matching the bundle verifier. Verified with focused Discover app-link Playwright coverage, focused dev-channel update Playwright coverage, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher external_download_url -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `npm run test:release-channels`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Account/session refresh errors from native renderer-safe session guards now render as normal player guidance. `renderer-redacted Minecraft sessions cannot be saved...` maps to a Microsoft sign-in refresh message, and packaged-app preview-session failures hide `THEBOYS_ALLOW_RENDERER_SESSION_SAVE` / local-development wording behind `Preview accounts are only available in web preview. Use Microsoft sign-in in the desktop app.` Added Advanced Settings `Renew` coverage for both messages.
- Pasted CurseForge file-page links with a slug and exact file id now install through a keyless native path. `https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/modpacks/<slug>/files/<fileId>` still enters from the React provider-link parser, but launcher-core now builds a public `https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/modpacks/<slug>/download/<fileId>` archive plan instead of requiring `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` just to resolve the slug. CurseForge catalog search, slug-only page installs, and latest-file resolution still use the official keyed API path.
- Discover unsupported-format guidance now avoids beta-like `yet` wording for older ATLauncher and FTB Legacy formats. The renderer still maps the same native unsupported-format failures, but users now see `cannot prepare automatically` copy instead of raw `not supported by automatic install yet` or `cannot set up automatically yet` text. The Prism-style CurseForge install-link coverage was also expanded to include `prismlauncher://install?platform=curseforge&addonId=<projectId>&fileId=<fileId>`. Verified with focused pasted CurseForge and unsupported-format Playwright coverage.
- Discover paste installs now recognize Prism-style CurseForge app install links such as `curseforge://install?addonId=<projectId>&fileId=<fileId>` and route them through the existing keyless exact project/file native installer. The validator now lets supported provider app links through while keeping direct archive downloads HTTPS-only. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pasted CurseForge"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_exact_project_file_links_build_keyless_download_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Native curated-pack planning no longer describes already-installed packs as `Reinstall`. Current installed packs now emit `Prepare queued for ...` / `Prepare plan is ready to execute.`, and the Tauri completion receipt says `Pack is ready.` instead of `Pack reinstalled successfully.`, keeping Activity and receipts aligned with the simplified Play/Prepare UX.
- Advanced Settings now uses the same beginner-facing Java override wording as profile customization: `Global Java override` became `Global Java path override`, with the matching `Global Java path override` accessibility label. Installed-pack setup progress also says `Preparing pack` instead of `Reinstalling pack`, so normal install/play flows avoid repair/reinstall terminology.
- Technic Discover installs now support an additional Prism-style legacy Forge path: when a Technic archive has `bin/modpack.jar` without `version.json`, launcher-core reads nested `forgeversion.properties`, derives the Forge loader version, falls back to the provider Minecraft version, and still extracts the pack files into the managed profile. Truly opaque legacy jar patches still fail with beginner-facing copy instead of pretending they can launch.
- Library profile Advanced controls now use friendlier per-profile Java labels: `JVM args` became `Java launch options`, and `Java override` became `Java path override`. The controls remain hidden behind Advanced and still write the same `jvmArgs` / `javaRuntimeOverridePath` fields, but the UI no longer exposes JVM terminology in the profile customizer.
- Activity/download file-event copy now rewrites cached/waiting/failed file updates into friendlier progress language: `Already have file: ...`, `Waiting for file: ...`, and `File download failed: ...`. Native `Artifact...` / `File...` event parsing is unchanged, but normal Activity/sidebar surfaces no longer need to expose `already present` wording for cached downloads.
- Activity > Games now labels the collapsed process troubleshooting panel as `View game details` with `game details` accessibility labels instead of `View technical details`. The same process id, executable, working directory, and exit-code information remains available for troubleshooting, but the normal Activity screen reads less like an engineering console.
- FTB Discover typed search now uses the live provider search endpoint `https://api.modpacks.ch/public/modpack/search/{limit}?term=...` instead of scanning the first chunk of the full FTB pack index and filtering locally. Blank Browse still uses the public pack index, but search results now come from FTB's own lookup path and then fetch pack details concurrently for install-ready rows. Added parser coverage proving the FTB search response keeps only FTB pack IDs and ignores the companion CurseForge IDs.
- Microsoft sign-in error sanitization now covers native auth URL validation and callback request parser failures. Bad auth URLs show `Microsoft sign-in could not start. Check the launcher setup and try again.`, and unexpected localhost callback requests show `Microsoft sign-in received an unexpected browser response...` instead of leaking `not supported` OAuth/HTTP parser text.
- Discover provider cards now set clearer expectations instead of labeling every provider as equally `Install` ready. CurseForge and Modrinth are marked `Best supported`, All Sources is `Search ready`, ATLauncher/FTB/FTB Legacy/Technic are `Compatible packs`, and FTB Private is `Code lookup`; summaries now explain compatible-pack handling and older-format guidance while keeping all implemented native provider routes available.
- CurseForge `.zip` and Modrinth `.mrpack` archive installs now persist the pack version into `installed_pack_version` from `manifest.json` / `modrinth.index.json`, so Discover-installed profiles keep useful update/status metadata instead of looking like unknown-version custom imports. Added archive-planner assertions for both provider formats.
- ATLauncher provider installs now support client-side `type: "extract"` entries in addition to the top-level `Configs.zip` bundle. The launcher downloads extract archives into managed profile metadata, validates MD5 when supplied, safely strips `extractFolder` prefixes, unpacks into root/config/resource/etc. profile destinations without path traversal, and records the bundled-file extraction through the same Tauri install flow. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_install_plan_extracts_client_archive_entries -- --test-threads=1`, focused Discover Playwright coverage, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- ATLauncher Discover installs now include the provider's `Configs.zip` bundle when the live manifest advertises `configs`. The launcher plans the config archive with SHA-1/size validation, downloads it with the rest of the pack files, extracts it safely inside the managed profile root, and records the extraction in Activity. The normal account UI also now says `Account` / `Use preview account` / `Minecraft account` instead of exposing stored-session wording on visible controls. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, focused account and Discover Playwright coverage, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover paste-install copy now matches the full implemented provider set. The paste panel mentions FTB Legacy links, tells users that private FTB packs use the FTB Private code lookup, and generic `.zip` links show as `Pack .zip` instead of implying every zip is CurseForge. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|FTB Legacy CDN"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Profile version dropdown accessibility now matches the visible beginner-facing copy. New Profile and Library Customize both expose `version channel` labels instead of stale `version type` aria names, and the focused profile tests now use those labels. Verified with focused Playwright profile coverage, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover pack-link parsing now recognizes public FTB Legacy CDN archive links such as `https://dist.creeper.host/FTB2/modpacks/<dir>/<version>/<file>.zip` and routes them through the native `ftb_legacy` provider installer instead of the generic zip importer. The URL parser also guards malformed percent-encoded paths so bad paste input cannot crash the renderer. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|Modrinth|FTB|Technic).*provider link|FTB Legacy CDN"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Settings account actions are less cluttered on the normal screen. The main Minecraft account card now keeps only the primary account action and `Manage accounts`; `Refresh accounts` moved fully into the Manage accounts modal where account maintenance belongs. Multiple-account switching/removal behavior is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings native account actions|settings native mode keeps renderer-created preview session actions hidden|settings lists stored Minecraft accounts|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account|settings render memory"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Activity download-detail copy now uses beginner-facing wording. The collapsed verbose download bucket says `View download details (... download updates)` instead of `View file details (... file events)`, keeping per-file noise available without making the normal Activity screen read like an event log implementation detail. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|live file download events|activity file progress uses mod loader|activity active operation without percent"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Discover provider presentation was tightened after promoting all implemented providers into the main grid. The old additional-source CSS was removed, provider cards now use a compact auto-fit grid with two-line summaries so eight providers stay readable at the desktop base size, and the side summary now says `Sources: All visible providers` instead of listing only the older provider subset. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Discover now treats every implemented provider as a first-class choice in the main provider grid. FTB Legacy and FTB Private are no longer hidden behind an additional-sources expander; All Sources, CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic are all directly visible, while All Sources still excludes FTB Private from aggregate public search because private packs require a code. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover Technic search install|profile creator explains setup can finish"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Sidebar progress now treats active download/setup events with no numeric percent as real live work instead of ignoring them. A `download_artifacts` event with kind `queued`, `planning`, `downloading`, or `verifying` now switches the bottom-left card to `Preparing files` even when `progressPercent` is absent, using the existing indeterminate/starting progress style until a percent arrives or the operation completes. This keeps streamed native installs from looking stuck or stale during unknown-size work. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events|sidebar ignores stale active setup|activity active operation without percent|activity file progress uses mod loader"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Profile customization now has an immediate per-profile save guard. Rapid or forced duplicate clicks on the Library profile editor Save button collapse to one native `update_profile` request, the row shows `Saving...` while the save is pending, and Play is also disabled for that profile during the save so users cannot launch stale/in-between settings. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "profile editor (syncs native refreshed|ignores rapid duplicate|keeps profile values)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover provider implementation remains native for Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic. CurseForge pasted pack pages now have a keyless latest-export download plan through CurseForge's public pack download route, while exact project/file links still preserve the selected file id. Full CurseForge catalog search still uses the official `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` path. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "plain CurseForge pack page|pasted CurseForge provider link|CurseForge project file"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Release preflight now matches the post-promotion branch model. `scripts/preflight-v4-release.mjs` defaults to `main` as the release branch, expects `backup/main-v3-2026-06-29` to exist and differ from current `main`, verifies the release branch is pushed, includes the dev/stable release workflow and release-channel verifier in required files, and supports `THEBOYS_PREFLIGHT_SKIP_GITHUB=1` / `THEBOYS_PREFLIGHT_REPO_ROOT` for isolated offline testing. `npm run test:release-channels` now creates a tiny promoted-main fixture and proves the preflight accepts the current v4-on-main model instead of the old rewrite-branch-before-promotion model. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Friends-service status text now goes through a dedicated user-facing sanitizer before appearing in the bottom-left status or Advanced Settings readout. Raw backend/service internals such as `backend lock poisoned`, `failed to start friends service: ...`, `THEBOYS_BACKEND_EXE`, and `social-backend.exe` are rewritten into beginner-facing friends-service language. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings backend start waits|native startup prefers configured hosted social backend|settings native friends service action failures|settings friends service action falls back"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming there are no remaining direct `setActivity(status.message)` / `setActivity(reachableStatus.message)` backend writes.
- Bottom-left launcher status messages now use the same user-facing sanitizer as Activity rows for native plan/receipt success text. Import completion, Discover provider install receipts, direct pack-link install receipts, pack install plan messages, and profile deletion receipts no longer set raw native text directly, which keeps old `Profile repair completed.` / `Profile repair failed:`-style wording from leaking through status even when older events or receipts are encountered. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover Modrinth search install|discover archive install refreshes|library delete action"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming no remaining direct `setActivity(receipt.message)` / `setActivity(finalEvent?.message)` calls.
- Discover/import/profile creation success paths now focus the Library filter on the profile that was just installed, imported, created, or duplicated. This prevents a stale Library search from hiding the new profile immediately after the launcher switches to Library. Direct archive and provider-result installs use the refreshed native snapshot to prefer the saved profile name over guessed archive/provider text. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover archive install refreshes|discover Modrinth search install"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover all sources|discover (Modrinth search install|CurseForge search install|ATLauncher search install|FTB search install|FTB Legacy search install|FTB Private code install|Technic search install|Technic browse result)|pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|Modrinth|FTB|Technic).*provider link"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover pack-link copy now matches the implemented Prism-style provider routes. The paste box advertises CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, and Technic provider links plus direct `.zip` / `.mrpack` archives, and the side summary now says `Provider pages, .zip, .mrpack` instead of implying only CurseForge zip and Modrinth `.mrpack` imports. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Activity/native status failures now hide internal storage/registry wording. `event log database unavailable` renders as `Activity history is unavailable right now. Try again after restarting the launcher.`, and `process registry unavailable` renders as `Game status is unavailable right now. Try again after restarting the launcher.` Existing Activity rows and Library game-state cleanup behavior are preserved. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native event log refresh failure preserves real activity state|native process refresh failure clears stale process rows|activity screen loads event log fallback"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Discover native search failures now clear stale result rows before showing the failure guidance. This prevents a failed CurseForge/provider search from leaving old All Sources results visible, and All Sources/CurseForge provider setup failures hide raw `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` details behind pack-link guidance. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover CurseForge search explains|discover native search failure clears|discover shows provider plan|discover all sources"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Profile creation/customization copy is a little friendlier for new PC users. The visible profile version selector now says `Version channel` instead of `Version type`, old build channels show as `Classic beta` / `Classic alpha`, and unavailable-channel guidance now says `No snapshots are available right now...` instead of repeating manifest-style wording. The underlying dropdown values, safe version filtering, and create/update commands are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|profile creator falls back|library new profile action falls back|library profile editor saves|library profile editor can choose snapshot|library profile editor syncs|library profile editor keeps"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Manage Accounts now frames saved-account removal as removing that account from this launcher, not signing out of Minecraft globally. The modal row action says `Remove`, the guarded confirmation says `Remove account`, pending state says `Removing...`, and bottom-left status copy says `Confirm removing...` / `Removed...` while still calling the existing native `remove_minecraft_account` command. Verified with focused Settings account Playwright coverage and `npm run build`.
- Packaged Windows no-console behavior is now guarded in automation. `src-tauri/src/main.rs` already uses `#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]`; `npm run verify:tauri-security` now checks that attribute so release builds cannot accidentally regress to opening a console window, and `scripts/preflight-v4-release.mjs` includes the same release-preflight check. Verified with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run verify:package-resources:debug`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover promotes the implemented Prism-style public/provider routes into the main Discover grid. Native `provider: "all"` fans out across CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic, while FTB Private remains code-based and intentionally excluded from aggregate public search. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover all sources|discover (Modrinth search install|CurseForge search install|ATLauncher search install|FTB search install|FTB Legacy search install|FTB Private code install|Technic search install|Technic browse result)"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core all_discover_search_uses_public_catalog_providers -- --test-threads=1`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- The React Discover tab now calls native `search_discover_modpacks` once with `provider: "all"` for All Sources instead of reimplementing provider fan-out in the renderer. Search results still carry their concrete provider, so installing a mixed-source result routes through the correct native provider installer. The old per-provider UI aggregation test was replaced with aggregate-command coverage, and provider buttons use tighter selectors so the All Sources summary text cannot shadow individual provider cards. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover all sources|discover (Modrinth search install|CurseForge search install|ATLauncher search install|FTB search install|Technic search install)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Native Discover search now accepts `provider = "all"` in `launcher-core` and fans out across the public catalogs: CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic. Provider failures are tolerated when at least one source returns results, while FTB Private stays code-based and is intentionally excluded from public All Sources search. This keeps the Rust/Tauri discovery contract aligned with the React Discover tab instead of leaving aggregation as UI-only behavior. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core all_discover_search_uses_public_catalog_providers -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run build`.
- Hosted friend requests now require a real Minecraft-backed session instead of silently falling back to a local queued request. In native hosted mode, trying to add/request a friend without Minecraft sign-in sets the normal sign-in recovery action and does not call `/dev/sessions`, does not call friend mutation routes, and does not add a fake `Request sent` row. The sign-in detector now recognizes `Sign in to Minecraft to use hosted friends.` as an auth recovery condition. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "hosted friends search|hosted friend requests require|stored session upgrades cached dev backend session|stored session uses native token exchange|settings native account actions|sign in action surfaces|Microsoft callback"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Sidebar progress selection now suppresses stale non-download helper operations after a later completed install/setup lifecycle event for the same subject. This prevents old `Setting up...`/helper progress from reclaiming the bottom-left status once the launcher has already recorded a completed pack/profile lifecycle. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events|sidebar ignores stale active setup|pending native install surfaces operation progress|activity screen loads event log fallback"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover now opens on an `All sources` mode that searches CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic in parallel while still installing through the selected result's concrete provider. Combined search tolerates one provider failing, so a missing CurseForge API key or temporary provider outage no longer empties the whole Discover experience, mixed results show their source before the author/loader/version metadata, and partial outages are summarized as unavailable sources instead of looking like an empty catalog. Provider-specific browsing remains available, and ATLauncher/FTB pasted-link parsing accepts common singular/plural URL forms. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover all sources|discover (CurseForge search install|FTB search install|ATLauncher search install|FTB Legacy search install|FTB Private code install|Technic search install|Technic browse result|pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|Modrinth|FTB|Technic).*provider link|pack-link form)"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover all sources|discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Manage Accounts now has explicit pending guards for account refresh, switch, and removal. The modal shows `Refreshing...`, `Switching...`, or `Removing...` on the active control, disables other account actions while a native account operation is running, and ignores forced repeat clicks so duplicate `select_minecraft_account` / `remove_minecraft_account` calls cannot queue. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings lists stored Minecraft accounts|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account|settings native account actions|settings can manage preview Minecraft session"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Microsoft manual callback recovery now explains the localhost redirect requirement. If a user pastes an old `127.0.0.1` or otherwise mismatched callback URL into Advanced Settings after browser sign-in, the launcher now says `Use the localhost Microsoft callback URL from the browser tab, then try Finish sign in again.` instead of exposing the raw `configured redirect URI` OAuth error. Missing query parameters also prompt for the full localhost callback URL. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "Microsoft callback|sign in action waits|sign in action surfaces|settings callback completion"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Profile version dropdowns now normalize both launcher-shaped `versionType` entries and Mojang/raw-manifest-shaped `type` entries at the frontend boundary. This keeps the New Profile and Library Customize version selectors from silently falling back to common releases if native/test data uses the raw manifest field name, and preserves snapshot selection behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes the native bootstrap snapshot"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library new profile action falls back|profile creator falls back|library profile editor can choose snapshot"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- CurseForge Discover project-file links now preserve the exact file selection. Pasting `https://www.curseforge.com/projects/<projectId>/files/<fileId>` into the Discover pack-link box sends both the numeric project id and file id to the native provider installer instead of treating it as a generic project install. The Discover plan copy now explicitly advertises pack pages, project files, and zip links. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|pasted CurseForge provider link|CurseForge project file|discover CurseForge search install|discover CurseForge search explains"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Java recovery copy now matches the automatic-launcher behavior. Sidebar and Activity recovery actions say `Prepare Java` instead of `Set up Java`, and the Activity operation label for managed Java install also uses `Prepare Java`. The underlying recommended-runtime, managed-Java install, and retry behavior is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "Java preflight|auto-installs Java|managed Java executable|friend join Java automation|Prepare Java|Set up Java"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming `Set up Java` is gone from frontend/tests.
- Account removal confirmation now makes the scope explicit. In the Manage accounts modal, confirming a saved Minecraft account removal shows `Removes it from this launcher only.` before the `Remove account` action, so users do not confuse launcher sign-out with deleting their Microsoft/Minecraft account. The native `remove_minecraft_account` flow is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings can manage multiple native Minecraft accounts|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account|settings native account actions|settings native mode keeps renderer-created preview session actions hidden|settings can manage preview Minecraft session"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Settings update-channel copy now reassures users that checking Stable or Dev does not change anything until they install the launcher setup. The existing dev/stable side-by-side behavior and trusted-installer validation are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings exposes stable and dev|settings dev channel|settings render memory"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run test:release-channels`.
- Activity/Import copy got another small beginner-facing pass. Import review now asks users to choose what to do with `matching files` instead of `conflicts`, with matching-file choice aria labels, and Activity overview/file progress labels now say `waiting` instead of `pending`. Internal event/plan state names remain unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen|native import|empty native launcher checks for importable|activity screen loads event log fallback|activity overview"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming the old visible conflict wording remains only as a negative Playwright assertion.
- Profile customization is less cluttered in the normal Library row editor. The always-visible profile form now keeps only name, version type, version, and memory; window size, default server, loader, JVM args, Java override, and launch-detail checks live behind the existing `Advanced` toggle. This preserves all save/update behavior and validation while keeping the new-to-PC path focused on the choices most users understand. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor can choose snapshot|library profile editor syncs native refreshed profile values|library profile editor keeps profile values|hide-from-library|library delete action is disabled"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Profile removal wording now separates non-destructive Library cleanup from destructive deletion. The Library profile customizer danger action that calls native `archive_profile` is labeled `Hide from Library`, status copy says `Hiding profile from Library` / `<name> hidden from Library`, and the web-preview fallback says `Profile hidden from Library in web preview`. The core behavior is unchanged: `archive_profile` removes only the profile catalog entry and keeps profile files, while `Delete` remains the explicit file-removal path with confirmation. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "hide-from-library|library profile editor saves preview changes|library delete action is disabled|pack play archive|archive"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core archive_profile -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover paste-install copy now uses beginner-facing `Pack link` wording instead of the misleading `Archive URL` label. The same box still accepts supported provider pages plus direct `.zip`/`.mrpack` downloads, but empty/invalid validation now says `Paste a pack link first`, `Use a direct https:// link to a pack download or provider page`, and `Use a pack download ending in .zip/.mrpack, or a supported provider page link`. Advanced Java archive controls still keep archive wording because they are actually archive-specific. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover archive form|discover archive install refreshes|pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|Modrinth|FTB|Technic).*provider link"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover pack-link form"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Launch preflight setup-recovery copy now catches another native missing-files shape. Renderer sanitization maps `launch asset index is missing: ...` and `launch natives directory is missing: ...` to `Game files are missing. The launcher will set them up automatically.`, so Activity/history and the sidebar no longer expose cache paths or `launch asset` wording for that common setup-needed failure. The existing auto setup-then-launch behavior is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library play action surfaces native launch preflight|launch command preflight failure offers setup recovery|failed native repair surfaces"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Dev-channel installer handoff now matches the actual dev product name. The renderer trusted-download check accepts the encoded `TheBoysLauncher%20Dev_<version>_x64-setup.exe` asset path while still rejecting lookalike names such as `TheBoysLauncherDev_...`, and the updater manifest generator now URL-encodes setup asset filenames so spaces from `productName: "TheBoysLauncher Dev"` do not produce literal-space URLs. The bundle verifier decodes the manifest filename before comparing it to the NSIS setup artifact, and `npm run test:release-channels` now creates a temp dev bundle with a spaced filename to prove generation plus verification. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings .*channel"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
- Discover Modrinth installs now use the same native `install_discover_modpack` provider command as CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy/private, and Technic. Tauri resolves the selected/latest Modrinth `.mrpack`, downloads/extracts it, runs vanilla/modloader setup, and persists the installed profile inside the unified provider install flow; React no longer special-cases Modrinth with `resolve_modrinth_modpack_archive` plus `install_modpack_archive`. Added pasted Modrinth provider-link coverage so `https://modrinth.com/modpack/.../version/...` also routes through the native provider installer. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover (Modrinth search install|pasted Modrinth provider link|pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|FTB|Technic).*provider link)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.
- Discover provider coverage is wired through the React Discover tab, Tauri native commands, and launcher-core provider parsers/installers for CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic. Focused provider E2E coverage passes for search/install-to-library refresh, and focused launcher-core provider tests pass for the implemented parser/planner paths.
- The launcher-core Java runtime test guard now serializes fake Java-runtime overrides for the whole guard lifetime, fixing concurrent test leakage where Java 8 and Java 21 launch-plan tests could swap runtimes under each other. `cargo test -p launcher-core --lib` is green again.
- Stored-session friend join sign-in recovery now preserves the original server target. If an authenticated friend join fails because the stored Microsoft/Minecraft session needs sign-in, the sidebar recovery action returns as `Try join again` after sign-in and launches the same profile/server target instead of falling back to generic Play.
- Verified this slice with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "stored session friend join|friend join"`, `npm run build`, `cargo test -p launcher-core --lib`, and `cargo check --workspace`.
- Dev/stable release-channel automation now has a cheap offline verifier: `npm run test:release-channels` creates an isolated tagged git repo to prove stable/dev auto-version resolution and uses a temp Tauri config to prove the stable/dev product name, bundle identifier, version, and updater endpoint mutations. The v4 release workflow runs this verifier immediately after resolving the next version, before expensive Rust/Tauri packaging work. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, update-channel Playwright coverage, `npm run build`, and `cargo check --workspace`.
- Hosted social backend status was rechecked against current local/public endpoints. `npm run social:check` passes for `http://127.0.0.1:4074` and `https://launcher.dylan.lol`, including CORS preflight/read checks for `https://launcher.dylan.lol`, `tauri://localhost`, `http://tauri.localhost`, and `https://tauri.localhost`. Hosted-mode renderer coverage also passes for no local auto-start and no dev-session request without Minecraft sign-in.
- Debug package-resource verification caught a stale staged `social-backend` resource after rebuilding the backend. Rebuilt with `npm run build:social-backend:debug`, restaged with `npm run stage:social-backend:debug`, and `npm run verify:package-resources:debug` now verifies both `src-tauri/resources/social-backend.exe` and the portable `social-backend` copy against `target/debug/social-backend.exe`.
- The v4 foundation workflow now watches `scripts/configure-tauri-release-channel.mjs`, `scripts/resolve-v4-release-version.mjs`, and `scripts/test-release-channel-scripts.mjs`, and runs `npm run test:release-channels` during the normal PR/codex validation job so release-channel script regressions fail before an expensive publish workflow. Verified locally with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run build`, and `npm run verify:tauri-security`.

- Social Backend Image tag run `28345109081` succeeded and published the GHCR backend image.
- GitHub Release `v4.0.0` is published with MSI, NSIS setup exe, signatures, and `latest.json`.
- The updater endpoint resolves through `https://github.com/dilllxd/theboyslauncher/releases/latest/download/latest.json`.
- The released backend image `ghcr.io/dilllxd/theboyslauncher/social-backend:v4.0.0` runs locally through WSL/Docker and returns healthy on `http://127.0.0.1:4074/health`.
- Local Windows Caddy reverse-proxies `launcher.dylan.lol` to the hosted backend on `127.0.0.1:4074`, and `https://launcher.dylan.lol/health` returns healthy.

## Context

This repository originally contained a Go/Fyne Minecraft launcher that used Prism Launcher as its backend. The desired direction changed: the new launcher should be a fresh foundation, not a Prism wrapper, not a `cmd-launcher` fork, and not constrained by the old Go app.

The product goal is a modern, easy-to-use, cross-platform Minecraft launcher for friends:

- one-click curated modpack installs and updates;
- custom vanilla/modloader profiles for normal Minecraft use;
- social-first home screen showing what friends are playing;
- pack-bound servers and one-click join;
- migration/import from old TheBoysLauncher/Prism and other launchers;
- hosted backend for friends/presence/pack metadata;
- strong E2E testing to reduce regressions.

## Chosen Foundation

Use:

- Tauri v2 for the desktop shell;
- React + TypeScript + Vite for the frontend;
- Rust for the local launcher core;
- Rust backend with Postgres and WebSockets for social features.

Do not preserve Fyne. Do not keep Go as the main foundation. Do not rely on Prism, MultiMC instance format, or a third-party launcher core as a black box.

## Current Branch

Branch created for this rewrite:

```text
codex/fresh-foundation-rewrite
```

The repository has no commits yet, so this branch currently contains the working tree state.
