The Activity fallback regression now verifies the copied process output and status message, and the native log/export plus launch supervisor regressions still pass with the expanded action row. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native process log export refreshes exited process state|library play action opens native process supervisor after launch"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest process output retention: launcher-core now retains the latest 1000 managed-process output lines instead of only 200 before dropping older stdout/stderr entries. The Activity view still shows a compact visible tail, but copied output/log exports now have a much better diagnostic window for noisy real Minecraft/Forge/Fabric startup logs while continuing to report total and dropped line counts. The truncation regression now derives its noisy-output fixture from the configured capacity to prove the retained tail and dropped count stay correct. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core process_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest desktop runtime verification: reran `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:launch` against `target/live-smoke/winterpack`; the live WinterPack artifacts still start a real Java Minecraft process, survive startup, and stop cleanly. The Tauri security verifier remains green. Debug desktop resources were stale/missing for the current build output, so `npm run build:social-backend:debug` and `npm run stage:social-backend:debug` were run, then `npm run verify:package-resources:debug` confirmed both staged resource names (`social-backend.exe` and `social-backend`) match the freshly built debug backend. The packaged-backend startup Playwright slice also passes, proving the desktop shell still detects/starts the packaged backend path used by Settings/native startup. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:launch`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:package-resources:debug`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native startup auto-starts packaged social backend|settings backend"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest running-process lifecycle guard: native `install_pack`/update and `repair_profile` now refuse to mutate a profile while that profile has a running or stop-requested managed Minecraft process, matching the existing save/archive/delete protections. Pack cards and the Home primary/secondary actions now show `Running` and stay disabled for update/repair/install affordances when the target profile is active, so the UI no longer invites a mutation that native code must reject. The current Dylan/WinterPack live packwiz plan smoke was rerun first and still passes. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:plan`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher active_managed_process_for_profile_matches_running_and_stop_requested_processes -- --test-threads=1`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "pack update action is disabled while the target profile process is running"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "disabled while.*running|pack update action is disabled"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest Activity recovery guard alignment: Activity event-row recovery actions now also respect active managed processes. Failed launch repair, failed install retry, and failed repair retry buttons show `Running` and stay disabled when the target profile already has a running or stop-requested Minecraft process, matching the native mutation guard instead of offering a retry that must be rejected. The new Playwright regression covers a failed repair event while WinterPack is running and verifies no `repair_profile` command is invoked. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "activity repair retry is disabled while the target profile process is running"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "disabled while.*running|pack update action is disabled|activity repair retry is disabled"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest authenticated command-preview recovery: Library `Command` now treats stored-session refresh/token failures the same way Play and friend Join do. If the native `build_stored_authenticated_launch_command` path fails with an expired/invalid Microsoft refresh token or missing stored Minecraft session, the shell clears the stale renderer session and exposes the sidebar `Sign in` recovery action for the affected profile instead of leaving command diagnostics at a dead-end error. Playwright covers the failed authenticated command preview path, verifies the stale session disappears from Settings, and confirms the Microsoft sign-in recovery flow is invoked. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "authenticated launch command refresh failure"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "launch command action shows native command preview|authenticated launch command refresh failure|authenticated launch refresh failure offers sign-in recovery|authenticated launch sign-in recovery"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest process lifecycle output guard: launcher-core managed-process output readers now share a lifecycle snapshot with the registry instead of broadcasting every stdout/stderr update as `Running`. Stop/exited state updates refresh that snapshot before lifecycle broadcasts, so late output arriving after a stop or natural exit can still update retained logs without resurrecting the process row as running in the desktop UI. A focused regression drives the output reader directly with a stopped/exited lifecycle snapshot and verifies the broadcast preserves `Exited`, `stopRequested`, exit code, and output text. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core process_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest Activity stale-plan cleanup: after a live WinterPack launch smoke passed against the current tree, the React event merge was tightened so locally merged preflight install/repair plan operations are discarded once native launcher events arrive for the same operation and subject under the native command's real operation id. This prevents Activity from showing an orphaned `Install/Update/Repair plan is ready to execute.` row beside the actual native completion/failure operation after one-click install/update/repair flows. The existing install regression now uses different local/native operation ids and asserts the stale local plan row disappears while the completed `Pack installed successfully.` event remains playable. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:launch`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "installing a pack refreshes|updating a pack plans|home repair action refreshes|library profile repair action"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest desktop Library action density cleanup: a rendered 1100x720 desktop QA pass found Library profile action buttons stretching vertically into oversized blocks in the wrapped three-column action layout. Profile action groups now align to content height and compact lifecycle buttons have a stable 34px height, keeping Play/Update/Repair/Command/Save/Archive/Delete readable without the heavy stretched-button look at the supported minimum. The dense desktop Playwright regression now asserts profile action buttons stay compact while preserving the existing no-horizontal-overflow checks. Browser plugin navigation to the local Vite target timed out twice, so validation fell back to regular Playwright; fallback render metrics showed no horizontal overflow, no app console errors beyond Vite/React dev messages, and compact 34px profile action buttons after the fix. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "dense desktop panels fit"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest native process fallback boundary: React `loadManagedProcesses` no longer injects the web-preview fake process list when the shell is running under Tauri/native mode and `list_managed_processes` fails. This matters for real install/repair failure recovery: a missing or transient process-registry response should not make failed install/repair Activity rows think WinterPack is already running and replace actionable `Retry`/`Repair` controls with disabled `Running`. The failed install and failed repair regressions now simulate native failures whose final launcher event uses a different operation id than the locally merged preflight plan, assert the stale local `plan is ready to execute` rows are gone, and verify the recovery buttons stay available. Web-preview Activity fallback and native launch/log process paths remain covered. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "failed native pack install|failed native repair"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|library play action opens native process supervisor|native process log export"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest native process stale-state cleanup: a live WinterPack repair smoke passed against the current Dylan catalog, proving repair still restores a deleted launch dependency and reaches launch preflight. React `loadManagedProcesses` now also clears cached native process rows and the short-lived returned-launch fallback cache when the native process registry fails, instead of leaving stale `Running` rows that can keep profile actions disabled after a transient registry error. The new Playwright regression launches WinterPack through the native supervisor mock, forces the next process refresh to fail, and verifies Activity drops the stale process row, the quick-status strip returns to `No active process`, and Library `Play` is enabled again. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:repair`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native process refresh failure clears stale process rows"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native process refresh failure clears stale process rows|library play action opens native process supervisor|library play action keeps returned native process visible|failed native pack install|failed native repair|activity screen loads event log fallback|native process log export"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest catalog shape compatibility: verified the live `https://modpacks.dylan.lol/modpacks.json` endpoint still returns the raw legacy array shape and that the live WinterPack `pack.toml` metadata smoke remains green. The shared catalog parser now also accepts simple wrapper objects with either `modpacks` or `packs` arrays, so a future v4 hosted catalog can add top-level metadata without breaking the launcher while preserving support for the current Dylan JSON and standard packwiz `pack.toml` entries. Shared-crate regressions cover both wrapper spellings. Verified with `cargo test -p shared modpack_catalog -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:metadata`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest native process-action failure handling: reran `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:install`, which still installs the live WinterPack artifacts and reaches launch preflight from the current Dylan catalog/packwiz metadata. React process controls now keep native process state truthful when Stop, Clear exited, Log export, or Open logs fail under Tauri: failed Stop no longer marks a still-running Minecraft process as stop-requested, failed Clear exited no longer removes exited rows locally, failed Log export no longer reports the web-preview mock message, and failed Open logs keeps the exported-log receipt visible while surfacing the native error. Playwright covers failed native Stop/Log/Clear exited and failed Open logs, and the surrounding process supervisor suite still passes. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:install`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native process action failures preserve process state|native process log reveal failure keeps exported log receipt"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native process action failures preserve process state|native process log reveal failure keeps exported log receipt|native process log export refreshes exited process state|stopping a native process|exited native process row can relaunch|clear exited process does not restore|native process refresh failure clears stale process rows|activity screen loads event log fallback"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest pending-launch duplicate guard: reran `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:launch`, which still starts the real WinterPack Java process, verifies it survives startup, and stops it through launcher-core supervision. The React shell now tracks a pending native launch before the process registry returns the managed process, feeds that state into the shared lifecycle busy label, and disables Play/Join/recovery launch affordances as `Launching...` during the gap. A ref-backed guard also rejects repeated launch attempts before React has rendered the disabled controls, preventing accidental duplicate `start_launch_process` calls from a fast double-click while the first native start is still building/spawning Java. Playwright holds the native launch promise open, verifies the Library profile Play button is disabled as `Launching...`, tries a repeated click, and asserts only one native launch command is issued before the process row appears. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:launch`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library play action is disabled while native launch is pending"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library play action opens native process supervisor|library play action is disabled while native launch is pending|library play action keeps returned native process visible|native process refresh failure clears stale process rows|installed pack card play|exited native process row can relaunch"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest pending server-join duplicate guard: friend/server Join now shares the pending-launch guard from profile Play and sets it before any asynchronous pack-ready install/update/repair check, closing the fast double-click window before `start_launch_process_for_server` is invoked. Friend Join buttons now show the shared lifecycle label such as `Launching...` instead of a generic `Busy` while native server launch is pending, and the ref-backed guard prevents a second join launch even when two DOM clicks happen before React repaints the disabled button. Playwright covers that double-click race and verifies only one `start_launch_process_for_server` call is made before the managed process appears, while the existing update-before-join, launch-preflight recovery, running-process disable, and stored-session authenticated join paths still pass. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "friend join is disabled while native server launch is pending"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "friend join is disabled while native server launch is pending|friend join updates pack before native server launch|friend join surfaces native launch preflight|friend join is disabled while the target profile process is running|stored session friend join passes server target|authenticated join|library play action is disabled while native launch is pending"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest lifecycle mutation duplicate guard: install/update/reinstall, repair, and delete now use a ref-backed renderer guard in addition to visible disabled button state, closing the same fast double-click window that Play/Join already guard. This prevents duplicate `plan_install_pack`/`install_pack`, `plan_repair_profile`/`repair_profile`, or `delete_profile` calls before React repaints the lifecycle controls, while preserving the existing native lifecycle gates as the final backstop. The pending update regression now fires two DOM clicks before any visible disabled state and verifies only one install plan/execution is issued; related lifecycle tests were aligned with the current truthful labels (`Busy`, `Updating...`, `Repairing...`, and ready `Play` after completion rather than stale `Running` when no process exists). Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "pack update action is disabled while native install is pending"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "installing a pack refreshes|updating a pack plans|pack update action is disabled while native install is pending|home repair action shows pending native repair state|home repair action refreshes|library profile repair action|library delete action|archiving a profile refreshes"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest command-preflight recovery alignment: Library `Command` now classifies native launch-command preflight failures the same way Play does. Missing Java command failures expose the Java recovery path, stored-session failures still clear stale session state and expose Sign in, and repairable launch-artifact failures such as missing asset indexes or natives now expose the sidebar Repair action instead of leaving command diagnostics at a dead-end error. The new Playwright regression has `build_launch_command` fail with a missing asset index, verifies the command preview is cleared and Repair appears, then drives the normal `plan_repair_profile`/`repair_profile` flow from the recovery action. Existing command preview copy/redaction and authenticated command sign-in recovery still pass. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "launch command preflight failure offers repair recovery"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library launch command action shows native command preview|launch command preflight failure offers repair recovery|authenticated launch command refresh failure|launch command action falls back"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest command Java recovery coverage: command-preview preflight recovery now has explicit Playwright proof for the missing-managed-Java path too. A native `build_launch_command` failure with `Install a managed Java runtime from Settings before launching` clears the command preview, exposes the sidebar Java recovery action, opens Settings, and applies the recommended Temurin 21 runtime draft from `recommended_java_runtime_manifest` while avoiding Repair. The broader command-preview cluster now covers web-preview fallback, successful command copy/redaction, repairable missing-artifact recovery, Java recovery, and stored-session sign-in recovery. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "launch command Java preflight failure offers Java recovery"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library launch command action shows native command preview|launch command preflight failure offers repair recovery|launch command Java preflight failure offers Java recovery|authenticated launch command refresh failure|launch command action falls back"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest native Activity event-log failure boundary: native `list_launcher_events` failures no longer inject the web-preview mock event stream into Activity. The React shell now preserves the existing real event timeline and surfaces the native error message, while the browser preview still uses the mocked event log fallback. Added Playwright coverage that loads a real native launch event, forces a later event refresh failure, verifies the real event remains visible, and confirms `Event log is mocked in web preview` is not shown under Tauri. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native event log refresh failure preserves real activity state"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|native event log refresh failure preserves real activity state"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest native Settings failure boundary: Settings actions that matter for launching now keep desktop state truthful when native commands fail. `save_settings` failures surface the native error instead of claiming a web-preview save, failed `clear_minecraft_session` preserves the visible stored session instead of clearing it locally, failed `discover_java_runtimes` no longer shows a fake preview Java runtime under Tauri, and failed `recommended_java_runtime_manifest` no longer shows preview Temurin recommendations in native mode. Browser-preview fallbacks remain unchanged. Added Playwright coverage for native save/clear-session failure preservation and native Java discovery/recommendation failures, plus reran the neighboring preview fallback tests. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings native save and clear-session failures preserve real state|settings native Java failures do not show preview runtimes"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings save action falls back|settings native save and clear-session failures preserve real state|settings Java discovery action falls back|settings native Java failures do not show preview runtimes|settings managed Java install falls back"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest Library log discoverability: successful native process log export now navigates the shell to Activity > Processes after refreshing managed-process state, so exporting a running Minecraft profile's log from Library immediately reveals the exported-log receipt and `Open logs` action instead of only writing a status message in the sidebar. Web-preview/mock failures still stay on the current view. Added Playwright coverage that loads a running WinterPack process, clicks `Log` from the Library profile row, and verifies Activity > Processes is active with the exported log path and open action visible. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library process log export opens the Activity log receipt"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "native process log export refreshes exited process state|library process log export opens the Activity log receipt|native process log reveal failure keeps exported log receipt|native process action failures preserve process state"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest command-preview stale recovery cleanup: reran `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:plan`, which still validates the live Dylan/WinterPack catalog, packwiz metadata, and Forge install planning path. A successful native `Command` inspection now clears stale repair/Java launch recovery state for that profile, and successful authenticated command inspection also clears stale sign-in recovery for that profile. This prevents the sidebar from continuing to offer `Repair` after a transient missing-artifact command preflight has been fixed and the command preview can now be built. Added Playwright coverage that makes the first `build_launch_command` fail with a missing asset index, confirms the `Repair` recovery appears, then makes the second command preview succeed and verifies the stale recovery action disappears. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:plan`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library launch command action shows native command preview|launch command preflight failure offers repair recovery|successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery|launch command Java preflight failure offers Java recovery|authenticated launch command refresh failure"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest launch recovery cleanup: successful native Play and friend Join launches now clear stale session sign-in recovery in addition to the existing repair/Java recovery profile state. This prevents a previous authenticated-launch refresh failure from leaving a sidebar `Sign in` action after the user successfully starts the same profile with the valid offline/local launch path. Added Playwright coverage where an authenticated launch fails with `invalid_grant`, the renderer clears the stored session and shows Sign in recovery, then a second offline launch succeeds and the stale Sign in recovery disappears while the managed process is visible. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "successful offline launch clears stale sign-in recovery after auth failure"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "authenticated launch refresh failure offers sign-in recovery|authenticated launch sign-in recovery can retry the blocked profile|successful offline launch clears stale sign-in recovery after auth failure|expired refreshable stored session still uses native authenticated launch"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest install/update recovery cleanup: successful native `install_pack`/update now clears stale repair recovery for the same pack/profile. This covers the path where `Command` or Play previously exposed a repairable missing-artifact recovery, then a normal update/install flow restores the profile artifacts; the sidebar no longer keeps offering `Repair` after the successful update. Added Playwright coverage that makes `build_launch_command` fail with a missing asset index, verifies the sidebar `Repair` action appears, runs the WinterPack update flow, and confirms the stale recovery action disappears after `Pack updated successfully.` The current live Dylan/WinterPack packwiz planning smoke still passes. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "successful pack update clears stale launch repair recovery"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "updating a pack plans native install workflow|successful pack update clears stale launch repair recovery|pack update action is disabled while native install is pending|successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery|launch command preflight failure offers repair recovery"`, `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:plan`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest profile removal recovery cleanup: successful native archive/delete now clears profile-specific recovery state for the removed profile, so the sidebar cannot keep offering `Repair`, Java retry, or Sign in actions for a profile that is gone. Added Playwright coverage that first creates a stale repair recovery via a failed `build_launch_command`, then confirms deleting that profile removes the stale sidebar `Repair` action while the delete event remains visible and the profile disappears from Library. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library delete action clears stale launch recovery for removed profile"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library delete action surfaces native event stream after removal|library delete action clears stale launch recovery for removed profile|library delete action keeps profile visible after native failure|successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery|launch command preflight failure offers repair recovery"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest desktop happy-path audit: the real Tauri app was run against `target/live-smoke/ui-proof`, and the Home, Library, Friends, Import, Activity, and Settings screens were inspected through the desktop window at the 1100x720+ baseline. A live Library repair flow was driven from the app: `Repair` streamed native events, eventually recorded `Profile repair completed.`, and Library returned both profiles to enabled `Play`/`Repair`/`Customize` buttons. A temporary profile was also created through the real Library `New profile` flow, Customize exposed `Archive`/`Delete` only after opening profile settings, `Delete` switched to the guarded `Confirm delete`/`Cancel` state, and `Cancel` preserved the profile; the temporary test profile was then cleaned directly from the isolated proof-root JSON so the proof root returned to `latest-release` plus `winterpack`. React now mirrors the lifecycle ref into state so terminal native lifecycle events can repaint controls immediately, and Settings account actions now use a dedicated Manage accounts modal for refresh/switch/sign-out maintenance instead of crowding the normal Settings card. A fresh isolated live delete smoke also passed at `target/live-smoke/delete-proof`, proving WinterPack delete removes profile data while preserving shared cache. Stored authenticated-launch behavior remains covered by Rust/Playwright tests, but a true live online authenticated launch still needs a stored Microsoft/Minecraft account in the root being tested; the inspected proof roots currently have no `minecraft-session`/account files. Verified with `node scripts/run-live-winterpack-smoke.mjs delete --fresh --root target/live-smoke/delete-proof`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "library delete action|library archive action|disabled while a profile process is running"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core stored_authenticated_launch_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "authenticated launch|stored session|Microsoft callback|settings lists stored Minecraft accounts"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings render memory|settings save action|settings save validates|terminal repair event clears"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest real desktop launch supervision proof: the running Tauri app was used to launch WinterPack from the Home `Play` button against `target/live-smoke/ui-proof`. The shell navigated to Activity > Processes, showed one active managed Java process, displayed redacted launch args (`--accessToken` masked in renderer output), streamed Forge/ModLauncher output, and updated the WinterPack profile `lastPlayed` marker to a fresh durable `unix:<seconds>` value after startup survived. Activity `Log` exported `target/live-smoke/ui-proof/logs/processes/1782606455-144f2c92-139e-4f55-8f95-43bec9af40db.log`; the file existed, retained 446 lines at export time, and did not contain the literal offline token value. The Activity `Stop` action moved the row to `STOPPED`, exposed relaunch/log/copy actions, set the quick status back to `No active process`, and the OS process table confirmed PID 3876/java was gone. The exported log captured real render/mod startup output including LWJGL/OpenGL, Embeddium/Oculus, and WinterPack mod messages.

Latest real desktop update and Settings polish proof: the isolated proof root `target/live-smoke/ui-proof` was manually set to stale WinterPack `installedPackVersion: "0.0.0-codex-update-proof"`, then the running Tauri app was refreshed and verified to show `Update` on the Home primary pack and Featured Packs card. Clicking the Home `Update` button drove the real native install/update path from the desktop UI, streamed artifact download/verification events in Activity, and returned `target/live-smoke/ui-proof/config/profiles.json` to `installedPackVersion: "2.3.7"` with no Java process left running. A follow-up desktop refresh showed WinterPack back to `Ready` with enabled `Play`/`Repair`. Settings was also tightened for beginner-friendly desktop use: the shell/workspace now have bounded `100vh` scrolling, advanced Settings groups are contained in clearer Account/Runtime/Service plus Sign in recovery/Managed Java cards, advanced fields wrap instead of causing horizontal overflow, and the 1100x720+ baseline was verified through the live Tauri window. Verified with `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings render memory|settings save action|settings save validates"`.

Latest stored-account management slice: multi-account Minecraft storage now has a native `remove_minecraft_account` command in launcher-core/Tauri. Removing a non-active stored account preserves the active session; removing the active account switches to the next stored account when one exists, updates the legacy active `minecraft-session.json`, or clears session state when no accounts remain. Settings now exposes per-account `Remove` with a required `Confirm remove`/`Cancel` step, so users can clean up stale saved accounts without wiping every account through the older active-session clear action. The Settings row stays compact at the desktop baseline through shared `row-actions` styling. This does not prove a true live online authenticated launch yet; the current live proof roots still do not contain a stored Microsoft/Minecraft account. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core stored_minecraft_account -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `npm run build`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings can remove a stored Minecraft account"`, and `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings.*Minecraft account|settings native mode|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account"`.

Latest signed-in UI clarity slice: usable stored Minecraft sessions now render compactly as `<username> signed in` in the top quick-status strip and account card instead of exposing token-expiry details in always-visible UI. The topbar account control now has an explicit `Signed in` label plus the active Minecraft username and an accessible `Minecraft account <username> signed in` name, while the unauthenticated state still shows the normal `Sign in` button. Expired/refreshable session wording remains intact so recovery flows still communicate `refreshes`/`expired` states. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "sign in action waits|settings.*Minecraft account|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest authenticated server-join command safety proof: Tauri's renderer-safe command-spec regression now verifies authenticated launch command inspection redacts token-bearing arguments and sensitive env vars while preserving non-secret quick-join arguments such as `--server play.theboys.example --port 25565`. The existing stored-session friend Join regression still proves the React shell passes the server target into `start_stored_authenticated_launch_process`, the native process row shows the authenticated join, and backend presence publishes the joined server. This strengthens the stored-session happy path without introducing a fake native sign-in path; live online launch still needs a real stored Microsoft/Minecraft account in the tested launcher root. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher renderer_safe_process_command_redacts_tokens -- --test-threads=1`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "stored session friend join passes server target"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest live stored-auth command smoke: found the prior authenticated proof root at `target/interactive-auth-smoke` with a DPAPI-protected stored Minecraft session for `Dilll` and installed WinterPack `2.3.7` artifacts. Added `live_winterpack_stored_authenticated_command_uses_saved_session` as an ignored launcher-core smoke and `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:auth-command`, which points at that root. The smoke loads the real stored session, builds a stored authenticated WinterPack launch plan, preflights the installed artifacts into a Java command, proves the command uses the stored username/compact UUID/access token from the unprotected local session, and preserves quick-join `--server`/`--port` arguments. This proves real stored-session authenticated command building/preflight from the local launcher root; the remaining live-auth gap is actually starting the Java process online with that stored account and observing successful authenticated startup. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:auth-command`, `cargo test -p launcher-core stored_authenticated_launch_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest live stored-auth launch smoke: added `live_winterpack_stored_authenticated_launch_process_survives_startup_and_can_stop` plus `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:auth-launch`, using the same `target/interactive-auth-smoke` root with the real stored `Dilll` session and installed WinterPack artifacts. The smoke builds the stored-authenticated WinterPack command, confirms the Java command uses the unprotected stored access token plus quick-join server args, starts the real Java process, waits through the startup grace window, confirms the process is still running, then stops it through the launcher-core process registry. A follow-up process-table check showed no leftover Java processes. This proves the core launcher can use the real local stored Microsoft/Minecraft session to start and supervise authenticated WinterPack Java; the remaining UI-specific proof is driving the same authenticated Play flow through the running Tauri/React desktop surface. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:auth-launch`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo test -p launcher-core stored_authenticated_launch_plan -- --test-threads=1`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest real desktop stored-auth launch and UI cleanup proof: the Tauri dev app was restarted with `THEBOYS_LAUNCHER_ROOT_DIR=target/interactive-auth-smoke` and `THEBOYS_MICROSOFT_CLIENT_ID=d10dfc60-1a42-44a8-b3af-edf4f5ee2c1f`, then Home, Library, Friends, Import, Activity, and Settings were inspected through the real Windows desktop window. The signed-in UI correctly showed `SIGNED IN / Dilll` in the topbar and `Dilll signed in` in the quick-status strip, and Settings now keeps the normal-user surface to account plus one Minecraft memory control while hiding UUID/expiry/minimum-memory/offline-name details behind Advanced tools. Activity now has one combined `Refresh` action instead of duplicated refresh buttons, and the app workspace is bounded to the desktop frame so the launcher no longer feels like a browser page at the 1280x852 dev window. From Home, clicking WinterPack `Play` with the real stored account launched Minecraft/Forge; Activity > Processes showed one active managed Java process, redacted `--accessToken` output, username `Dilll`, compact UUID, and quick-join `--server play.theboys.example --port 25565`. The UI `Stop` action moved the process row to `STOPPED`, quick status returned to `No active process`, and a Windows process-table check showed no remaining Java process. Verified with `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(home screen renders|settings)"`, and the live desktop launch/stop walkthrough above.

Latest clean-root desktop install-to-play proof: first-run native profile seeding no longer creates a fake stale WinterPack profile. New roots now seed only the vanilla `Latest Release` profile, so catalog WinterPack starts as a real `Install` action instead of `Update` from an old Fabric/date-based profile. The Tauri app was run against a fresh `target/live-smoke/ui-clean-install-proof` root containing only the stored `Dilll` Minecraft session and settings. Home showed `Dilll signed in`, one vanilla profile, and WinterPack `Install`; clicking Home `Install` drove the real native install path from the UI. The install downloaded vanilla/cache artifacts, wrote `data/profiles/winterpack/pack.toml` and `index.toml`, installed 244 mod files, fetched Forge metadata/dependencies, ran 6 modloader installer processors, and persisted WinterPack as Forge `1.20.1` with `installedPackVersion: "2.3.7"`. The quick Latest strip now prefers the lifecycle label such as `Installing...` while a long install is still running, instead of surfacing the planning event as `Install pack - completed` too early. After install, clicking Home `Play` launched the freshly installed pack with stored auth for `Dilll`; Activity showed one running Java process with redacted `--accessToken`, Forge/ModLauncher output, and the installed root paths. The UI `Stop` action moved the row to `STOPPED`, updated quick status to `No active process`, stamped WinterPack `lastPlayed: "unix:..."`, and a Windows process-table check showed no Java process left. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core profiles -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "installing a pack refreshes"`, and the live desktop install/play/stop walkthrough above.

Latest desktop UI cleanup and repair proof: the running Tauri app on `target/live-smoke/ui-clean-install-proof` was inspected screen-by-screen through the Windows desktop window after the clean install/play proof. Friends no longer seeds fake `Avery`/`Sam` requests or a `BlockedPreview` blocked account, so the real desktop Friends screen shows an honest empty social state until backend/account data exists. Settings now labels the signed-in add flow as `Add another account`, keeps it secondary instead of the primary green action, and tones down account `Remove` until the user enters confirmation; the default Settings surface remains account plus memory, with advanced details behind `Advanced tools`. A real desktop repair proof then deliberately renamed `data/profiles/winterpack/pack.toml` to `pack.toml.bak` inside the isolated proof root, clicked Home `Repair` in the app, and watched Activity stream repair events through completion. Repair restored `pack.toml`, preserved the installed Forge `1.20.1`/WinterPack `2.3.7` profile and 244 mod files, left no Java process running, and the UI quick status showed `Repair profile - completed`. The only remaining destructive desktop proof is profile delete through the app UI; it is intentionally waiting for action-time confirmation before clicking `Confirm delete` on the isolated proof-root WinterPack profile. Verified so far with `npm run build`, `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(friends screen starts|friends screen can send|settings render|settings lists stored|settings native mode)"`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and the live desktop repair walkthrough above.

Latest Library diagnostics wording cleanup: the Library profile diagnostic action no longer says `Command` in the user-facing UI. It is now labeled `Launch details`, opens a `Launch details preview`, distinguishes `Signed-in launch details` vs `Offline launch details`, and copies via `Copy details`/`Copy launch details for <profile>`. The underlying native command-inspection path, redaction, repair/Java/sign-in recovery classification, and copied diagnostic text remain unchanged. While updating the affected tests, stale Activity assertions were also aligned with the current single combined `Refresh` action instead of the old separate `Refresh events`/`Refresh processes` buttons; status assertions now avoid depending on transient refresh ordering when presence/process refreshes race. Verified with `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(launch command action|launch command preflight|successful launch command preview|launch command Java|authenticated launch command|activity screen loads event log fallback|native process refresh failure clears stale process rows)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest process log wording cleanup: process rows now label the log export action as `Save log` instead of the terse `Log`, both in Activity > Processes and in Library profile process actions. The behavior is unchanged: clicking it exports the retained process output, shows the Activity log receipt, and keeps the existing `Open logs` action for revealing the exported file. This makes the running/stopped Minecraft process controls read more like normal desktop actions for new PC users. Verified with `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(activity screen loads event log fallback|native process log export refreshes exited process state|library process log export opens the Activity log receipt|native process log reveal failure keeps exported log receipt|native process action failures preserve process state|exited native process row can relaunch)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest live metadata/command recheck: reran the non-destructive live WinterPack smokes after the desktop UI wording cleanup. `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:plan` still resolves the current Dylan catalog, WinterPack packwiz metadata, and Forge install planning path against `target/live-smoke/winterpack`. `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:metadata` still parses the live legacy catalog plus standard `pack.toml`. `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:auth-command` still uses the saved `Dilll` session in `target/interactive-auth-smoke` to build the stored-authenticated WinterPack Java command without starting Minecraft. An initial parallel metadata run correctly failed on the live-smoke root lock while the plan smoke owned the root; the sequential rerun passed.

Latest desktop minimum-window guard: the native Tauri window baseline is locked to a default `1280x820` and minimum `1100x720` in `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`, matching the CSS `--app-min-width`/`--app-min-height` and the Playwright `tauri-minimum-desktop-chromium` project. `scripts/verify-tauri-security-config.mjs` now enforces that the configured app window keeps `minWidth: 1100`, `minHeight: 720`, and a default size at least as large as the minimum, so the desktop-only layout baseline cannot silently regress. Verified with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(home screen fits the configured desktop minimum width|launcher status card fits long native backend messages at desktop minimum|dense desktop panels fit the configured minimum width)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Settings beginner-UX cleanup: the real Tauri desktop window was inspected again screen-by-screen, with Settings still the highest-clutter surface. The default Settings view now keeps saved Minecraft accounts inside the Minecraft account card instead of as a separate technical list, shows beginner-friendly memory presets (`4 GB`, `6 GB`, `8 GB`) plus the existing custom MB field, shortens the active account status to `Selected account`, and changes the visible account removal action to `Sign out` while preserving the guarded `Confirm remove` flow. Advanced tools remain collapsed and visually secondary. Verified with `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "settings (render|lists stored|save validates|can remove|native account actions|native mode)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Activity process-row clarity cleanup: Activity > Processes now leads managed Minecraft rows with the resolved launcher profile name (for example `WinterPack`) instead of raw `javaw.exe`, while keeping the executable, PID, exit code, and launch-argument count as secondary diagnostics and preserving the existing `javaw.exe process actions` accessibility/test hook. Empty Activity copy no longer references unfinished "live streaming" work; it now explains that installs, repairs, launches, and errors appear there. Verified with `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(library play action opens native process supervisor|installed pack card play action opens native process supervisor|activity screen loads event log fallback|native process log export refreshes exited process state|library process log export opens the Activity log receipt|native process action failures preserve process state|exited native process row can relaunch|stopping a native process)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest parallel-play gating cleanup: installing/updating/repairing one profile no longer disables Play for unrelated installed profiles. The frontend now distinguishes target-specific lifecycle work from global mutation serialization: install/repair/delete style mutations remain guarded, duplicate launch starts remain guarded, and the affected profile remains blocked, but another ready profile can launch while WinterPack is installing/updating. Added Playwright coverage that starts a pending WinterPack update from Library, returns to Library, and successfully launches `Latest Release` while the update is still pending. Verified with `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(library can launch another profile while a pack update is installing|library play action is disabled while native launch is pending|library play action opens native process supervisor|pack update action is disabled while the target profile process is running)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Java/download/package slice: launcher settings and profiles now support optional Java runtime override paths while keeping automatic Java selection as the default. Launch planning chooses per-profile override, then global override, then the existing Minecraft-version-based discovered runtime selection, and pack metadata refresh preserves per-profile Java overrides. Settings hides the global Java override under Advanced tools, Library Customize exposes a per-profile `Java override` path, and profile summaries show `Automatic Java`/`Custom Java`. The Microsoft public client id `d10dfc60-1a42-44a8-b3af-edf4f5ee2c1f` is now bundled as the packaged-app fallback while `THEBOYS_MICROSOFT_CLIENT_ID` still overrides it for development. Download execution now runs independent file downloads concurrently with a bounded default concurrency of 8 (`THEBOYS_DOWNLOAD_CONCURRENCY`, capped at 32), serializes duplicate destinations, and user-facing download events say `files` instead of `artifacts`; the sidebar status card now shows live file-download progress without requiring Activity refresh. The install-from-home regression is covered so a completed pack install must make the installed profile visible in Library. `npm run tauri:build` succeeded and produced `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe`, `target/release/bundle/msi/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64_en-US.msi`, and `target/release/bundle/nsis/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64-setup.exe`; `npm run verify:tauri-bundles` verified the release bundles. The release exe was launched directly and inspected through the Windows desktop window: it showed `Dilll` signed in, WinterPack ready, and Library profiles including WinterPack with `Automatic Java`. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core download_plan_executor -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher microsoft_client_id -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "(live file download events surface|library profile editor syncs native refreshed profile values after save|installing a pack refreshes the native bootstrap snapshot status)"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run tauri:build`, and the packaged-exe desktop smoke.

Latest Clean Dark Glassless and vanilla compatibility slice: Home/pack details, Library/profile customize, Settings, Friends, Activity, Import, and visible file-progress surfaces were aligned further toward the flat dark desktop direction. Library filtering now has a clear toolbar, profile game versions use dropdowns, Settings keeps Java/JVM controls hidden under Advanced, Friends has a simple play-together/search layout, Import has a guided Scan/Review/Import overview, and Activity/sidebar progress displays user-facing `file` wording instead of raw artifact language. Added `scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` plus `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat`, which selects either explicit versions, every Mojang manifest version, or a representative sample of latest release/latest snapshot/latest old beta/latest old alpha, runs each in an isolated `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat/<version>` root, launches/stops the real Java process, and cleans each root by default. The first sample exposed real gaps that were fixed: modern `26.x` metadata now uses Mojang's declared `javaVersion.majorVersion` and managed Java 25 recommendations; native extraction mirrors files into `natives/<version>/java` for metadata that appends `/java`; legacy `${auth_session}` and `${game_assets}` placeholders are supported; legacy assets-dir-only launch args are allowed; and Java 8-era profiles now require Java 8 instead of accidentally selecting Java 17/21. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --sample`, which passed `26.2`, `26.3-snapshot-1`, `b1.8.1`, and `a1.2.6` with cleanup and no leftover Java processes, plus `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check --workspace`, focused launcher-core regression tests for Java/runtime/assets/native extraction, and targeted Playwright UI tests for Import, Activity, Friends, and Settings.

Latest packaged-exe launch window fix: the release executable was rebuilt and launched directly through the Windows desktop app. A real signed-in WinterPack `Play` click previously spawned Minecraft through `java.exe` with a separate command/console-style window; launcher-core now applies the Windows `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` creation flag to shared child process launches while leaving non-Windows behavior unchanged. The rebuilt packaged exe showed the Clean Dark Glassless shell, `Dilll` signed in, and WinterPack ready; clicking `Play` opened the actual Minecraft/Forge loading window without an extra command prompt, and the Java test process was stopped afterward with no leftover `java`/`javaw` processes. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core windows_child_processes_use_no_console_creation_flag`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check --workspace`, `npm run tauri:build`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and the packaged-exe desktop Play smoke.

Latest vanilla compatibility matrix expansion: `scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` now has a `--matrix` mode that selects the sample set plus representative release-era, pre-release/snapshot, beta, and alpha pivots from Mojang's live manifest. The current matrix dry-run selected 16 versions: `26.2`, `26.3-snapshot-1`, `b1.8.1`, `a1.2.6`, `1.21.11`, `1.20.6`, `1.18.2`, `1.16.5`, `1.13.2`, `1.7.10`, `1.0`, `1.21.11-rc3`, `1.13.2-pre2`, `1.7.10-pre4`, `b1.7.3`, and `a1.1.2_01`. The prior sample had already passed the first four; this pass ran the remaining 12 in two live batches. All 12 installed their vanilla artifacts in isolated roots, built launch plans, spawned the real Java client, survived startup, stopped through the process registry, and cleaned their per-version roots afterward. Follow-up checks showed no leftover `java`/`javaw`/launcher processes and no leftover directories under `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat`. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --matrix --dry-run`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 1.21.11 --version 1.20.6 --version 1.18.2 --version 1.16.5 --version 1.13.2 --version 1.7.10 --version 1.0`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 1.21.11-rc3 --version 1.13.2-pre2 --version 1.7.10-pre4 --version b1.7.3 --version a1.1.2_01`, `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest vanilla compatibility chunking and beta sweep: the live compatibility runner now supports resumable manifest chunks for the eventual full Mojang manifest campaign: `--all --type <release|snapshot|old_beta|old_alpha> --offset <n> --limit <n>`. Dry-runs confirmed the current first chunks for release, snapshot, old_beta, and old_alpha. The first old_beta chunk then passed all 10 selected versions: `b1.8.1`, `b1.8`, `b1.7.3`, `b1.7.2`, `b1.7`, `b1.6.6`, `b1.6.5`, `b1.6.4`, `b1.6.3`, and `b1.6.2`. Each version installed in an isolated launcher root, launched the real Java client, survived startup, stopped through the registry, and cleaned its root. Follow-up checks again showed no leftover Java/launcher processes and no leftover `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat` roots. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type release --limit 10 --dry-run`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --limit 10 --dry-run`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type old_beta --limit 10 --dry-run`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type old_alpha --limit 10 --dry-run`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type old_beta --limit 10`, `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check --workspace`.

Latest vanilla alpha sweep: the first old_alpha chunk passed all 10 selected versions from Mojang's current manifest: `a1.2.6`, `a1.2.5`, `a1.2.4_01`, `a1.2.3_04`, `a1.2.3_02`, `a1.2.3_01`, `a1.2.3`, `a1.2.2b`, `a1.2.2a`, and `a1.2.1_01`. Each version installed into its isolated compatibility root, launched the real Java client, survived startup, stopped through the process registry, and cleaned the root. Follow-up checks showed no leftover `java`/`javaw`/launcher processes and no leftover directories under `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat`. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type old_alpha --limit 10 --dry-run`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type old_alpha --limit 10`, `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check --workspace`. Latest vanilla snapshot continuation 3: the fourth snapshot chunk at `--offset 15` selected `26.2-snapshot-2`, `26.1.2-rc-1`, `26.2-snapshot-1`, `26w14a`, and `26.1.1-rc-1`. The first batch found a real Java selection gap: `26w14a` requires Java 25 from Mojang's version metadata, but the live compatibility setup installed Java before the version JSON was cached, so it only discovered Java 17 and Java 8. Fixed this in `crates/launcher-core/src/lib.rs` by moving live vanilla managed-Java setup until after the vanilla download plan caches Mojang version metadata, making `ensure_live_managed_java_for_profile` use `required_java_major_for_profile_version` with cached details, and adding a `26w14a` fallback assertion to `required_java_major_tracks_minecraft_generations`. Mojang's game argument metadata for `1.21.11-pre1` and `1.21.11-pre2` is identical, so this is currently tracked as a prerelease client crash rather than a launcher argument/download gap. Added `--auth-session-file <path>` to `scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` and `THEBOYS_VANILLA_COMPAT_AUTH=stored` handling in the ignored Rust live smoke so deterministic auth-sensitive cases can be retested against a real stored Minecraft session without copying secrets into the repo. Follow-up checks showed no leftover Java/launcher processes and no leftover `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat` roots.
