Latest vanilla snapshot continuation 70: the first delegated worker lane completed offsets `450`, `455`, `460`, `465`, and `470`, passing all 25 selected versions from `1.13-pre8` through `18w10c` with serial isolated roots and no leftover lane roots or locks. Local parallel coverage then passed offset range `475-499` after a single transient network retry for `18w03a`. The next local chunk, `500-524`, exposed a deterministic old-snapshot narrator/JNA crash on `17w17b`, `17w17a`, `17w16b`, `17w16a`, `17w15a`, `17w14a`, `17w13b`, and `17w13a`: those versions ship `net.java.dev.jna:jna:4.2.2`, which crashes while initializing the Windows text-to-speech narrator on the current Windows/Java combination. The launcher core now applies a focused compatibility override only for version metadata that declares that old JNA coordinate: it downloads verified `net.java.dev.jna:jna:5.14.0`, prepends it to the Minecraft classpath, skips the old `jna:4.2.2` classpath entry, and gives each launched process an isolated Java/JNA temp directory under the profile working directory. Focused tests cover the download plan and classpath override, and the real live smokes `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 17w17a --quiet`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 17w13a --quiet`, and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 500 --limit 25 --jobs 5 --quiet` now pass.

Latest Discover/provider update: Prism Launcher source was inspected under ignored `target/prism-source` as behavior reference for provider boundaries. Prism detects local archive imports by top-level `modrinth.index.json` for Modrinth, `manifest.json` for CurseForge/Flame, `bin/modpack.jar` or `bin/version.json` for Technic, and `instance.cfg` for MultiMC/Prism-like archives; its network provider pages separately cover ATLauncher, CurseForge/Flame, FTB current, FTB Legacy, Modrinth, and Technic. TheBoysLauncher now has a first local provider slice: `install_modpack_archive` can download a standard CurseForge zip URL, parse `manifest.json`, preserve the exact primary loader version such as `forge-43.4.23`, safely extract declared `overrides/` into the profile, store the original manifest under `.theboys/curseforge/manifest.json`, download required CurseForge files with direct `downloadUrl`s through the existing parallel downloader, prepare vanilla artifacts/native libraries, install modloader artifacts/processors, persist the profile, and refresh Library from the React Discover screen. The initial implementation intentionally requires direct `downloadUrl`s, so CurseForge exports that omit them still need a future CurseForge API/server-side resolver. The new Discover tab shows provider cards for CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic; only CurseForge archive URL install is active so far, while search/catalog browsing is the next provider slice. Future UX note from the user: the permanent Import tab should eventually be replaced by first-run/subsequent-start import suggestions when existing launchers/profiles are detected, but that migration is intentionally deferred for now. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_archive -- --nocapture`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check --workspace`, `npm run build`, and `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`.

Latest CurseForge archive validation: the real user-provided Enigmatica 9 Expert archive at `https://i.dylan.lol/dylan/Enigmatica9Expert-1.27.0.zip` was inspected without running a full install. The zip is about 12 MB, contains a standard `manifest.json`, resolves to Minecraft `1.19.2` with primary loader `forge-43.4.23`, includes `overrides`, and all 232 required CurseForge file entries include direct `downloadUrl`s. That means the current first-slice CurseForge archive installer should be able to handle this exact archive shape; the remaining higher-risk validation is a full native install/launch smoke once the vanilla compatibility workers are no longer saturating Cargo/Java. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_archive -- --test-threads=1` and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest setup UX update: the pack details More menu no longer exposes the manual `Verify files` maintenance action. Normal users should press Play and let launch preflight/automatic setup install missing files in the background, with failed setup retries surfaced only from Activity when there is an actual failure. The renderer operation summary now treats terminal lifecycle events such as `Profile repair completed.` as the latest operation state even when an earlier synthetic UI active marker remains in local event state, which prevents the bottom-left quick status from getting stuck on `Set up files - active` after setup has completed. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "terminal repair event|home details action|pack play setup|failed native repair|home repair action"` and `npm run build`.

Latest setup UX refinement: Play now proactively runs setup for packs that already report `repair_needed` before attempting to spawn Java, then launches after setup succeeds. This removes the avoidable first failed launch event for known missing-file states while keeping the same automatic retry behavior for unknown launch-preflight failures. The new profile form now defaults to a compact beginner-friendly layout with only name/version controls visible; loader and memory remain available under Advanced, and profile customization uses the same short `Advanced`/`Basic` toggle language to avoid wrapped controls. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|library new profile|pack play setup|library profile launch actions hide manual repair"` and `npm run build`.

Latest sidebar progress refinement: the bottom-left launcher status card now renders an accessible progress bar even for active queued/starting download events at `0%`, using a small pulsing accent segment until real progress arrives. This keeps artifact/file setup visibly live from the first event instead of appearing stuck until progress exceeds zero, while completed download/setup events still clear the bar. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface"` and `npm run build`.

Latest CI/release optimization: `.github/workflows/v4-foundation.yml` no longer runs its full verification suite on `main` and `dev` pushes, because `.github/workflows/v4-release-channels.yml` already performs the heavy Rust/frontend checks and packaged release build/publish flow for those branches. `v4-foundation` still runs on PRs, manual dispatch, and `codex/**` pushes for development validation, reducing duplicate GitHub Actions minutes while preserving release gates. Verified with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and `node scripts/resolve-v4-release-version.mjs dev/stable`.

Latest update-channel coverage: Playwright now verifies the Settings Stable/Dev channel switch beyond the visible segmented buttons: selecting Dev, checking updates, reading the mocked `latest-dev.json`, surfacing the available Dev build, and passing the trusted GitHub release installer URL to the native `open_external_url` command. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings .*launcher update channels|settings dev channel"` and `npm run build`.

Latest release-plumbing update: `.github/workflows/v4-foundation.yml` now runs on pull requests, pushes to `main`/`dev`/`codex/**`, manual dispatch, and `v*` tags. The existing Windows verification job remains intact, and a new Windows packaging job runs `npm run tauri:build`, verifies the generated MSI/NSIS bundles with `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and uploads the installer artifacts for every successful run. Tag pushes publish the uploaded Windows installers to the matching GitHub Release automatically; manual dispatch can also publish by setting `publish_release=true`, with `release_tag` required when the selected ref is not already a `v*` tag, plus optional `release_draft` and `prerelease`.

Release secrets/config assumptions:

- `GITHUB_TOKEN` is enough for the workflow to create/update GitHub Releases on tag/manual release runs because the publish job grants `contents: write`.
- `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD` must be added as GitHub Actions secrets before release tags can produce updater signatures. The local development keypair used to validate this flow lives under ignored `target/tauri-updater.key`; do not commit the private key.
- Updater runtime support is now wired through `tauri-plugin-updater` and `tauri-plugin-process`, with the committed public key in `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` and endpoint `https://github.com/dilllxd/theboyslauncher/releases/latest/download/latest.json`. The React shell checks for updates once after native startup, exposes a simple Settings update card, downloads/installs available signed updates, then relaunches.
- `npm run tauri:build` now emits MSI/NSIS installers, `.sig` updater signatures when signing secrets are present, and `target/release/bundle/latest.json` through `scripts/generate-tauri-updater-manifest.mjs`. `npm run verify:tauri-bundles` now verifies both installers and the updater manifest.
- To save GitHub Actions minutes, the workflow's normal pull-request and branch push path runs verification only. The expensive Windows packaging job runs only on `v*` tags or manual dispatch with `package_windows=true` or `publish_release=true`; publishing still requires a tag or manual `release_tag`.
- Recommended release flow for tonight: align the bundle version/tag, push the release commit, create and push a tag such as `v4.0.0`, let `V4 Foundation` finish, then inspect the draft/published release assets for the MSI, NSIS installer, `.sig` files, and `latest.json` before announcing it. Manual release publishing must pass the same value through `release_tag`; the workflow forwards that value into `THEBOYS_RELEASE_TAG` so generated `latest.json` points at the release being published.

Latest release-plumbing update 2: auto-update is now configured end to end for Windows releases. Added `tauri-plugin-updater`/`tauri-plugin-process`, a default Tauri capability for updater/process permissions, committed the updater public key, generated a local ignored updater keypair for validation, and added a Settings card plus startup check/install/relaunch flow. The GitHub workflow is optimized for billing: PRs and normal branch pushes run checks only, while Windows installer packaging runs only for `v*` tags or explicit manual packaging/publishing. Local signed packaging succeeded with `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` set from `target/tauri-updater.key` and produced `target/release/bundle/msi/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64_en-US.msi`, `target/release/bundle/nsis/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64-setup.exe`, both `.sig` files, and generated `target/release/bundle/latest.json`. Verified with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check --workspace`, `npm run tauri:build` using the local signing key, `npm run generate:updater-manifest`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, `node --check scripts/generate-tauri-updater-manifest.mjs`, `node --check scripts/verify-tauri-bundles.mjs`, and `npx prettier --check .github/workflows/v4-foundation.yml`.

Latest release-plumbing update 3: the updater signing key was rotated from the temporary local-dev password to a fresh generated password. `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` now contains the rotated updater public key, and GitHub Actions secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` plus `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD` were set successfully through authenticated `gh` for `dilllxd/theboyslauncher`. The local private key/password remain only under ignored `target/tauri-updater.key` and `target/tauri-updater-password.txt`. A signed `npm run tauri:build` with the rotated key produced MSI/NSIS bundles, both `.sig` files, and `latest.json` for `v4.0.0`; `npm run verify:tauri-bundles` passed against those artifacts. The Settings Playwright smoke now asserts the user-facing Launcher updates card remains visible. Verified with `gh secret list`, signed `npm run tauri:build`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, `cargo check --workspace`, and `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "settings render memory and offline profile data"`.

Latest hosted-deploy update: the social backend now has a Linux Docker deployment path for WSL/server hosting instead of relying on players' PCs. Added `crates/social-backend/Dockerfile`, `scripts/manage-hosted-social-backend.mjs`, npm scripts `social:build`, `social:up`, `social:down`, `social:restart`, `social:logs`, `social:status`, and `social:secret`, plus `.github/workflows/social-backend-image.yml` for an optimized GitHub Container Registry build/push path. The image workflow runs only on `v*` tags or manual dispatch so normal PR/branch checks do not spend Docker build minutes. Local WSL Docker does not have the Compose plugin, so the management script uses direct `docker` commands through either Windows Docker or `wsl docker`: it creates a private Docker network, starts Postgres, starts `theboyslauncher/social-backend:local`, waits for `/health`, and exposes `127.0.0.1:4074` by default for a reverse proxy. Required production knobs: set a strong `THEBOYS_BACKEND_SESSION_SECRET` (`npm run social:secret` prints a candidate), set real Postgres credentials through `THEBOYS_POSTGRES_*`, set `THEBOYS_BACKEND_HOST`/`THEBOYS_BACKEND_PORT` if the reverse proxy needs a different bind, and keep `THEBOYS_BACKEND_CORS_ORIGINS=https://launcher.dylan.lol` unless the release domain changes. Verified with `npm run social:build` through WSL Docker, a throwaway-secret `npm run social:up`, `GET http://127.0.0.1:4074/health` returning `200 {"ok":true,"service":"social-backend"}`, `npm run social:down`, `npm run social:status`, `node --check scripts/manage-hosted-social-backend.mjs`, and `npx prettier --check .github/workflows/social-backend-image.yml`.

Latest branch migration note: this local checkout has now fetched `origin/main` and `origin/dev`. GitHub has `main` at `022b8e3c81c0132140cff279387e10110b03d893`, `dev` at `40b3b19d75542050f38e32b137897ddab0a29873`, and the rewrite branch at `f5abc72382a1b9883c842e6c389d074d2475c7af`. The non-destructive backup branch `backup/main-v3-2026-06-29` has been pushed to GitHub at the current v3 `main` commit, so there is now a rollback/reference point before any v4 main replacement. Because the current rewrite branch is unrelated to the old v3 main history, moving v4 onto `main` still requires explicit approval for a force-with-lease push or a GitHub PR strategy that intentionally replaces main. See `docs/RELEASE_RUNBOOK.md` for the current release commands and required secrets.

Latest release-readiness update 4: the v4 rewrite branch is now pushed and green at `9cff831ea6ed57886ca5736f73622e1b5bbf2bdb`. GitHub Actions run `28343669378` succeeded on `codex/fresh-foundation-rewrite`, covering Rust formatting, workspace check, launcher-core tests, social-backend tests, frontend build, Tauri security verification, debug backend resource staging, Playwright install, and minimum desktop Playwright smoke. A manual non-publishing package validation run `28343968252` also succeeded with `package_windows=true`, `publish_release=false`, and `release_tag=v4.0.0`; it built signed Tauri bundles, ran `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and uploaded `TheBoysLauncher-windows-9cff831ea6ed57886ca5736f73622e1b5bbf2bdb`. Downloaded artifact contents were verified locally: `latest.json`, `msi/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64_en-US.msi`, `msi/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64_en-US.msi.sig`, `nsis/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64-setup.exe`, and `nsis/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64-setup.exe.sig`. The `latest.json` points at `https://github.com/dilllxd/theboyslauncher/releases/download/v4.0.0/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64-setup.exe`, so the actual release tag/published release still needs to be created after explicit approval to replace `main`. The v4 workflow path filters now include `scripts/generate-tauri-updater-manifest.mjs` so updater-manifest changes trigger verification.

Latest hosted-deploy verification 2: the hosted backend WSL/Docker path was revalidated after the final release packaging checkpoint. With a throwaway `THEBOYS_BACKEND_SESSION_SECRET`, `npm run social:build` rebuilt `theboyslauncher/social-backend:local`, `npm run social:up` created the Docker network/Postgres/backend containers, and `GET http://127.0.0.1:4074/health` returned `{"ok":true,"service":"social-backend"}`. `npm run social:status` showed both `theboyslauncher-hosted-postgres` and `theboyslauncher-social-backend` running, then `npm run social:down` removed both and a final `npm run social:status` showed both missing. GitHub currently does not expose `.github/workflows/social-backend-image.yml` through the Actions workflow API because the workflow file is not on default `main` yet; it should become dispatchable after v4 replaces `main` or after a release tag includes that workflow.

Latest setup-friction polish: the frontend now sanitizes native launch/setup errors before showing them in the desktop shell, so raw guidance like `Install or repair the profile before launching.` is replaced with friendlier automatic setup wording. Failed setup recovery buttons now say `Set up again` instead of a generic retry/repair-style action, while the existing native `repair_profile` command remains an internal implementation detail. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pack play setup|library profile launch actions hide manual repair"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "failed native repair surfaces"`, and `npm run build`. The five delegated vanilla snapshot compatibility lanes are still running in parallel over the remaining assigned offsets; do not duplicate those lanes unless a worker finishes or fails.

Latest profile-picker polish: the new-profile and customize profile version dropdowns now show an inline friendly empty-state hint when a selected category such as Snapshots, Old beta, or Old alpha has no manifest entries available, instead of leaving a disabled version dropdown unexplained. This keeps raw manual version entry out of the normal flow while making manifest/fallback limits understandable. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library new profile action|library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor can choose snapshot"` and `npm run build`.

Latest sidebar progress hardening: the bottom-left launcher status now clears older active `download_artifacts` events after any later install/setup lifecycle completion, even when the download subject is a shared Minecraft version like `1.20.1` rather than the pack id. This covers the previously observed `Verified modloader installer processor 6/6 outputs` message sticking after the pack had already completed. Newer active download events still surface normally. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface"` and `npm run build`.

Latest launch-concurrency polish: Activity completion rows now use per-profile lifecycle checks for their `Play` action instead of the global install/setup lock, so a user can launch a ready profile while an unrelated pack install is still running. Pack cards already used per-profile lifecycle checks for Play; this closes the remaining Activity surface. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity play stays available"` and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged-state verification: existing local Windows bundles remain present under `target/release/bundle` (`msi/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64_en-US.msi`, `nsis/TheBoysLauncher_4.0.0_x64-setup.exe`, signatures, and `latest.json`), and `npm run verify:tauri-bundles` verified the two release bundles plus updater manifest for `4.0.0`. `npm run verify:tauri-security` passed. `npm run verify:package-resources` initially caught a local staged sidecar mismatch, then `npm run stage:social-backend && npm run verify:package-resources` restaged and verified both `src-tauri/resources/social-backend.exe` and `src-tauri/resources/social-backend` against the current release binary hash `7f5b8b257cc00ad39b582b4b896683f00cc2693fa846614ec83ff291af44cb90`; this did not leave tracked worktree changes.

Latest sidebar active-operation polish: when there is no live file-download event yet, the bottom-left launcher status now shows the latest active non-download launcher operation with its progress bar, so a pending install/setup plan no longer looks idle before individual files start streaming. `download_artifacts` stays routed through the stale-aware download selector so completed installs still clear old processor/file messages. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pending native install .*sidebar|pending native install plan surfaces|live file download events surface"` and `npm run build`.

Latest setup-recovery polish: the sidebar's generic missing-file recovery action now says `Set up again` instead of `Try again`, keeping repair/check-files language out of the normal flow. Successful `repair_profile` receipts are also merged into the local launcher event stream as terminal completion events, so sparse native event logs cannot leave synthetic `Setup is running` progress stuck after setup succeeds. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "launch command preflight failure offers setup recovery|successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery|successful pack update clears stale launch repair recovery|library delete action clears stale launch recovery"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pending native install .*sidebar|pending native install plan surfaces|live file download events surface"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest native setup wording polish: the Tauri bridge no longer tells users to stop a running profile before "repairing" when setup is blocked by an active process; the message now says to stop it before `setting up files`. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher active_managed_process_for_profile_matches_running_and_stop_requested_processes -- --test-threads=1` and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest automatic-launch setup wording polish: automatic missing-artifact setup failures in the Tauri launch path now say `Automatic profile setup before launch failed` instead of `Automatic profile repair before launch failed`. A focused regression test locks that wording. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher automatic -- --test-threads=1` and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest automatic-setup status polish: the frontend now says `Setting up missing files before launch/joining` instead of `Installing missing files` for automatic missing-artifact recovery paths, keeping pack installation separate from profile setup in user-visible status. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pack play setup refreshes|friend join surfaces native launch preflight"` and `npm run build`.

Latest renderer setup-wording fallback: renderer-side native error sanitization now also rewrites legacy/cached `Automatic profile repair before launch failed:` messages to `Automatic profile setup before launch failed:`. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "launch command preflight failure offers setup recovery|failed native repair surfaces"` and `npm run build`.

Latest launch concurrency backend fix: the Tauri launch commands no longer reject profile launches just because an unrelated install/setup/delete lifecycle operation is active. Launch still reuses or rejects by per-profile active process state, while mutating lifecycle operations keep the global gate. This matches the intended UI behavior where a ready instance can be opened while another pack is installing. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher lifecycle_operation_gate -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher active_managed_process_for_profile_matches_running_and_stop_requested_processes -- --test-threads=1`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest launch concurrency UI fix: sidebar launch-recovery Play buttons and Activity process-row relaunch now use the target profile lifecycle state instead of the global lifecycle busy flag, so unrelated installs no longer disable relaunch/play affordances for a ready profile. The Activity relaunch regression now starts an unrelated pack install and still relaunches an exited WinterPack process. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "exited native process row can relaunch|activity play stays available"` and `npm run build`.

Latest vanilla snapshot worker result: the delegated `615-674` snapshot lane passed all 60 selected versions from `1.8.2-pre6` through `14w08a` using `--all --type snapshot --offset 615 --limit 60 --jobs 1 --quiet`. `1.8-pre2` had one transient exit `4294967295` and then passed on the required exact-version retry. The worker reported no failures after retry, no leftover roots from that exact slice, and no leftover Java/javaw processes. Remaining active coverage lanes at the time of this note were `350-414`, `525-554`, `555-614`, plus the old-snapshot single retry lane descended from the `675-735` worker.

Latest legacy snapshot launch fix: the `675-735` worker found deterministic failures on `13w23a`, `13w22a`, `13w21b`, `13w21a`, `13w19a`, `13w18c`, `13w18b`, `13w18a`, `13w17a`, `13w16b`, and `13w16a` because those old `minecraftArguments` versions reject the modern `--width/--height` flags. The launcher core now appends fallback resolution flags only when no version metadata is available; metadata-provided modern `arguments` still controls custom resolution, and legacy `minecraftArguments` strings are respected exactly. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core legacy_minecraft_arguments_do_not_get_modern_resolution_flags -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core launch_plan_can_use_minecraft_version_json_arguments_and_main_class -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core launch_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 13w23a --quiet`, and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 13w23a --version 13w22a --version 13w21b --version 13w21a --version 13w19a --version 13w18c --version 13w18b --version 13w18a --version 13w17a --version 13w16b --version 13w16a --jobs 5 --quiet`; all 11 previously failing versions passed.

Latest vanilla snapshot gap follow-up: the `415` worker passed `19w09a` through `19w04b`, then aborted on a stale/in-use `19w04a` root, while its separate `525-554` lane passed all 30 selected versions from `16w43a` through `1.9-pre3`. After confirming no active process owned `19w04a`, the stale `19w04a` root/lock was removed and the skipped `415` tail was resumed. The long resumed run progressed through the gap and left only tail roots when the parent shell timed out; the tail roots were then cleared and explicitly verified with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 18w31a --version 18w30a --version 1.13-pre10 --version 1.13-pre9 --jobs 2 --quiet`, which passed all four. Remaining active coverage lanes at the time of this note were `350-414` and `555-614`.

Latest setup wording core cleanup: the launcher core now emits `Setup queued for ...` and `Setup is ready to start.` for `repair_profile` operation plans instead of sending new native logs through user-facing repair wording first. The renderer still sanitizes older cached `Repair queued`/`Repair plan` logs as a fallback, and its local-plan supersede logic now recognizes the new setup-ready message so failed native setup events replace stale synthetic `Setup is running` sidebar state. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "failed native repair surfaces|home repair action shows pending native repair state|terminal repair event"` and `npm run build`. A focused Rust unit rerun was deferred until the active live vanilla compatibility cargo processes release the Windows test binary.

Latest verification refresh: while the remaining compatibility lanes continued, a broad Rust workspace check passed in an isolated target directory with `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=target/codex-workspace-check cargo check --workspace`, avoiding the live-smoke workers' Windows test-binary locks. Release packaging checks still pass: `npm run verify:tauri-security` and `npm run verify:tauri-bundles` both succeeded. The remaining active vanilla snapshot lanes at the time of this note were `350-414` and `555-614`.

Latest vanilla compatibility parallelization: the runner now recovers exact stale per-version `.lock` directories when their recorded owner PID is no longer running, while still refusing roots whose owner PID is alive and reporting the owning version/PID. Verified the script change with `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` and `node scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs --version 1.21.8 --dry-run`. Five non-overlapping compatibility lanes are active: snapshot offsets `350-414` and `555-614` from earlier delegated workers, plus new delegated lanes for `release` (`--jobs 3`), `old_beta` (`--jobs 2`), and `old_alpha` (`--jobs 2`). Last observed live roots showed ongoing progress through snapshots around `1.15-pre4` and `15w35e`, releases around `1.21.x`, beta around `b1.6.x`, and alpha around `a1.2.0.x`; do not duplicate those lanes unless a worker finishes or fails.

Latest profile-creator hardening: native profile creation now treats a failed Mojang version-manifest load as a blocking state instead of silently falling back to preview release versions. The New profile form clears the selected version, disables the version dropdown/Create action, and shows `Minecraft versions could not load. Check your connection and try again.` while preserving preview fallback behavior for the web mock. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|profile creator blocks"` and `npm run build`. Packaging/update checks were also refreshed with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and `npm run verify:package-resources`.

Latest vanilla family compatibility results: the delegated `old_alpha` lane passed all 35 Mojang old-alpha versions with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type old_alpha --jobs 2 --quiet`. The delegated `old_beta` lane passed 25 of 26 versions and failed `b1.2_02` only because C: ran out of disk while writing the managed Java 8 download; after removing exact old generated proof roots under `target/live-smoke` (`neoforge`, `fabric`, `ui-clean-install-proof`, `ui-proof`, `winterpack`, `vanilla`, `delete-proof`) C: free space rose to about 14.4 GB and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version b1.2_02 --quiet` passed. Treat old beta coverage as all 26 passed after exact retry. The release lane and two remaining snapshot lanes are still active.

Latest snapshot lane result: the delegated snapshot `555-614` lane passed all 60 selected versions with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 555 --limit 60 --jobs 1 --quiet`. `16w06a` and `15w43c` had initial failures and both passed exact-version retry; no failures remained after retry. The worker reported no leftover cargo/java processes or roots attributable to that slice. Remaining compatibility lanes are snapshot `350-414` and full `release`. Low-conflict verification also passed with `cargo fmt --all --check` and `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=target/codex-goal-check cargo check --workspace`.

Latest release compatibility result: the delegated full `release` lane passed 99 of 102 Mojang releases on first pass with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type release --jobs 3 --quiet`. Initial failures were `1.21.6` and `1.21.2` transient asset download errors plus `1.19` exiting during startup with `Some(-1073741819)` while building the data fixer. The exact retry `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --version 1.21.6 --version 1.21.2 --version 1.19 --jobs 3 --quiet` passed all three, so release coverage is 102/102 after retry. Remaining vanilla compatibility lane is snapshot `350-414`.

Latest vanilla compatibility completion: the final delegated snapshot `350-414` lane passed all 65 selected versions with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 350 --limit 65 --jobs 1 --quiet`, including `20w13b` through `19w11a`; no retry failures remained. Combined with earlier documented lanes, the vanilla coverage sweep now has passing evidence for all Mojang releases, old beta, old alpha, and all snapshot offsets previously left open after `240`, including the recovered `415` gap and the old-snapshot `675-735` fixes. Final cleanup check found no leftover `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat` roots and no live `node`/`cargo`/`java`/`javaw`/`rustup` vanilla-smoke processes; C: had about 15.4 GB free.

Latest automatic Java recovery UI polish: native launch failures that are already being handled by automatic managed-Java setup are now rewritten in the renderer to friendly `Preparing Java ... automatically for this Minecraft version.` messages instead of surfacing raw `Install a managed Java runtime from Settings before launching.` guidance. Activity rows and the bottom-left recovery area only show the Java recovery button when manual Java recovery is actually required, so packaged-app launch from a fresh profile should not ask new users to go to Settings while the launcher is already downloading/installing the right runtime. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library play action surfaces native Java runtime recovery and retry|missing managed Java executable path auto-installs Java and retries|launch command Java preflight failure offers Java recovery"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run verify:tauri-security`.

Latest packaged process-status polish: native managed-process updates now use compact user-facing sidebar text such as `Latest Release is running` instead of streaming raw Java executable paths or stdout lines into the bottom-left status chip. Raw process output remains available from Activity > Processes > View output. The updated `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` was rebuilt with `npx tauri build --no-bundle` and smoke-tested against `target/packaged-goal-smoke-java-polish`: Library > Latest Release > Play automatically used the managed Java 21 runtime, opened Minecraft 1.21.8, showed `1 active process`, and kept the bottom-left status friendly while the process was running. The full `npm run tauri:build` produced the exe/MSI/NSIS bundles but stopped at updater signing because this local shell does not have `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY`; CI or a signing-enabled shell still needs that secret for signed updater metadata. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library play action opens native process supervisor after launch"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity noise polish: the Events tab now keeps high-signal lifecycle failures/completions visible while hiding per-file download chatter behind a collapsed download-details section. This keeps large vanilla/modpack installs from filling the Activity screen with hundreds of asset-object rows, while preserving exact file-level debugging details when opened. Sidebar live file progress is unchanged and still shows the current active file. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface|pending native install .*sidebar|pending native install plan surfaces"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest profile customization cleanup: Library profile customization now mirrors the beginner-friendly New profile flow. The default customize surface keeps only Name, Version type, and Version visible; loader, memory, window size, server, JVM args, and per-profile Java override all live under the collapsed Advanced section. This keeps Java/loader/memory controls out of normal screens while preserving full power-user edits. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor can choose snapshot versions from the manifest|library new profile action"` and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged profile-customize smoke: after rebuilding `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` with `npx tauri build --no-bundle`, the real desktop app was launched against `target/packaged-goal-smoke-ui-latest` and Library > Latest Release > Customize was inspected with Computer Use. The default editor shows only Name/Version controls, Advanced opens loader/memory/window/server/JVM/Java override, and the Advanced/Basic toggle now has a fixed-width action column so its label no longer collapses in the packaged app. Verified again with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|library new profile action"` and `npm run build`.

Latest native setup wording cleanup: launcher-core missing-artifact and modloader-processor preflight errors no longer emit `Install or repair the profile...`; they now say `Set up the profile files...` while preserving the precise missing artifact paths for automatic setup detection and diagnostics. The renderer still keeps legacy sanitizers/tests for older cached messages, but new Rust/Tauri errors use setup wording at the source. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core process_command_spec -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core modloader_processor_command_spec_rejects_missing_classpath_entries -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher missing_launch_artifact_errors_trigger_automatic_repair -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace`.

Latest profile creator name-sync polish: the New profile form now keeps its friendly auto-generated profile name in sync with the selected Minecraft version until the user edits the name manually. Changing from Release to Snapshot now updates the default from `Minecraft 1.21.8` to the selected snapshot name, while a typed custom name is preserved across later version changes. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes"` and `npm run build`.

Latest vanilla compatibility harness polish: `scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` now retries failed versions once by default before failing the campaign, logs retry attempts with an `attempt` field in `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat-results.jsonl`, and keeps `--retry-failures 0` available for strict first-pass-only runs. This matches the manual exact-version retry workflow used during the full sweep and reduces agent babysitting on transient Mojang/download/JVM startup failures. Verified with `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 207 --limit 2 --dry-run`, and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 207 --limit 2 --retry-failures 0 --dry-run`.

Latest vanilla compatibility reporting polish: added `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:summary`, backed by `scripts/summarize-vanilla-compat-results.mjs`, to read `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat-results.jsonl` without launching Minecraft and report per-campaign final status by version. The helper supports `--json` for machine-readable handoff/audit output and treats later retry rows as the final status for a campaign/version pair. Verified with `node --check scripts/summarize-vanilla-compat-results.mjs`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:summary`, and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:summary -- --json` while the live snapshot runners continued.

Latest vanilla compatibility summary improvement: the read-only summary helper now also reports the active version, node PID, expected selected-version count, and remaining versions for each running campaign by combining live `*.lock/owner.json` files, current `run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` node command lines, and campaign offset/limit labels, including active campaigns that have not written their first JSONL result yet. This removes the need for separate PowerShell process probes during long sweeps. Verified with `node --check scripts/summarize-vanilla-compat-results.mjs`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:summary`, and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:summary -- --json`.

Latest vanilla snapshot lane completion: the delegated snapshot `603-735` lane passed all 133 selected versions with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 603 --limit 133 --quiet`, covering `15w34a` through `1.3`. The worker reported no remaining failed versions, no retained failure roots, no assigned roots/locks left under `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat`, and no leftover owned Java/javaw/theboyslauncher/social-backend processes after cleanup. A replacement verification worker is now running the previously uncovered snapshot `0-34` lane, while active lanes continue for `75-206`, `207-230`, `231-338`, `339-470`, and `501-520`.

Latest vanilla compatibility duplicate-lane cleanup: the replacement `207-230` worker cleared the prior `23w04a` first-pass failure, but a queued duplicate full-range `207-230` runner later overlapped with the trimmed `209-230` tail worker. The duplicate PID was stopped, stale `23w04a` root/lock plus the stale `all-snapshot-offset-207-limit-24` campaign lock were removed after verifying their owner PID was no longer a live campaign runner, and the `209-230` worker continued as the only active tail lane. The summary helper now ignores active lock rows unless the recorded PID still maps to a live `run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` node command. Verified with `node --check scripts/summarize-vanilla-compat-results.mjs` and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:summary`.

Latest production-readiness triage and setup-wording polish: rechecked Mojang's current manifest and it still matches the completed vanilla sweep totals (`736` snapshots, `102` releases, `26` old beta, `35` old alpha), so there are currently zero vanilla compatibility offsets left to delegate. Three read-only subagent audits identified the next high-leverage work: make CurseForge archive installs handle exports without direct `downloadUrl`, add real Modrinth `.mrpack` support instead of only provider copy, disable/retire legacy Go/Fyne release workflows, tighten website/Caddy/backend deploy routing, and improve first-run onboarding plus Activity process summaries. This slice removed more normal-flow repair wording at the source: Tauri `repair_profile` now emits `Profile setup completed.` / `Profile setup failed`, automatic missing-artifact setup completion uses setup wording, repair-needed pack install plans say `Set up queued...`, bottom-left/Activity retry icons now read as setup/play/retry actions instead of wrench repair affordances, and account removal buttons now say `Sign out` / `Confirm sign out` consistently for sighted and accessibility users. The archive installer now rejects `.mrpack` URLs before download with a clear `Modrinth .mrpack archives are planned... CurseForge zip exports` message instead of accepting the file name and later parsing it as CurseForge. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "home repair action shows pending native repair state|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account with confirmation"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_pack_plan_uses_setup_label_for_repair_needed_profiles -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher modrinth_archive_urls_fail_before_download_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher native_operation -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace`.

Latest CurseForge archive compatibility: CurseForge zip archive planning no longer requires every required file entry to include a direct `downloadUrl`. Manifest entries with only `projectID`/`fileID` now use CurseForge's public `/api/v1/mods/{projectId}/files/{fileId}/download` redirect endpoint; entries with `fileName` keep that jar filename, and entries without either a direct URL or filename fall back to a deterministic `curseforge-{projectId}-{fileId}.jar` destination under the profile's `mods` directory. Empty `downloadUrl` strings are treated as missing so exported manifests can still install. Direct `downloadUrl` support remains unchanged. A live check against `https://www.curseforge.com/api/v1/mods/306770/files/4031402/download` returned `307` to ForgeCDN. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_archive -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace`, `npm run build`, and the live endpoint redirect check.

Latest release workflow safety cleanup: the legacy Go/Fyne `Stable Release` and `Dev Prerelease` workflows have been replaced with tiny manual-dispatch stubs that fail immediately and point maintainers to `.github/workflows/v4-release-channels.yml` with `channel=stable` or `channel=dev`. They now use read-only contents permissions and no longer contain Go setup/build, `version.env` mutation, Inno Setup, or legacy `actions/create-release@v1` publishing steps, so an accidental click cannot publish non-v4 assets. Verified with `npx prettier --check .github/workflows/stable-release.yml .github/workflows/dev-prerelease.yml .github/workflows/v4-release-channels.yml` and `rg` checks for removed legacy release commands.

Latest Activity process polish: Activity > Processes no longer shows the full Java executable path in the normal process row. The row now shows a friendly compact summary (`pid ... - ... launch args`), timing, and output summary, while executable path, working directory, and environment count live behind `View technical details`; `View output` remains dedicated to stdout/stderr. This keeps long managed Java paths from cluttering normal launcher use while preserving diagnostics when needed. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity process actions stay visible with long Java paths"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-creation setup clarity: the Library new-profile primary action now says `Create and set up` instead of plain `Create`, matching the native flow that immediately calls `prepare_profile` after profile creation. The action shows `Setting up...` while that new profile is in setup and remains disabled during setup, and the native/web-preview/profile-version-failure Playwright coverage now targets the clearer label. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|profile creator blocks|library new profile action"` and `npm run build`.

Latest first-run empty-state polish: Home no longer shows disabled `Choose`/`Details` actions when the launcher has no packs or profiles. The empty Home hero now offers `Create profile` and `Discover packs`, and Library distinguishes a truly empty launcher (`No profiles yet`) from a filtered search miss (`No profiles found`) with a direct `Create and set up profile` action. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --grep "empty launcher guides"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Modrinth archive support: `install_modpack_archive` now accepts `.mrpack` downloads instead of rejecting them up front. The launcher-core parses standard top-level `modrinth.index.json`, derives Minecraft and loader versions from dependencies, plans required client file downloads to their declared profile-relative paths with Modrinth hashes/sizes, copies `overrides/` and `client-overrides/`, skips server-only and optional files until an option-selection UI exists, stores the original index under `.theboys/modrinth/`, then reuses the same vanilla-artifact, modloader-artifact/processor, parallel pack-file download, profile persistence, Library refresh, and Activity event flow used by CurseForge archives. Discover copy now advertises CurseForge zip and Modrinth `.mrpack` archive imports while keeping searchable provider catalogs as future work. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core modrinth_archive -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_archive -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher modrinth_archive_urls_build_download_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-modrinth-check'; cargo check --workspace`.

Latest live Modrinth archive validation: added `npm run smoke:live:modrinth:install`, which wraps an ignored launcher-core live smoke for a small public Fabric `.mrpack` (`[Fabric]Haste-1.21.1`). The smoke downloads the real Modrinth archive, validates `modrinth.index.json`, extracts overrides, installs required client pack files, prepares vanilla 1.21.1 artifacts, installs the exact Fabric loader declared by the archive, ensures the managed Java runtime is available, persists the profile, and proves launch preflight builds a Fabric Knot command from the installed artifacts. Verified fresh against isolated `target/live-smoke/modrinth-mrpack` with `npm run smoke:live:modrinth:install -- --fresh`; also reran `cargo test -p launcher-core modrinth_archive -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest setup wording source cleanup: native lifecycle-gate descriptions and the launcher-core direct setup receipt no longer say `repairing profile` / `Profile repair planned and queued`; they now say `setting up profile files...` and `Profile setup planned and queued.` so concurrent-operation errors or receipts cannot leak repair wording into normal UI surfaces. Legacy renderer sanitizers still translate older cached repair messages. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher lifecycle_operation_gate -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core native_actions_validate_required_subjects -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core repair_profile_rejects_unknown_profiles -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged-app verification refresh: current release-mode `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` was rebuilt with `npx tauri build --no-bundle` after the Modrinth archive and setup-wording changes. Release/security/package checks still pass with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:package-resources`, and `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`. A fresh packaged smoke launched the rebuilt exe against isolated `target/packaged-goal-smoke-current-clean` with hosted backend URL override, opened a `TheBoysLauncher` window, created `data/`, `config/`, `cache/`, `logs/`, `config/profiles.json`, and `config/settings.json`, then closed cleanly with no remaining `theboyslauncher` process.

Latest Activity file-progress polish: the Activity overview file summary now chooses the current file from the latest per-file state, so a file that has already emitted a later finished/already-present event is not kept as `Current`. The summary wording now says `downloading` instead of `started`, and the preview regression expects the pending mod to become current after the previous client download finished. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback"` and `npm run build`.

Latest process-stop status polish: rechecked the documented vanilla compatibility campaign and Mojang manifest totals still show zero remaining offsets to delegate (`736` snapshots, `102` releases, `26` old beta, `35` old alpha all covered after retries). Instead of spawning duplicate version workers, this slice removed another normal-flow process leak: stopping a native game now reports friendly profile text such as `WinterPack stopped`, and pending stop operation events are sanitized to `Stop requested` instead of showing pid/executable details like `javaw.exe` in the bottom-left status or Activity overview. Raw process output remains under Activity > Processes details. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "stopping a native process"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-edit setup automation: native profile saves now automatically run `prepare_profile` when the saved profile's Minecraft version or loader changes, including canonical values returned by the Rust command. The Library row uses the same setup busy state as profile creation, refreshes the bootstrap snapshot and launcher events after setup, and falls back to `updated; setup will retry on Play` if setup fails, so changing a custom profile version no longer leaves the next Play click to discover missing artifacts. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|library profile editor syncs native refreshed|library profile editor can choose snapshot"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest process-log status polish: saving a managed-process log no longer pushes the full filesystem export path into the bottom-left launcher status. The global status now says `Process log saved (...)`, while the Activity log-export receipt still shows the exact path plus `Open logs` for users who intentionally need it. Existing local MSI/NSIS/updater artifacts remain valid after the current checks. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "process log export"`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and `npm run build`.

Latest launch-preflight wording polish: native launch/Launch details failures caused by missing asset indexes, natives directories, or aggregate launch artifacts now surface as `Game files are missing. The launcher will set them up automatically.` in the bottom-left status instead of exposing cache paths such as `assets/indexes/...json`. The setup recovery detection still recognizes the sanitized wording, and Activity failed setup events can still show precise setup failure details. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "successful pack update clears stale launch repair recovery|launch command preflight failure offers setup recovery|successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery|library delete action clears stale launch recovery|failed native repair surfaces"`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher missing_launch_artifact_errors_trigger_automatic_repair -- --test-threads=1`, and `npm run build`.

Latest sidebar download-progress polish: the bottom-left live install/download status now summarizes file work by friendly category (`Downloading Minecraft assets`, `Downloading mod loader files`, `Waiting to download pack files`, etc.) instead of showing raw per-file labels like `asset-object-minecraft/sounds/...` or `File pending: forge-bootstrap`. Activity details and file-progress rows still keep the exact file/event text for troubleshooting. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface|pending native install polls launcher events"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity file-progress summary polish: Activity Overview's compact `Latest file progress` line now uses friendly file categories such as `pack files` instead of exposing the current raw pack/mod/asset filename. The collapsed `View details` section still contains exact download event names and percentages for troubleshooting. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|live file download events surface|pending native install polls launcher events"` and `npm run build`.

Latest native event-push status polish: native `launcher-event` pushes now route their fallback bottom-left activity text through the same user-facing formatter used by the sidebar instead of writing raw event messages directly. This keeps pushed setup/download/process events consistent with the newer friendly status wording while Activity details still retain exact event rows. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface|terminal repair event clears stuck native repair busy state|stopping a native process keeps playing presence"` and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged executable verification refresh: rechecked the frontend for normal user-facing `Repair` / `Check files` wording; remaining repair/check hits are internal command names, state keys, legacy sanitizers, tests, or friendly setup labels. Current release/security/package verification passes with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, `npm run build`, `npm run verify:package-resources`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace`. Rebuilt the local release executable with `npx tauri build --no-bundle`, producing `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` (`21,356,032` bytes, modified `2026-06-29T18:22:08-04:00`) with the current friendly status/download UI, and confirmed no `theboyslauncher`, `social-backend`, `java`, or `javaw` processes were left running afterward.

Latest Discover Modrinth slice: the Discover tab now has a real Modrinth browse path instead of only provider-plan copy. Launcher-core can search Modrinth's public modpack catalog through `/v2/search` with `project_type:modpack`, map search hits into typed launcher results, resolve the newest downloadable `.mrpack` from a project's version list, and reject projects with no HTTPS `.mrpack` archive. Tauri exposes `search_modrinth_modpacks` and `resolve_modrinth_modpack_archive`; the frontend shows a compact Modrinth search panel with result rows, loader/version/download summaries, and an Install action that resolves the archive and reuses the existing `install_modpack_archive` pipeline. Browser-preview Playwright gets deterministic preview rows, while native desktop uses the live Modrinth API. Verified the live Modrinth search/version endpoint shape with small `Invoke-RestMethod` checks, then ran `cargo test -p launcher-core modrinth_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace`.

Latest fast Modrinth Discover smoke: added `npm run smoke:live:modrinth:discover`, a quick live metadata smoke that searches Modrinth for public modpacks, selects Fabulously Optimized when present, resolves the project version list to an HTTPS `.mrpack` archive, and validates archive filename/size without downloading the pack. This gives the new Discover browse/install handoff a low-cost live check distinct from the heavier full `.mrpack` install smoke. Verified with `node --check scripts/run-live-winterpack-smoke.mjs`, `npm run smoke:live:modrinth:discover`, `cargo test -p launcher-core modrinth_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged executable refresh after Discover work: reran release-side verification after the Modrinth Discover/search additions. `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:package-resources`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace` all passed. Rebuilt the local release executable with `npx tauri build --no-bundle`; the current `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` is `21,535,232` bytes and was modified `2026-06-29T18:39:48-04:00`. No `theboyslauncher`, `social-backend`, `java`, or `javaw` processes were left running afterward.

Latest Activity cached setup-failure polish: the common launcher-event formatter now sanitizes cached/native launch-artifact setup failures, `Automatic profile repair before launch failed`, and `Profile setup failed` messages instead of leaving that cleanup only in direct native-error handling. Install or repair the profile before launching.` text after loading older event-log entries; they now show the same friendly `Game files are missing. The launcher will set them up automatically.` wording as the sidebar. Latest packaged executable refresh after cached setup-failure polish: refreshed checks and rebuilt the local release executable after the Activity cached-event sanitization. `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:package-resources`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace` passed, then `npx tauri build --no-bundle` rebuilt `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` at `21,535,232` bytes with modified time `2026-06-29T18:46:04-04:00`. Latest Activity refresh status polish: Activity's combined Refresh action now refreshes managed processes without overwriting a launcher-event refresh failure with a lower-priority success message like `Loaded 0 processes`. Process refresh errors still surface normally; only the success activity line is suppressed during the combined refresh. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native event log refresh failure preserves real activity state|activity event log sanitizes cached launch setup failures"` and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged executable refresh after Activity refresh status polish: refreshed the release-side checks after the combined Activity Refresh status fix. `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:package-resources`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-goal-check'; cargo check --workspace` passed, then `npx tauri build --no-bundle` rebuilt `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` at `21,535,232` bytes with modified time `2026-06-29T18:51:28.4998377-04:00`. No `theboyslauncher`, `social-backend`, `java`, or `javaw` processes were left running afterward.

Latest vanilla snapshot parallel-smoke checkpoint: the next snapshot chunk passed all 5 selected versions at `--offset 70` using the runner's parallel mode: `1.21.6-pre4`, `1.21.6-pre3`, `1.21.6-pre2`, `1.21.6-pre1`, and `25w21a`. Verified with `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 70 --limit 5 --jobs 5 --quiet`, which ran five isolated version roots concurrently and cleaned successful roots. Current Mojang manifest counts are 899 total versions: 102 `release`, 736 `snapshot`, 26 `old_beta`, and 35 `old_alpha`; release, old beta, and old alpha are complete, and snapshots are now documented through offset 74 except for the already-investigated deterministic `1.21.11-pre1` client crash. Five verification agents were launched for the remaining snapshot ranges without editing docs or committing: offsets `75..206`, `207..338`, `339..470`, `471..602`, and `603..735`, each running the serial harness for its assigned range so total Minecraft process concurrency stays near five.

Latest vanilla campaign-runner hardening: `scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` now creates a per-campaign lock under `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat-campaign-locks` for non-dry-run executions, keyed by mode/type/offset/limit or explicit versions. This prevents accidentally starting the exact same large offset range twice while still allowing different offset chunks to run in parallel. Stale campaign locks are removed only when the recorded owner PID is no longer running, mirroring the existing per-version lock behavior. Verified with `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs`, `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 75 --limit 2 --dry-run`, and a fake active-owner lock for `all-snapshot-offset-75-limit-1`, which made the runner fail before any Minecraft download or launch with `Vanilla compatibility campaign is already running`.

Latest Activity progress polish: Activity overview operation rows now render active operations without a numeric `progressPercent` as an indeterminate `Working` state with a small pulsing progress fill instead of a dead-looking `--` label and 0-width bar. Known numeric progress still renders the exact percentage and ARIA value. Verified with `npm run build` and `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity active operation without percent uses working progress"`.

Latest vanilla campaign result logging: `scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` now appends each completed live version result to `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat-results.jsonl` immediately after the version smoke exits, including campaign label, selected type/offset/limit, version id, pass/fail status, exit code, and signal. This makes long snapshot chunks easier to resume or audit if a shell/agent dies late in a large range. Verified without adding more Minecraft load using `node --check scripts/run-vanilla-compat-campaign.mjs` and `npm run smoke:live:vanilla:compat -- --all --type snapshot --offset 75 --limit 2 --dry-run`; the active five-agent snapshot sweep was left running.

Latest profile-create polish: the Library `New profile` flow now defaults the profile name to the selected latest release, such as `Minecraft 1.21.8`, instead of a generic `Custom Profile N`, while keeping the existing version dropdown and automatic setup-after-create behavior. Duplicate default names are made unique with a numeric suffix. Verified with `npm run build` and `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "empty launcher guides first profile setup"`.

Latest current-session launcher checkpoint: after the PC rebuild, the branch was clean/synced and the toolchain was available (`node`, `npm`, `rustc`, `cargo`; Docker remains WSL-side rather than Windows PATH). The Import screen now has a custom folder path fallback for profiles stored outside the standard scanner roots, and Playwright covers the native `plan_profile_import` request plus review modal. Discover/CurseForge confidence was tightened: `npm run smoke:live:curseforge:plan` now downloads the live Enigmatica 9 Expert CurseForge ZIP, validates Forge `43.4.23` / Minecraft `1.19.2`, confirms all 232 required mod downloads use direct ForgeCDN URLs, and extracts overrides without downloading every mod. Discover archive installs also have a native-stub Playwright regression proving a successful `install_modpack_archive` refreshes Library with the installed profile. Verified this slice with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen"`, `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover"`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_archive -- --test-threads=1`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-curseforge-check'; cargo check -p launcher-core`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-curseforge-smoke'; npm run smoke:live:curseforge:plan -- --fresh`. Vanilla compatibility lanes are still running under `target/live-smoke/vanilla-compat-campaign-locks`; exact retry evidence has already cleared `24w20a`, while `1.14.4-pre5`, `1.14.4-pre6`, and `26.1-pre-2` are still pending retry/summary reconciliation.

Latest launcher-focus pivot: broad vanilla compatibility campaigns were intentionally stopped and their stale campaign locks removed so work can return to finishing the actual launcher UX and happy paths. The active goal now excludes broad all-version sweeps unless explicitly resumed. Eight campaign runners were stopped (`snapshot` offsets `6`, `124`, `211`, `256`, `375`, plus `release 0..33`, `old_beta`, and `old_alpha`), and the prior subagent was closed. Current launcher polish in this slice: the sidebar diagnostic recovery action says `Set up files` instead of `Set up again`, and failed setup Activity rows now expose `Play`, routing through the normal setup-then-launch path instead of asking users to think about repair/setup. Discover now shows only actionable providers (`CurseForge` archive install and `Modrinth` search/archive install) in the main provider grid; ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic are moved into a collapsed `Coming later` list so the screen reads less like unfinished placeholder work. Hosted social backend verification found the published `v4.0.0` backend image rejects `tauri://localhost` CORS origins; the running local hosted backend was restored on `theboyslauncher/social-backend:local`, which includes the newer CORS validation, and `npm run social:check` now passes for local/public health, `/packs`, and preflight/read checks from `https://launcher.dylan.lol`, `tauri://localhost`, `http://tauri.localhost`, and `https://tauri.localhost`. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover|successful pack update clears stale launch repair recovery|launch command preflight failure offers setup recovery|successful launch command preview clears stale repair recovery|failed native repair surfaces|library delete action clears stale launch recovery"`, `npm run build`, and `npm run social:check`.

Latest profile safety polish: changing the Minecraft version in either the New profile form or the Library profile customizer now automatically resets an unsupported manual Forge/NeoForge loader choice back to Vanilla. This avoids leaving new users with a hidden advanced selection that blocks Create/Save after they change versions. Fabric, Quilt, and already-compatible Forge/NeoForge choices are preserved. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library new profile action falls back|creating a profile refreshes|library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor can choose snapshot"` and `npm run build`.

Latest first-run import polish: Home now shows an `Import profiles` action whenever the user has no local profiles, even if the hosted pack catalog has already loaded a featured pack. The empty Library state also exposes `Import profiles` below the primary create-profile action. This keeps migration visible for first-run users without removing the Import tab yet. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "empty launcher guides|discover shows provider plan|library new profile action falls back|library profile editor saves preview changes"` and `npm run build`.

Latest packaged-exe smoke refresh: release/channel checks passed with `npm run verify:tauri-security`, `npm run verify:package-resources`, `npm run verify:tauri-bundles`, and `node scripts/resolve-v4-release-version.mjs dev/stable` (`dev` resolved to `4.0.2-1` / `v4.0.2-dev.1`, `stable` to `4.0.2` / `v4.0.2`). Rebuilt the current release executable with `npx tauri build --no-bundle`; `target/release/theboyslauncher.exe` is now `21,639,680` bytes, modified `2026-06-30T12:37:22-04:00`. Packaged smoke launched that exe with `THEBOYS_LAUNCHER_ROOT_DIR=target/packaged-smoke/20260630-123805` and hosted backend override, observed a `TheBoysLauncher` window, created isolated `data/`, `config/`, `cache/`, `logs/`, `config/profiles.json`, and `config/settings.json`, then closed cleanly with no remaining `theboyslauncher`, `java`, `javaw`, or smoke-root process. `npm run social:check` still passes after the smoke.

Latest Settings cleanup: the Settings screen now keeps backend/service implementation details out of the main visible cards. The normal Launcher card shows friendly `Connection` and `Mode` status with exact service addresses hidden behind `Connection details`; the Advanced toggle now reads `Advanced settings`, and its action groups use player-facing labels (`Find Java`, `Recommendations`, `Check friends`, `Start local service`, `Stop local service`) while keeping the same native Java/backend/session commands wired. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|settings backend|settings Java|settings managed Java|native startup prefers configured hosted"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity wording cleanup: the Activity surface now uses player-facing labels instead of developer console wording. The running-game view is labeled `Games` instead of `Processes`, the event-log view is labeled `History` instead of `Events`, the sidebar quick-status strip also says `Games`, the header summary says `games` / `updates`, the activity toolbar is exposed as `Activity controls`, and event lists are labeled `Recent activity` / `Activity history`. Internal state names and native process/event commands are unchanged. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads|activity process|activity active|activity event|activity repair|library play action surfaces|successful pack update|home repair action|failed native repair|pending native install|live file download|managed Java install runs|stopping a native process|clear exited|process log"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Games/log wording cleanup: the Activity `Games` view now hides process IDs from the main rows and moves them into `View technical details`, says `launch settings` instead of `launch args`, shows `Clear finished` instead of `Clear exited`, uses `Auto refresh` instead of `Live`, and labels saved logs as `Game log saved` under `Last game log export`. Native process-log export/reveal errors are sanitized to `Game log export failed` / `Opening game log folder failed` while preserving the underlying native command behavior. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity process actions|activity screen loads|native process log|library process log|native process log reveal|clear exited|stopping a native process|process row labels|process log export"` and `npm run build`.

Latest sidebar game-status wording cleanup: the sidebar quick-status strip now says `No games running`, `1 game running`, or `<n> games running` instead of `active process`, and managed-process operation summaries display as `Game status` while keeping the same internal process registry and event types. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "empty launcher guides|activity screen loads|activity process actions|clear exited process|stopping a native process|library play action surfaces native launch preflight"` and `npm run build`.

Latest friends-service wording cleanup: Settings/status copy now says `friends service` / `Hosted service` / `Local service` instead of exposing `social backend` / `backend` terminology, including native-error sanitization for raw backend messages. The online pack refresh Playwright expectation now also guards the intended merge behavior: hosted catalog packs are added while existing local packs such as WinterPack remain visible instead of disappearing when omitted from the online response. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings.*service|native startup prefers configured hosted|home screen can refresh pack metadata|home screen replaces preview friends|launching a profile can publish local presence|friends screen can send requests"` and `npm run build`.

Latest launcher-completion checkpoint: broad Minecraft version compatibility testing remains paused by request so the next work can focus on finishing the launcher product itself. A process check found no active repo-local vanilla campaign, Java, Minecraft, Vite, Playwright, Cargo, or Tauri runner to stop; only Codex helper processes, Windows WebView, unrelated Node jobs in another project, and the WSL Docker watchdog were visible. Discover archive installs now validate on the client before invoking native install: users must provide a pack name plus a direct `https://` URL ending in `.zip` or `.mrpack`, with friendly copy for missing names, missing links, non-HTTPS links, malformed URLs, and non-archive links. The install button is disabled while the archive form is invalid, and the valid native path still submits the trimmed name/URL to `install_modpack_archive` and refreshes Library. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover"` and `npm run build`.

Latest game-status wording cleanup: Activity/process registry user-facing copy now uses `game` / `game status` language instead of exposing process-registry terminology. Loading/stopping/clearing states now say `Loading games`, `Stopping game`, `Clearing finished games`, `Game status is mocked in web preview`, and sanitized native errors convert `Process stop failed`, `Clearing exited processes failed`, and `process registry` wording into player-facing equivalents. Empty output now says `Waiting for game output`, while detailed Activity controls still keep native process commands and IDs behind technical/detail UI. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover|activity process actions|native process log|clear exited|stopping a native process|process row labels|process log export"` and `npm run build`.

Latest import UX cleanup: the Import screen keeps the existing tab and native scan/plan/execute wiring, but the visible flow now uses beginner-friendly copy. The main action is `Find profiles`, scan status says `Looking for profiles` / `Found N profiles to import`, custom-folder review is disabled until a folder path is provided, and the empty state points users to either automatic discovery or pasting a folder path. Import review copy now says files will be copied and the original profile will not be changed, conflict rows say `Matching files already exist` / `Matching files will rename|skip|overwrite` instead of developer-ish conflict wording, and the final modal action now says `Bring over` / `Bringing over...` to match the flow.
