Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen|native import"` and `npm run build`. Rendered QA through the in-app browser at `http://127.0.0.1:1420/` confirmed the Import page loads, custom-folder review starts disabled with the path hint, entering a folder path enables `Review folder`, the review modal opens with `The original profile will not be changed`, `Bring over` is enabled, and console warning/error logs are empty; the temporary Vite server was stopped afterward.

Latest profile customization cleanup: the Library profile customizer now exposes practical per-profile options without making users open Advanced. Name, version type, version, memory, window size, default server name/address, and server port are visible in the normal customize panel; loader, JVM args, and Java override remain hidden behind Advanced so Java/JVM controls stay out of normal screens. The profile editor grid now uses three stable columns so it fits beside the action column at desktop widths without clipping server fields. Verified with `npx playwright test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves|library profile editor can choose snapshot|library profile editor syncs|library profile editor keeps|library new profile action falls back"` and `npm run build`. Rendered QA through the in-app browser confirmed memory/window/server are visible immediately, JVM args and Java override are absent until Advanced is opened, the server address field stays inside the editor with no horizontal document overflow, and console warning/error logs are empty; screenshot saved at `C:\Users\server\AppData\Local\Temp\theboyslauncher-profile-editor-qa-fixed.png`, and the temporary Vite server was stopped afterward.

Latest account/channel polish checkpoint: broad version compatibility testing remains stopped; the known testing subagent `019f18e7-2ea2-7bb2-bb25-9e3862aadf74` was closed while still `pending_init`, and a process sweep found no Java, Minecraft, Tauri, Vite, Cargo, Playwright, or repo-local version campaign running. The Settings account card and account manager now use clearer multiple-account language: accounts are `Selected` or can be `Switch`ed, removal is labeled `Remove` / `Remove account`, and copy explains that the selected account is used for Minecraft launches. The update channel card now explains Stable vs Dev in player terms, shows `Installed now` / `Checking`, and cross-channel install actions read `Get Dev build` / `Get Stable build` instead of exposing installer mechanics as the primary label. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings exposes stable and dev|settings dev channel check|settings lists stored Minecraft accounts|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account|settings native account actions|settings native mode keeps"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Pack Details menu cleanup: the Pack Details `More` menu is no longer a placeholder that only closes the panel. It now exposes wired actions that use existing launcher behavior: `Show in Library`, the current pack action (`Play`, `Update`, `Install`, or setup/busy state), and a guarded `Delete profile` confirmation when the pack has a local profile. The delete path reuses the normal native `delete_profile` flow, refreshes the bootstrap snapshot and Activity events, and keeps unsupported actions such as Open folder/Duplicate hidden until real native commands exist. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "home details action|pack details more menu"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile folder command: profile folders can now be opened from real launcher UI instead of being deferred as a placeholder action. Tauri exposes `open_profile_folder`, which requires an existing saved profile, creates/resolves `data/profiles/<profile-id>`, canonicalizes it under the launcher-managed profiles root, rejects missing or unsafe ids, and then opens it through the OS file manager. Pack Details `More` now shows `Open folder` for local profiles, and Library profile rows show a `Folder` action beside `Launch details`; web preview gets a friendly mocked fallback. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher open_profile_folder -- --test-threads=1`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-open-folder-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "home details action|pack details more menu"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest launcher-focus continuation: version compatibility testing remains paused by request, and the known testing subagent `019f18e7-2ea2-7bb2-bb25-9e3862aadf74` was closed while still `pending_init`. Profile duplication is now a real launcher action instead of a deferred menu idea. Shared/Tauri expose `duplicate_profile`, launcher-core clones a saved profile into a unique `Copy` name/id, safely copies only the managed `data/profiles/<source-id>` directory, rejects unsafe ids and symlinks/special files, clears `installedPackVersion` plus `lastPlayed` so the copy is independent from curated catalog update semantics, and cleans up copied files if profile persistence fails. Library rows now show `Duplicate`, and Pack Details `More` shows `Duplicate profile` for installed profiles; both refresh the native bootstrap snapshot after success and web preview gets a mocked copy. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core duplicate_profile -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "duplicate|home details action|pack details more menu"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-duplicate-tauri-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover archive polish: the Discover archive installer no longer forces users to manually name a pack when the URL already has a usable `.zip` or `.mrpack` filename. The form now infers a friendly name from the archive filename, shows `Will install as ...` when the name field is blank, detects and displays generic `Pack .zip` or `Modrinth .mrpack` link type, keeps HTTPS/direct-extension validation, and submits the inferred name to the existing native `install_modpack_archive` command. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Settings save polish: Settings now has a persistent save-status strip below the main settings cards so changes outside the Memory card, such as Privacy diagnostics or Advanced settings, no longer depend on users noticing the Memory-card `Save changes` button. The strip shows `Settings saved` when the draft matches persisted settings, switches to `Unsaved settings` with validation guidance when preferences change, and exposes a dedicated `Save settings` action wired to the existing native `save_settings` command. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|settings save action|settings save status|settings native save|settings save validates"` and `npm run build`.

Latest launcher-cleanup pivot checkpoint: broad Minecraft version compatibility work remains paused, no new version-test agents were spawned, the previously documented version-test subagent id is no longer available, and a process sweep found no active Java, Minecraft, Tauri, Vite, Playwright, Cargo, or repo-local vanilla campaign process to stop. The active work is now product cleanup and launcher completion. This slice removed more user-facing process/backend wording from normal screens: Library running-profile warnings now say `This game is running`, Library and Activity action groups are exposed as game actions, Activity's copy action says `Copy log text`, and Advanced diagnostics says `Service status` / `Local service is running` instead of `Service process` / `Managed process`. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity game actions|library delete action is disabled|activity screen loads event log fallback|library process log export|exited native process row"` and `npm run build`.

Latest setup-check wording cleanup: the Library profile action that previously read `Launch details` now reads `Check setup`, so normal users see it as a safe setup/preflight check instead of a command-inspection tool. The underlying native launch-command/preflight path is unchanged; the preview panel is now labeled `Setup details`, copy/close controls say setup details, and related status copy says `Checking setup` / `Setup details ready`. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "setup check|launch command preflight|successful launch command preview|Java preflight|authenticated launch command|profile editor saves preview|stored session switches"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover first-run cleanup: the Discover archive installer no longer opens with a hardcoded Enigmatica URL/name prefilled. The form now starts blank with validation text asking users to paste a CurseForge `.zip` or Modrinth `.mrpack` link, while still inferring a friendly pack name and archive type once a URL is entered. This keeps production Discover from looking tied to one test pack, while preserving the native `install_modpack_archive` path and Modrinth browse/search flow. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity copy-label polish: the Activity Games view already exposes `Copy log text`; the confirmation state now matches that wording with `Copied log text for <profile>` instead of `Copied output...`, keeping the action/result language consistent for normal users while leaving the copied stdout/stderr text unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback"` and `npm run build`.

Latest native running-game error cleanup: frontend native-error sanitization now catches saved-profile guard failures that still mention a `running managed process` or PID from Rust diagnostics, and surfaces them as `This game is running...` messages for save/archive/delete/duplicate/update/setup races. The underlying native guards are unchanged; this only prevents process wording from leaking into normal UI. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native failure|archive action keeps"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity status-label cleanup: Activity overview/details/history rows now display friendly event states (`Queued`, `Working`, `Done`, `Needs attention`) instead of raw launcher-event kind values such as `completed`, `downloading`, or `failed`. Operation grouping, progress math, and native event data are unchanged; only the visible status labels were softened for normal users. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads|activity overview|activity event log|failed native pack install|pending native install"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-creator fallback polish: the desktop New Profile flow no longer blocks profile creation when the live Mojang version manifest fails to load. Native failures now keep a friendly warning visible but fall back to the bundled common release list, defaulting to `1.21.8`, so users can still create and set up a vanilla profile instead of being stuck on a disabled version dropdown. The native `create_profile` and `prepare_profile` path is unchanged once the fallback selection is submitted. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "profile creator falls back|creating a profile refreshes|library new profile action falls back"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-creator recovery-copy polish: when the live Minecraft version list is unavailable, the sidebar now says `Live Minecraft versions unavailable; showing common releases` instead of echoing the raw native manifest error, matching the recovered fallback behavior in the New Profile form. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "profile creator falls back"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover copy cleanup: the Discover provider cards and sidebar now describe the actually supported install paths instead of hinting at unfinished catalog work on the main surface. CurseForge is framed as direct zip export install, Modrinth is framed as live search or direct `.mrpack`, and deferred providers remain collapsed under `Coming later`. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover archive form|discover archive install"` and `npm run build`.

Latest update-channel installer hardening: the native `open_external_url` bridge used for switching between Stable and Dev now validates that renderer-requested downloads are TheBoysLauncher GitHub release installer assets ending in `.exe` or `.msi`, rejecting release JSON and other origins before opening the OS handler. This keeps the cross-channel installer flow tight while preserving the existing signed-installer UX. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher external_download_url -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings exposes stable and dev|settings dev channel check"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest account-manager confirmation cleanup: switching accounts or successfully removing an account now clears any armed `Remove account` confirmation in the accounts modal, so a stale destructive confirmation does not remain visible after the selected account changes. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings lists stored Minecraft accounts|settings can remove a stored Minecraft account"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-editor validation polish: the Library profile customizer now explains disabled Save states for common beginner mistakes. Invalid memory values, incomplete/invalid window sizes, and out-of-range server ports show inline hints instead of silently disabling Save. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor keeps profile values"` and `npm run build`.

Latest custom import path cleanup: the Import custom-folder flow now accepts Windows folder paths pasted with surrounding quotes, trims the quotes before validation, and sends the clean path to the native `plan_profile_import` command. This keeps the beginner-friendly folder fallback from failing on common Explorer/File Properties paste behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen can review a custom folder path"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Library setup-check cleanup: normal Library profile rows no longer show the diagnostic `Check setup` action beside Play. The same native setup/preflight command remains available inside the profile customizer's collapsed Advanced section, so troubleshooting stays reachable without making everyday launching feel like users need to repair or inspect profiles. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "setup check|launch command|library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile rows no longer expose repair|library delete action clears stale launch repair recovery|stored session switches launch actions"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover Modrinth install coverage: the Modrinth search-result install path now has Playwright native-stub coverage proving the full renderer contract: search result selection calls `resolve_modrinth_modpack_archive`, sends the resolved `.mrpack` URL plus pack title to `install_modpack_archive`, refreshes the bootstrap snapshot/events, and lands the user in Library with the installed Fabric profile visible. This complements the direct archive URL regression for CurseForge/Modrinth links. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover Modrinth search install|discover archive install refreshes|discover shows provider plan|discover archive form"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Modrinth exact-version install polish: Discover search-result installs now pass the result's `latestVersionId` through the renderer/native bridge when resolving the `.mrpack`, so clicking a Modrinth result installs the exact advertised version instead of asking native to pick the first project archive generically. Tauri keeps the argument optional for backwards-compatible project-level resolution, and launcher-core now has a version-specific resolver that reuses the same HTTPS/archive filename validation. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core modrinth_archive_resolution -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover Modrinth search install"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-modrinth-version-tauri-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest sidebar archive-install progress cleanup: the sidebar/bottom-left progress logic now treats `install_modpack_archive` completion events as lifecycle completions, matching `install_pack` and setup completion. This prevents low-level download or modloader processor messages such as `Verified modloader installer processor 6/6 outputs` from staying visible after a direct CurseForge/Modrinth archive install reports success. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface"` and `npm run build`.

Latest friends-service native wording cleanup: renderer-facing native service status/errors now say `friends service`, `local service`, and `packaged service` instead of leaking `social backend`, `local backend`, or `packaged binary` wording into Settings diagnostics/status messages. Internal executable names, environment variables, and logs remain technical where appropriate. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p theboyslauncher backend_status -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "launcher status card fits|settings backend start waits|native startup prefers configured hosted|settings friends service action|settings native friends service"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest archive-install operation alignment: direct CurseForge/Modrinth archive installs now emit their native completion event as `install_modpack_archive` / `Install modpack` instead of piggybacking on `install_pack`, so Activity distinguishes direct archive installs from curated catalog pack installs while sidebar completion handling still clears stale download/processor progress. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher completed_modpack_archive_event_uses_archive_operation -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover archive install refreshes"`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-archive-operation-check2'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover placeholder cleanup: the Discover screen now only shows working provider paths in the normal UI: CurseForge direct zip archives and Modrinth search/direct `.mrpack` archives. ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic remain documented as deferred providers in code/docs, but the visible `Coming later` block was removed so the screen reads like a usable installer instead of a roadmap. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover archive form|discover archive install|discover Modrinth search install"` and `npm run build`.

Latest multi-provider Discover browse slice: Discover now has a provider-neutral search result DTO/command and live browse/search support for ATLauncher public packs and current FTB app packs alongside existing Modrinth search/install. React renders ATLauncher and FTB as first-class Browse providers with shared result rows and clear install notes; Modrinth remains install-ready through exact `.mrpack` resolution, and CurseForge remains direct zip archive install. Technic is still not enabled in the main provider grid because the public Technic API rejected unauthenticated requests from this environment, so it needs a supported API path or credential strategy before browse/install can honestly work. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_result -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-discover-providers-check2'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest FTB Discover install slice: current FTB app packs are now install-ready from Discover. Shared/Tauri expose `install_discover_modpack`; launcher-core fetches `api.modpacks.ch/public/modpack/{packId}/{versionId}`, derives a managed profile from Minecraft/modloader targets and recommended memory, filters required client files, validates HTTPS/safe profile-relative destinations/SHA-1 metadata, and feeds the resulting file plan through the existing vanilla/modloader/download/profile persistence pipeline. React now sends FTB search-result installs to the native provider installer and refreshes Library/Activity after success. ATLauncher remains live browse-only, and Technic remains disabled until a supported API path is available. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover FTB search install"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-ftb-install-check2'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest ATLauncher Discover install slice: public ATLauncher packs are now install-ready from Discover for standard manifests. The launcher fetches `https://download.nodecdn.net/containers/atl/packs/{safeName}/versions/{version}/Configs.json`, derives the profile Minecraft/modloader target, maps required client-capable files into standard profile folders such as `mods`, `config`, `resourcepacks`, `texturepacks`, `shaderpacks`, and `saves`, validates HTTPS/safe paths/MD5 metadata, and runs the files through the same vanilla/modloader/download/profile persistence pipeline used by FTB. Unsupported required ATLauncher file types such as `extract`, `root`, `jar`, and browser-only downloads intentionally fail with a clear message rather than creating a broken profile. React now sends ATLauncher search-result installs to `install_discover_modpack` and refreshes Library after success. Technic remains disabled until a supported API path is available. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-atlauncher-install-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover FTB search install|discover ATLauncher search install"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest FTB Legacy Discover install slice: FTB Legacy moved from browse-only to conservative install-ready support for common legacy zip packs. Launcher-core still parses the public and third-party XML feeds with `quick-xml`, and now builds a legacy archive download plan from `https://dist.creeper.host/FTB2/modpacks/{dir}/{version_with_underscores}/{file}`. The installer downloads the legacy zip, requires `minecraft/pack.json`, extracts the archive's `minecraft/` folder safely into the managed profile, derives Forge from `pack.json` Maven coordinates such as `net.minecraftforge:forge:1.12.2-14.23.5.2860`, then runs the existing vanilla/modloader/profile persistence pipeline through `install_discover_modpack`. Older jar-mod-only packs and unsafe archive paths fail with clear errors instead of producing broken profiles. React now marks FTB Legacy install-ready and routes its search-result Install button to the native provider installer; Playwright covers the command request and Library refresh. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_legacy -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover FTB Legacy search install"`, `npm run build`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-ftb-legacy-install-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Technic Discover browse slice: Technic is no longer hidden as a deferred provider. Prism's current request shape was rechecked, and the live Technic platform search/trending endpoints work with `build=multimc`; launcher-core now calls `https://api.technicpack.net/trending?build=multimc`, `.../search?build=multimc&q={query}`, and exact `#slug` metadata lookups, parsing both `modpacks[]` list responses and single-pack JSON into shared Discover results. React renders Technic as live browse/search with a clear note that installation still needs reliable Technic zip/Solder metadata and download handling before it can be automatic; installs remain disabled rather than creating broken profiles. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-technic-browse-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Technic Discover install slice: Technic direct-zip and Solder packs are now install-ready from Discover exact `#slug` metadata. Direct packs download the single HTTPS zip; Solder packs fetch the Solder pack/build metadata, download all module zips with MD5 validation, read `bin/version.json` or nested `bin/modpack.jar/version.json`, derive Forge/Fabric/Quilt loader metadata, safely extract profile-relative archive contents, then run the existing vanilla/modloader/profile persistence pipeline. React now marks Technic available and routes installable results through the native provider installer with Library refresh coverage. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Technic search install"`, `npm run build`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-technic-solder-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Library diagnostics wording cleanup: the hidden Advanced profile troubleshooting action no longer says `Check setup` or opens `Setup details`; it is now labeled `Diagnostics`, opens a `Launch diagnostics` panel, and uses diagnostics copy/status text. Normal Home/Library cards still avoid manual Repair actions and profile create/update continues to run automatic `prepare_profile` setup after saving. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library diagnostics|launch command preview|successful launch command preview|stored session switches launch actions"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Technic Discover UX cleanup: Technic search-list results that do not include install metadata no longer dead-end as a browse-only row. Clicking `Check details` now performs the exact `#slug` metadata lookup, replaces the row with the exact result, and continues into the normal native `install_discover_modpack` flow automatically when the exact result exposes direct-zip or Solder install support. This keeps the Prism-style `#slug` detail lookup as an implementation detail instead of something users must know. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover provider-copy consistency cleanup: the Discover sidebar provider summary now lists all currently working searchable catalogs (`Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, Technic`) instead of the older partial list, and provider selection status copy now says `Browse public <provider> modpacks` instead of implying every provider is only a search box. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity/sidebar file-progress wording cleanup: user-facing download-operation labels now say `Prepare files` / `Preparing files` instead of `Download files` / `Downloading files`, matching the real lifecycle where the launcher may download, verify, reuse cached files, copy pack files, or run modloader setup. The lower-level event parsing and technical detail rows are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|live file download events surface|pending native install polls launcher events"` and `npm run build`.

Latest CurseForge Discover catalog slice: CurseForge is now a real searchable Discover provider when `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` is configured in the launcher build or runtime environment. Launcher-core calls CurseForge Core API `/mods/search` for Minecraft modpacks, selects downloadable `.zip` pack exports, downloads the selected file into the staged modpack archive cache, then reuses the existing CurseForge zip manifest importer for overrides, mod files, vanilla files, modloader setup, and profile persistence. The Discover UI now shows CurseForge search like the other providers while keeping direct zip paste as the no-key fallback path. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover CurseForge search install"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-curseforge-discover-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest setup/Discover copy polish: packs whose files need setup now show the status `Ready to set up` instead of the vague `Getting ready`, while the Play action still performs automatic setup before launch. CurseForge Discover search also catches the missing `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` native error and shows a normal fallback message telling users to paste a CurseForge zip link instead of leaking build/environment-variable wording. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover CurseForge search explains|discover shows provider plan|pack play setup refreshes"` and `npm run build`.

Latest install-error wording cleanup: renderer-facing install/setup messages now sanitize generic modpack file errors such as `missing mod artifact`, `mod artifact`, and `pack artifact` into `missing mod file`, `mod file`, and `pack file`. This keeps native/backend terminology out of failed install banners and Activity rows while preserving the raw native test fixtures underneath. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "failed native pack install surfaces the failed launcher event"` and `npm run build`.

Latest launch-event wording cleanup: normal Activity/sidebar event rendering now says `Game started for ...` and `Game closed during startup...` instead of leaking native `Launch process started...` / `launch process exited during startup...` wording. Native launch diagnostics remain unchanged; this only affects renderer-facing event text. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library play action opens native process supervisor|activity event log sanitizes launch"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity event log sanitizes cached launch setup failures"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest multi-provider Discover verification: the Prism-style Discover provider work is present in the current v4 tree for Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic. This pass tightened the FTB Playwright selector so it no longer also matches `FTB Legacy`, then reran the full Discover provider/install E2E matrix: provider plan, Modrinth install, CurseForge install, FTB install, ATLauncher install, FTB Legacy install, Technic install, and Technic exact-detail install. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `npm run build`, `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-discover-provider-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest profile/import/discover loader-label cleanup: Library profile summaries, the new-profile draft summary, Discover result rows, installed Discover profile rows, and Import review summaries now display friendly loader names such as `Vanilla`, `Fabric`, `Forge`, `NeoForge`, and `Quilt` instead of raw internal ids like `vanilla`, `fabric`, `forge`, `neoforge`, or `quilt`. Stored profile values, provider payloads, import metadata, and native requests are unchanged; this is only user-facing text. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover archive install refreshes|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover FTB search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result|import screen plans|native import disables|library new profile action falls back|library profile editor saves preview changes|creating a profile refreshes"` and `npm run build`.

Latest friends-service error wording cleanup: renderer-facing native error sanitization now also rewrites raw `packaged backend executable`, `packaged backend`, `local backend`, and `hosted backend` phrases into friends-service language before showing status messages. The Settings local-service failure path now shows `packaged friends service is missing` instead of leaking `packaged backend executable is missing`, while internal command names and test fixtures remain unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings backend start waits|native startup prefers configured hosted|settings friends service action|settings native friends service"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Java recovery action wording cleanup: Java launch-recovery actions now say `Set up Java` in the sidebar recovery strip and Activity failed-launch rows instead of the terse `Java` button label. The deeper Advanced Java settings controls are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native Java runtime recovery|missing managed Java executable|launch command Java preflight"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Library row declutter: normal Library profile rows now keep file-management actions behind a `More` menu. `Open folder` and `Duplicate profile` remain wired to the existing native commands, but the everyday row no longer shows Folder/Duplicate beside Play/Customize, reducing visible clutter for newer PC users. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile launch actions hide manual repair|library profile editor saves preview changes|library duplicate action refreshes native profiles"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity game-log wording cleanup: the Activity Games rows now say `launch options`, `View game log`, `Waiting for game log`, and `log lines kept` instead of the more technical `launch settings`, `View output`, and retained/dropped output wording. Saved log receipts use the same log-line language while the copied text keeps stdout/stderr prefixes for support usefulness. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity game actions stay visible|native process log export refreshes exited process state"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover first-screen cleanup: the provider search box no longer opens with the hardcoded demo query `optimization`. Discover now starts with a blank search field and clear copy explaining that users can search or press Search empty to browse public packs, so the screen feels production-ready instead of preloaded with a test term. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover browse-action polish: the provider search button now says `Browse` when the search field is empty and switches back to `Search` only once the user types a query. This matches the native provider behavior where empty queries browse/trend public packs for Modrinth, CurseForge when configured, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover CurseForge search explains|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover FTB search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"` and `npm run build`.

Latest account diagnostics privacy cleanup: the Advanced Settings `Minecraft session` readout no longer exposes the raw Minecraft UUID. It now shows a friendly sign-in/expiry summary such as `Player - expires ...`, `Player - expired ... - refreshes automatically`, or `Not signed in`, keeping account identifiers out of normal troubleshooting UI. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|settings native save and clear-session failures"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover implementation confirmation: the Prism-style provider paths are present in this tree for CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic through shared search result DTOs, Tauri `search_discover_modpacks` / `install_discover_modpack`, and provider-specific launcher-core planners. This pass fixed the final stale Discover status copy so empty searches say `Browse` consistently, then reran the provider E2E matrix and core provider parser/install-plan tests. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, and `npm run build`.

Latest Microsoft sign-in error cleanup: renderer-facing account/session failures no longer expose raw OAuth refresh errors such as `Microsoft token exchange failed: invalid_grant`. The shared launcher-event/native-error sanitizer now rewrites token exchange, expired stored-session, missing-session, and missing-refresh-token failures into friendly sign-in guidance while preserving the existing recovery flow and raw native fixtures. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "Microsoft callback|launch diagnostics session recovery|launch sign-in recovery|successful offline launch clears stale sign-in recovery"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "authenticated launch command refresh failure offers sign-in recovery"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity process-status wording cleanup: Activity game rows no longer label normal exits as `Exited cleanly` or unexpected nonzero exits as `Crashed with exit code ...` in the main status pill. The visible status now says `Closed` or `Closed with an error`, while detailed event/log output still preserves exit-code information for support. User-requested stops continue to display `Stopped` and are guarded against being treated as errors. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface|user-stopped|native process log export refreshes exited process state|native process action failures preserve process state|exited native process row can relaunch"` and `npm run build`.

Latest first-run import onboarding slice: native desktop first-run now checks once for importable launcher profiles automatically when there are no saved profiles and no import candidates already loaded. It does not navigate or interrupt the user; it quietly populates `snapshot.imports`, updates status to the found count, and changes the Home/Library empty-state action from `Import profiles` to `Review found profiles` when candidates exist. Web preview and existing-profile users remain unchanged, and the manual Import screen still has `Find profiles` plus custom-folder review. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "empty launcher guides|empty native launcher checks|import screen exposes scan action|import screen plans"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Microsoft callback-tab cleanup: the local browser callback page shown after desktop Microsoft sign-in failures no longer echoes raw OAuth/token/configuration errors such as `invalid_grant` or `Microsoft token exchange failed`. Tauri now maps callback completion failures to plain browser-tab guidance (`sign in again from TheBoysLauncher` or `check the launcher setup`) while the launcher UI still receives the original native error for recovery handling and diagnostics. Verified with `cargo test -p theboyslauncher microsoft_callback -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest update-channel manifest hardening: the renderer now validates cross-channel update manifests with the same intent as the native opener instead of accepting any GitHub release URL prefix. Dev/stable channel manifests must point at a `TheBoysLauncher_*_x64-setup.exe` asset under the official GitHub releases path before the Settings install action is shown or opened; poisoned manifests that point back to JSON or other assets surface `Update manifest is missing a trusted installer URL` and expose no installer action. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings .*channel"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Technic Discover install-readiness cleanup: normal Technic browse/search now resolves exact pack metadata for visible non-installable rows in launcher-core and replaces them with install-ready direct-zip or Solder metadata when Technic exposes it. The frontend exact `#slug` fallback remains as a backup, but the normal Discover list should now feel more like the other providers instead of asking users to press `Check details` first. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest Technic Discover speed cleanup: Technic browse/search exact-metadata enrichment now runs in bounded chunks of four concurrent metadata checks instead of checking visible rows strictly one after another. This keeps the install-ready upgrade behavior from the previous slice while making normal Discover browsing feel less sluggish and avoiding an unbounded burst against Technic. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-technic-concurrency-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest hosted friends-service Settings cleanup: Advanced Settings no longer shows disabled `Start local service` / `Stop local service` controls when the launcher is configured for the hosted friends service. Hosted builds now show a single `Check friends` action in that slot, and the main connection label says `Friends offline` instead of `Hosted offline`, keeping deployment details out of the normal troubleshooting surface. Local/dev builds still expose Start/Stop local service. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|native startup prefers configured hosted|settings friends service action|settings native friends service|settings backend start"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover provider-state cleanup: the Prism-style Discover implementation is now reflected directly in the React UI instead of carrying old staged-rollout states. The provider card model no longer has `browse_only` / `coming_soon`, all six implemented catalogs render as install-ready providers (`CurseForge`, `Modrinth`, `ATLauncher`, `FTB`, `FTB Legacy`, `Technic`), and the sidebar catalog summary now includes FTB Legacy. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest sidebar status wording cleanup: the bottom-left launcher quick-status card now uses the same friendly operation state labels as Activity (`Done`, `Working`, `Needs attention`) instead of raw event kinds such as `completed`. This keeps completed setup/install progress from surfacing internal event enum text. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "Launcher quick status|live file download events surface|pack play setup refreshes|pending native install polls launcher events"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity operation-label cleanup: Activity rows and sidebar operation summaries now use more player-facing labels for common operations: `Start game` instead of `Launch profile`, `Import profiles` instead of `Import profile`, and `Set up Java` instead of `Install Java runtime`. Internal operation ids and native event plumbing are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library play action opens native process supervisor|activity event log sanitizes cached launch setup failures|activity event log sanitizes launch startup process failures|settings managed Java install runs"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity update-count wording cleanup: the visible Activity overview no longer says `launcher events` or counts operation rows as `events`. Refresh status now says `Loading activity updates` / `Loaded N activity updates`, and operation rows count `steps`, matching the existing latest-operation detail panel. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|activity overview sorts operations|native event log refresh failure preserves real activity state|live file download events surface"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity game-row timing cleanup: Activity > Games rows now show player-facing timing copy (`Ran for ...`, `Started ...`, `Closed ...`) instead of the previous lower-level `runtime ... - started ... - exited ...` line. The game-log export regression was also aligned with the current `log lines kept` wording. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity game actions stay visible|activity process row labels user-stopped|native process log export refreshes exited process state|library process log export opens|activity screen loads event log fallback"` and `npm run build` after rerunning the corrected log-export slice.

Latest Settings advanced-action declutter: Advanced Settings no longer shows `Check friends` inside the Java action group. Java now only exposes Java actions (`Find Java`, `Recommendations`), while the Friends service group owns `Check friends` plus local Start/Stop controls when applicable; hosted mode still shows only the hosted friends check. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|settings friends service action|settings native friends service|native startup prefers configured hosted"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Settings sign-out wording cleanup: the Advanced Settings account maintenance action now says `Sign out` instead of the vague `Clear`, and related status/fallback copy says `Signing out of Minecraft`, `Signed out of Minecraft`, or `Signing out is mocked in web preview` instead of exposing stored-session clearing language. The settings save fallback test now tolerates either a signed-in preview session or signed-out state while still asserting the raw UUID is not shown. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|settings save action falls back|settings native save and clear-session failures|settings native mode keeps"`, `npm run build`, and a narrow search confirming old `Clearing Minecraft session` / `Minecraft session cleared` text is gone.

Latest Java setup wording cleanup: Advanced Java install/recovery now uses player-facing status copy instead of managed-runtime implementation language. Missing manual Java inputs say `Choose a Java recommendation or enter a Java download URL first`, manual installs say `Preparing Java install`, preview fallback says `Java install is mocked in web preview`, and Java install Activity/sidebar messages render as `Java download queued`, `Java download finished`, `Java setup queued`, and `Java is ready` even when native events still carry runtime ids internally. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings Java discovery|settings native Java failures|settings managed Java install|settings Java recommendations|library play action surfaces native Java runtime recovery|missing managed Java executable|launch command Java preflight"`, `npm run build`, and a narrow search confirming old managed-Java status strings are gone.

Latest Import conflict-label cleanup: the Import review modal now shows beginner-friendly conflict choices `Skip`, `Replace`, and `Keep both` instead of raw internal values `skip`, `overwrite`, and `rename`. Conflict summaries now say matching files will be skipped, replaced, or kept as a new copy, while the underlying native payload still uses the existing typed resolution values. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen can review|import screen plans|native import disables"`, `npm run build`, and a narrow search confirming the old raw conflict labels are not visible in UI/test text.

Latest Discover provider-copy cleanup: normal Discover copy no longer mentions CurseForge API keys, provider manifests, or overrides. CurseForge is described as catalog access or direct zip export install, and provider setup copy now says the launcher reads pack instructions and copies included files before downloading/preparing Minecraft. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover archive form|discover CurseForge search explains|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install"`, `npm run build`, and a narrow Discover-facing search for old API-key/manifest/override wording.

Latest Discover provider speed cleanup: the Prism-style Discover paths are present for CurseForge, Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic. This pass made current FTB app-pack search/browse read pack details in bounded chunks of six concurrent requests instead of one-by-one, while preserving the same search filtering and install payloads. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install"`, `npm run build`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-discover-ftb-concurrency-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest update-channel copy cleanup: Settings dev/stable update checks keep the trusted GitHub installer URL validation, but renderer copy no longer talks about update manifests or signed installers. Failed channel metadata now says `Update information is missing a trusted launcher download`, available side-channel builds say installing opens `the launcher setup`, and install state says `Opening Dev/Stable build ...`. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings .*channel"`, `npm run build`, and a narrow search confirming the old `Update manifest...`, `signed installer`, and `installer is opening` phrases are gone from frontend/tests.

Latest HTTP-error wording cleanup: renderer-facing native errors that match `... failed with HTTP/status <code>` now become `... is unavailable right now. Try again later.` so normal Settings/Discover/Activity surfaces do not expose raw transport status codes. The update-channel failure path now has mocked-native Playwright coverage proving `HTTP 503` is hidden while the trusted-download validation remains intact. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings .*channel"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Java failure-copy cleanup: Settings Java failures no longer surface raw `Java runtime scan failed` or `Java recommendation manifest unavailable` wording in the desktop UI. The native-error sanitizer now maps them to `Java scan is unavailable right now. You can still use automatic Java when launching.` and `Java recommendations are unavailable right now. Try again later.` while preserving the same automatic Java recovery/install flows. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings Java discovery|settings native Java failures|settings managed Java install|settings Java recommendations|library play action surfaces native Java runtime recovery|missing managed Java executable|launch command Java preflight"` and `npm run build`.

Latest hosted session-routing cleanup: native hosted friends-service mode no longer falls back to `/dev/sessions` when no Minecraft-backed backend session is available. Local/web preview and local packaged service paths still use development sessions, and saved Minecraft sessions still upgrade cached dev sessions to Minecraft-backed authorization. Hosted native users without a usable Minecraft session now stay on local/preview social data until sign-in instead of calling a production-disabled dev endpoint. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native startup prefers configured hosted|hosted friends search|stored session upgrades cached dev backend session|stored session uses native token exchange|launching a profile can publish local presence|friends screen can send requests"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover provider implementation recheck: the Prism-style provider methods are not just placeholder UI in this tree. Tauri and launcher-core currently support real Discover search/install paths for `Modrinth`, `CurseForge`, `ATLauncher`, `FTB`, `FTB Legacy`, and `Technic`, using provider-specific search/parsing plus archive/install planners where each provider requires it. This pass re-verified the provider matrix and finished the pending profile editor server-address validation so users cannot save URL/path-style default server addresses that native launch would later reject. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result|library profile editor saves preview changes"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='target/codex-discover-confirm-check'; cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover preview-copy cleanup: Discover web-preview rows no longer describe provider results as `preview result`. The fallback rows now use normal user-facing pack descriptions for CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic while leaving native provider search/install behavior unchanged. Non-installable provider fallbacks also now say the source does not provide an automatic install instead of saying install support is `not ready yet`. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Technic browse result"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity file-progress accuracy cleanup: the Activity `Latest file progress` summary now counts each file by its latest state instead of counting every historical event for the same file. A file that started downloading and then finished no longer remains counted as both downloading and finished, which makes the Activity panel and sidebar progress less stale/confusing during large Minecraft or modpack installs. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|live file download events surface|pending native install polls launcher events|pending native install plan surfaces operation progress"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-create submit guard: the Library new-profile flow now has an immediate `Creating...` disabled state before the native `create_profile` command returns, so accidental repeated clicks cannot queue duplicate create/setup work. The automatic `prepare_profile` step and existing setup retry-on-play behavior are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|library new profile action falls back|profile creator falls back|library profile editor saves preview changes"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Microsoft callback timeout wording cleanup: renderer-facing Microsoft sign-in callback listener failures now show normal sign-in guidance instead of raw loopback/OAuth listener text. Callback timeouts render as `Microsoft sign-in timed out. Start sign-in again from TheBoysLauncher.`, and callback-listener startup failures render as guidance to close old sign-in tabs and retry. The Prism-style Discover implementation was also rechecked in the same pass: Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, and Technic provider paths are implemented through Tauri/native search/install calls and provider-specific launcher-core planners. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "launch diagnostics session recovery|authenticated launch refresh failure offers sign-in recovery"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, and `npm run build`.

Latest setup-retry wording cleanup: failed setup rows in Activity now label their retry action `Try play again` instead of the bare `Play`. The action still runs the existing automatic setup-then-launch path, but the UI no longer makes a failed setup row look like a plain launch button or exposes repair terminology. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "failed native repair surfaces|activity repair retry is disabled|home repair action shows pending native repair state|library profile launch actions hide manual repair"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile-editor save-state cleanup: the Library profile customizer now labels the hide-editor action `Close` instead of `Done`, so it does not imply unsaved edits were saved. When profile fields change, the edit action group now shows `Unsaved changes` for valid drafts or `Fix highlighted fields` when validation blocks Save. This keeps beginner-facing customization safer without changing the native update-profile contract. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor keeps profile values after native save failure|library profile editor syncs native refreshed profile values after save|library profile editor can choose snapshot"` and `npm run build`.

Latest CurseForge Discover fallback-copy cleanup: missing CurseForge catalog/search configuration no longer appears as `not configured in this build` in the player-facing Discover surface. The provider plan now says search works when available, and missing search availability tells users to paste a CurseForge zip link instead. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover CurseForge search explains|discover CurseForge search install|discover archive form"` and `npm run build`.

Latest import pending-state cleanup: first-run automatic import scanning and manual `Find profiles` now share the same `Looking...` disabled state, so users cannot start overlapping native scans. Import review planning also shows `Preparing...` and disables other scan/review actions while the native review plan is loading, including custom folder review. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen exposes scan action|import screen can review a custom folder path|native import scan disables duplicate scans|import screen plans a preview migration|native import disables duplicate submissions"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Microsoft sign-in pending-state cleanup: Microsoft sign-in start and manual callback completion now have explicit pending guards across the topbar, sidebar recovery action, Settings account card, Advanced sign-in actions, and account manager modal. Start actions show `Opening...`, callback completion shows `Finishing...`, repeated clicks do not start duplicate auth flows/token exchanges, and a successful local callback sign-in clears the stored auth flow so Settings no longer offers stale `Finish sign in`. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "sign in action waits|settings callback completion surfaces|settings shows Microsoft callback completion guard|sign in action surfaces"` and `npm run build`.

## Prompt For Next Session

Use this prompt in the new Codex session on the development PC:

```text
We are continuing TheBoysLauncher v4 rewrite on branch `codex/fresh-foundation-rewrite`.

Context:
- This is a fresh foundation for a social Minecraft launcher.
- Do not preserve the old Go/Fyne/Prism app as the architecture.
- Use Tauri v2 + React/TypeScript + Vite + Rust launcher core + Rust backend.
- The legacy Go/Fyne app remains in the repo root and is reference/migration material only.
- Read `docs/REWRITE_HANDOFF.md` first.

Current focus:
1. Keep pushing the working launcher happy path, not old scaffold work:
   - real catalog/packwiz install/update/repair;
   - Microsoft stored-session refresh and launch;
   - Java/Forge launch planning;
   - process supervision, stop, logs, and delete;
   - clear desktop UI progress/errors for 1100x720+.
2. Start with `git status --short --branch`, then read the newest "Latest ..." notes above.
3. Prefer focused Rust tests, Playwright launcher-shell tests, and live WinterPack smoke scripts. Recent verified commands include:
   - `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:metadata`
   - `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:plan`
   - `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:repair`
   - `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:delete`
   - `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:install`
   - `npm run smoke:live:winterpack:launch`
   - `npm run build`
   - `cargo fmt --all --check`
   - `cargo check --workspace`
4. Next useful slices are likely UI cleanup around install/repair/launch status, command/log discoverability, and any gaps between the Playwright shell and actual Tauri runtime behavior.

Keep edits focused on the new v4 foundation. Do not delete legacy code. Add tests with each launcher behavior change.
```

Latest FTB private-code Discover slice: the Prism-style Discover provider set now includes `FTB Private` as a separate provider for legacy private pack codes. Launcher-core can search `https://dist.creeper.host/FTB2/static/privatepacks/<code>.xml`, preserves the private code in the install project id, and reuses the existing FTB Legacy archive download/extract/install pipeline with the `privatepacks` CDN path. Tauri accepts `ftb_private` through `install_discover_modpack`, and React shows the provider with native install routing plus preview fallback. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, and `npm run build`.

Latest dev/stable update action cleanup: Settings update checks and installer launches now have code-level in-progress guards in addition to disabled buttons, so forced double-clicks cannot start overlapping update checks or open multiple dev/stable installers. The install action shows `Opening...` for cross-channel installer handoff and `Installing...` for current-channel update install while busy, and channel/check controls are disabled during that handoff. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings .*channel"` and `npm run build`.

Latest repair/setup wording cleanup: normal launcher surfaces no longer ask users to manually `Set up files` for repair-needed profiles. Repair-needed pack status now reads `Ready`, repair-profile operations render as `Game setup`, recovery actions use `Try play again`, and automatic profile/create/update/launch setup activity says `Preparing ...` / `Preparing game...` while keeping the same native `repair_profile` and automatic setup-before-launch behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "repair|setup|launch command preflight|stale repair|profile launch actions hide manual repair|activity screen loads event log fallback"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Friends action-state cleanup: the Friends screen now has explicit pending guards for hosted account search and friend-request sends. Search shows `Searching...`, disables until the request completes, and ignores forced repeat clicks; Add/Request shows `Sending...`, disables duplicate sends, and keeps request creation single-shot while the backend call is in flight. Empty Add and too-short Search are disabled before submission. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "friends screen|hosted friends|stored session.*backend|native stored session exchange|stored session friend join"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover provider recheck: the Prism-style Discover provider methods are already implemented in this tree for `Modrinth`, `CurseForge`, `ATLauncher`, `FTB`, `FTB Legacy`, `FTB Private`, and `Technic`, with React calling Tauri/native install paths and launcher-core doing provider-specific parsing/download/install planning. CurseForge search still uses the official keyed API path; public web search currently rejects launcher-style requests, while direct CurseForge zip installs and CurseForge archive planning remain supported. The pending new-profile wording cleanup is also verified: creation now says `Create profile` / `Preparing...` while still running automatic prepare/setup after creation. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "empty launcher guides|creating a profile refreshes|library new profile action falls back|library profile editor saves|library profile editor can choose snapshot|library profile editor syncs|library profile editor keeps"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, and `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`.

Latest Activity game-row detail cleanup: Activity > Games no longer shows raw `exited with code N` text in the normal game row. Closed games still show player-facing `Closed`, `Stopped`, or `Closed with an error`, and the exact exit code remains available in the collapsed `View technical details` panel for troubleshooting. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity process row labels|native process log export refreshes|activity game actions stay visible|native process action failures preserve process state|exited native process row can relaunch"` and `npm run build`.

Latest friend-join Java automation: friend/server joins now mirror the normal Play path when Java is missing or too old. A native join failure that requires a newer managed Java runtime automatically loads the recommendation manifest, installs the right Java, and retries the same server join once before showing any manual recovery action. The repaired-join retry path uses the same automatic Java handling after profile setup. The friend-join update fixture was also aligned with current hosted-presence behavior so it does not erase the local test roster during background presence refresh. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "friend join"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover/full-shell recheck: the Prism-style Discover paths are verified in the current tree for `Modrinth`, `CurseForge`, `ATLauncher`, `FTB`, `FTB Legacy`, `FTB Private`, and `Technic`. CurseForge full catalog search/install still depends on the official `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY`, while direct CurseForge zip export installs remain keyless. The full desktop Playwright shell suite is back to green after aligning stale assertions with the current Clean Dark UI copy, first-run import onboarding, live presence clearing, profile diagnostics/editor flow, and Activity progress labels. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core modrinth_discover_search_resolves_installable_archive -- --ignored --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings dev channel check opens|settings dev channel hides raw|launcher status card fits"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Discover unsupported-format/status cleanup: native Discover install failures for older ATLauncher, Technic, and FTB Legacy pack formats now render beginner-friendly messages like `This ATLauncher pack uses an older install format the launcher cannot set up automatically yet` instead of leaking raw `not supported by automatic install yet` errors. Native Activity auto-load is silent so it no longer overwrites important install/delete failure or success messages with `Loaded 0 activity updates`, while web preview still loads fallback activity rows. The snapshot-version profile-editor fixture was also completed with startup account/process mocks after it exposed a long-run timeout in the full shell suite. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover provider install failures|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover Technic search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover Modrinth search install"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads|activity active operation|native event log refresh|activity event log|activity overview|activity process row|native process log|library process log|native process action|exited native process|stopping a native process"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|library profile editor can choose snapshot versions from the manifest|library profile editor syncs native refreshed profile values after save|library profile editor keeps profile values after native save failure"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium`, and `npm run build`; the full desktop shell suite is freshly green at 150/150.

Latest Microsoft setup-error wording cleanup: renderer-facing Microsoft sign-in failures now also hide raw development/setup strings such as `THEBOYS_MICROSOFT_CLIENT_ID is required` and `local callback timed out`. Those paths render normal player-facing guidance (`Microsoft sign-in is not configured for this launcher build.` or `Microsoft sign-in timed out. Start sign-in again from TheBoysLauncher.`) while preserving the native command flow, manual callback completion guard, and token-refresh recovery behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "sign in action surfaces native Microsoft auth setup errors|settings callback completion surfaces native Microsoft token errors|Microsoft callback|authenticated launch command refresh failure offers sign-in recovery"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Settings failure-copy cleanup: Settings save/sign-out failures no longer expose raw storage/keychain internals such as `settings store is locked by another process` or `session keychain delete failed`. The renderer now maps those to normal recovery guidance telling the user to close another launcher window or restart the launcher before trying again, while the native commands and state-preservation behavior remain unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings native save and clear-session failures|sign in action surfaces native Microsoft auth setup errors|settings callback completion surfaces native Microsoft token errors"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest generic status-code wording cleanup: renderer-facing native/fetch failures now sanitize both `... failed with HTTP 503` and the shorter `... failed with 503` style used by social/catalog/update fetch paths.
