Update-channel check failures now throw the shorter shared shape internally, but Settings still shows only `Update check is unavailable right now. Try again later.` and hides the raw status code. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings dev channel hides raw HTTP update failures|settings native save and clear-session failures|sign in action surfaces native Microsoft auth setup errors|settings callback completion surfaces native Microsoft token errors"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Friends add-validation cleanup: the Friends `Add` action now uses the same minimum-name validation as Search. One-character names keep Search and Add disabled, and the submit handler still defensively shows `Enter at least 2 characters` if a form submit is forced, preventing accidental one-letter queued friend requests. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "friends screen starts|friends screen requires enough characters|friends screen can send requests"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest profile-create pending-state cleanup: the Library new-profile `Cancel` action is now disabled while profile creation or immediate automatic setup is in progress. This keeps the create form from disappearing while native `create_profile` / `prepare_profile` work is still running, matching the already-disabled `Creating...` action. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|profile creator falls back|library new profile action falls back"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover provider-link install slice: the Discover paste box now accepts supported provider page links in addition to direct CurseForge `.zip` and Modrinth `.mrpack` archives. Modrinth, ATLauncher, FTB, and Technic links are parsed into native provider install requests; ATLauncher and current FTB installs can omit a version id because launcher-core resolves the latest provider version from live metadata before planning the install. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover archive form|pasted ATLauncher provider link|discover ATLauncher search install"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core latest_version_resolves -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest profile-busy wording cleanup: defensive Play/Join guards no longer tell users to wait for a generic `launcher operation`. They now say the profile is already getting ready and tell the user to try Play/join again when it finishes, while the normal disabled-button state still prevents duplicate starts. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library play action is disabled while native launch is pending"`, a source search confirming the old guard wording is gone, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Activity fallback-label cleanup: unmapped or metadata-light Activity rows now use `Launcher task` instead of `Launcher operation`, keeping unknown native work from exposing implementation language. The web-preview Activity fixture includes a metadata-light row so the fallback stays covered and the old label is asserted absent. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Activity task-label cleanup: the Activity overview accessibility labels and visible section kicker now say `Launcher tasks` / `Latest task` instead of `Launcher operations` / `Latest operation`. Internal component/class names remain unchanged, but user-facing and screen-reader copy now matches the friendlier Activity language. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|activity overview sorts operations|library play action surfaces native launch preflight|authenticated launch refresh failure offers sign-in recovery"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover provider-link expansion: pasted CurseForge modpack pages now route through the native provider installer instead of requiring a direct zip export. React recognizes `curseforge.com/minecraft/modpacks/<slug>` and optional `/files/<fileId>` links, launcher-core resolves non-numeric CurseForge slugs through the official keyed CurseForge search API before fetching the selected/latest export, and provider-link names are cleaned up from slugs (for example `enigmatica9expert` becomes `Enigmatica 9 Expert`). FTB typed search now scans a broader live catalog slice in concurrent chunks so searches are less likely to miss packs outside the first few IDs. Verified with `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pasted CurseForge provider link|pasted ATLauncher provider link|discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest launch-details wording cleanup: the Advanced profile command preview no longer says `Diagnostics` or `launch diagnostics` in normal launcher UI. The action is now `Check launch`, the preview is labeled `Launch details`, copy/export actions say `Copy details`, and status messages say `Checking launch details`, `Launch details ready`, or `Copied launch details` while keeping the same native `build_launch_command` / `build_stored_authenticated_launch_command` flow. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "launch details|launch command preflight|profile editor saves preview changes"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming old launch-diagnostics wording is gone from `frontend/src/main.tsx` and `tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts`.

Latest game-stop wording cleanup: Activity game rows and launcher task summaries no longer show the internal `Stop requested` state to users. Stopping games now show `Stopping` in the status pill and `Stopping game` for the sanitized native event message, while the existing process registry state, disabled Stop button, and presence-clearing behavior remain unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|stopping a native process|activity process row labels"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming `Stop requested` remains only in raw native fixture input and the sanitizer pattern.

Latest global Java override cleanup: clearing the Advanced Settings `Global Java override` field now correctly returns the launcher to automatic Java instead of blocking Save with the awkward `blank automatic` validation message. The input stores blank as `undefined`, the existing save path sends automatic Java through to native settings, and the save status falls back to the normal `Save when you are done changing launcher preferences` guidance. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings global Java override|settings save status tracks privacy changes|settings render memory"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, and a source search confirming the old `blank automatic` message is gone.

Latest Discover pasted-provider hardening: the already-implemented Prism-style Discover providers were rechecked, and pasted provider-page installs now have broader regression coverage. The Discover URL parser normalizes Technic public page links such as `technicpack.net/modpack/hexxit.552552` down to the installable slug `hexxit` before sending the native provider request, while direct pasted FTB modpack pages are covered against the native `install_discover_modpack` path. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|FTB|Technic).*provider link|Technic dotted"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest home status-card wording cleanup: the home/sidebar quick status now uses `No tasks yet` instead of the last remaining `No operation yet` copy, keeping the Clean Dark shell aligned with the friendlier Activity `Launcher tasks` wording. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "home screen renders the social launcher shell"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Settings privacy wording cleanup: the normal Settings privacy card now says `Usage sharing` with `Usage sharing on/off` instead of presenting a technical `Diagnostics` toggle, and the Advanced readout is labeled `Advanced launcher status` with `Usage sharing On/Off`. The underlying `telemetryEnabled` setting and save behavior are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory|settings save status tracks privacy changes"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and a source search confirming no runtime Settings `Diagnostics` labels remain.

Latest profile server-address validation wording cleanup: the Library profile customizer no longer says a server address has `unsupported characters`. Invalid punctuation now explains what to enter instead: `Use only a normal server address, like play.example.com or 192.168.1.10.` The existing URL/path/space/port validation remains unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and a source search confirming the old phrase is gone.

Latest Microsoft sign-in wording cleanup: account-flow status messages now consistently say `Microsoft sign-in` instead of mixing in `Microsoft login`. Starting, opened/waiting, manual callback guard, completion, and web-preview fallback messages all use the same sign-in wording while the underlying command/function names remain unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "sign in action falls back|settings shows Microsoft callback completion guard|sign in action waits"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and a source search confirming the old runtime copy is absent from `frontend/src/main.tsx` and current Playwright assertions.

Latest Microsoft browser-open wording cleanup: native browser handoff failures such as `failed to open Microsoft login in browser` now pass through the renderer sanitizer and show `Microsoft sign-in` wording in the launcher status card. This covers the Windows shell-open failure path without changing the Tauri command boundary. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "sign in action (surfaces native Microsoft auth setup errors|hides native Microsoft browser-open login wording|falls back|waits)"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover provider reality check: the Prism-style provider methods are implemented in the current tree rather than deferred. Discover routes Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic through native `search_discover_modpacks` / `install_discover_modpack` paths, and launcher-core owns the provider-specific search, download-plan, archive extraction, and install-plan logic. This pass cleaned up the CurseForge provider card copy so pasted pack-page links are advertised alongside zip links, then re-verified the full Discover provider matrix. CurseForge catalog search still intentionally uses the official `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` path; pasted CurseForge page/zip installs route through the native provider installer. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result|pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|Modrinth|FTB|Technic).*provider link"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest Discover additional-source copy cleanup: the already-implemented FTB Legacy and FTB Private paths no longer sit behind `Experimental discover providers` wording in the React shell, and a later pass promoted both into the main provider grid. FTB Private still switches the search panel to `Look up a private pack`, labels the field `Private code`, and uses a `Look up` action instead of making private-code installs look like public catalog search. Native provider routing is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install"`, the full Discover provider matrix grep, and `npm run build`.

Latest profile customize micro-polish: the Advanced profile `Check launch` action now uses the existing `ShieldCheck` icon instead of the terminal icon, keeping launch-detail verification from looking like a command-line/log feature in the normal Library customize path. Behavior is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|launch details|launch command preflight|profile editor can choose snapshot|profile editor keeps profile values"` and `npm run build`.

Latest profile creation form cleanup: the New Profile flow now keeps Memory in the basic form alongside name/version choices, matching the existing profile customizer, while Loader remains hidden behind Advanced for users who know they need Fabric/Forge/NeoForge/Quilt. The Create Profile tests now assert memory is visible before Advanced and loader is not. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "creating a profile refreshes|profile creator falls back|library new profile action falls back|library profile editor saves preview changes"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Import review label cleanup: the Import review dialog now displays friendly item labels instead of raw item ids. `resource_packs` / `shader_packs` render as `Resource packs` / `Shader packs`, and the preview import fixture covers a resource-pack row so the UI cannot regress to code-shaped labels. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "import screen exposes|import screen can review|native import scan|import screen plans|native import disables"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover provider implementation recheck: the Prism-style Discover methods are implemented in the current tree, not deferred. React routes Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic through native `search_discover_modpacks` / `install_discover_modpack`; Tauri accepts those providers; launcher-core owns the provider-specific search, download-plan, archive extraction, and install planning. CurseForge full catalog search still depends on the official `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY`, while CurseForge page/zip installs remain native. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install|discover Technic search install|discover Technic browse result|pasted (ATLauncher|CurseForge|Modrinth|FTB|Technic).*provider link"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core search_results -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core install_plan -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher_ -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity file-progress context cleanup: the Activity `Latest file progress` summary now carries the current file event subject into the category formatter, so modloader setup downloads such as library jars show as `mod loader files` instead of generic `Minecraft libraries`. Sidebar/download status behavior is unchanged, but the Activity overview now uses the same context-aware categorization. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity screen loads event log fallback|activity active operation without percent|activity file progress uses mod loader context"` and `npm run build`.

Latest updater bundle verification hardening: `scripts/verify-tauri-bundles.mjs` now verifies updater manifests point to a trusted `github.com/dilllxd/theboyslauncher/releases/download/{dev-latest|v...}/...-setup.exe` release asset, not just a matching setup filename. The release-channel script suite includes a negative fixture proving a same-named installer under a lookalike GitHub owner is rejected before publish. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, and `npm run build`.

Latest hosted friends sign-in copy cleanup: hosted native friends actions that cannot use dev sessions now surface `Sign in to use friends.` instead of exposing `hosted friends` / Minecraft-session implementation wording. The session-recovery detector recognizes the new copy, and the shared sanitizer still rewrites older cached `Sign in to Minecraft to use hosted friends` messages. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "hosted friends search|hosted friend requests|native stored session exchange|stored session exchanges Minecraft identity|stored session upgrades cached dev backend session|authenticated launch refresh failure offers sign-in recovery"`, `npm run social:check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover concurrency cleanup: the Prism-style Discover providers remain implemented for Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic, and Discover installs no longer get disabled just because an unrelated profile install/setup/launch task is running. The Discover paste/search install actions now only block on an active Discover install, so users can keep browsing/installing packs while another profile task is pending. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover installs stay available|discover shows provider plan|discover all sources"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Discover private-code shortcut: All sources still searches only public provider catalogs by default, but a user can now type `private:<code>`, `ftb private <code>`, `ftb-private <code>`, or `ftb_private <code>` into the All sources search box to route directly to the FTB Private lookup/install path. This keeps normal aggregate search clean while making the Prism-style private FTB method reachable without first selecting the provider card. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover Modrinth search install|discover CurseForge search install|discover ATLauncher search install|discover FTB search install|discover FTB Legacy search install|discover FTB Private code install|discover Technic search install|discover all sources"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core all_discover_search_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest automatic setup concurrency cleanup: profile Play no longer silently fails when the selected profile needs automatic setup while an unrelated pack install/update is already running. The hidden `repair_profile` setup path now blocks only the same profile, running profile processes, or another active profile setup/repair, and it avoids overwriting an unrelated lifecycle ref. This preserves automatic setup-before-launch while allowing the user to keep launching/setting up other profiles during a separate pack update. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library can (launch|set up and launch) another profile while a pack update is installing|library play action is disabled while native launch is pending"` and `npm run build`.

Latest Activity recovery concurrency cleanup: Activity failed-launch and failed-setup recovery buttons no longer disable just because an unrelated launcher task is running. They still block when the target profile is running or already being set up, but `Try play again` can now run the existing profile-specific automatic setup/launch path while another pack update is pending. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "activity failed launch recovery works|library can set up and launch another profile while a pack update is installing|failed native repair surfaces|activity repair retry"` and `npm run build`.

Latest hosted friends search cleanup: native hosted Friends search now requires a Minecraft-backed session instead of falling back to preview roster data. Without sign-in it clears stale search results, sets the normal sign-in recovery action, shows `Sign in to search friends`, and still avoids `/dev/sessions`, matching the already-hardened hosted friend-request path. Web preview fallback behavior remains available outside native hosted mode. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "hosted friends search requires|hosted friend requests require|stored session upgrades cached dev backend session|stored session exchanges Minecraft identity"` and `npm run build`.

Latest updater manifest hardening: `scripts/verify-tauri-bundles.mjs` now rejects updater manifest URLs with query strings or fragments, in addition to requiring the exact trusted GitHub release owner/repo/tag path and setup filename. The release-channel script suite includes a negative fixture proving a same-repo setup URL decorated with `?download=1` is rejected before publish, while generated dev manifests with encoded spaces still pass. Verified with `npm run test:release-channels`, `npm run verify:tauri-security`, and `npm run build`.

Latest native shell channel-label cleanup: the launcher sidebar no longer brands every native build as `Desktop alpha`. The shell now shows `Stable desktop` or `Dev desktop` from `VITE_THEBOYS_RELEASE_CHANNEL`, while web mode still says `Web preview`, so stable packaged builds do not look like unfinished alpha builds. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native shell shows|home screen renders|settings exposes stable"` and `npm run build`.

Latest native preview-fallback cleanup: desktop/native fallback paths no longer describe unavailable hosted/local social or pack services as `preview data`. Failed native friend search clears result rows and shows `Friend search is unavailable right now` instead of showing fake preview roster matches, and native pack catalog/detail/presence fallbacks use local/unavailable wording while browser preview keeps its mock-data copy. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "native home details fallback|home details action falls back|native friends search failure|friends screen requires enough"` and `npm run build`.

Latest friends-service action guard: Advanced Settings friends-service controls now have a real in-progress guard for status/start/stop actions. The active control shows `Checking...`, `Starting...`, or `Stopping...`, all service controls are disabled while a native service action is pending, and forced repeat clicks cannot queue duplicate `start_social_backend` calls. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings backend start waits|settings friends service action|native startup prefers configured hosted"` and `npm run build`.

Latest launch-details fallback wording cleanup: the remaining browser-preview fallback strings for the hidden Advanced profile launch check now say `Launch details require the desktop app` / `Signed-in launch details require the desktop app` instead of `launch diagnostics`, matching the current Clean Dark UI direction and keeping diagnostics wording out of normal profile flows. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library launch details action explains desktop app requirement|stored session switches launch actions"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest file-progress completion wording cleanup: completed download/setup events now render as `Files are ready.` instead of the bland `File download completed`, while active file progress still uses category-level messages like `Downloading Minecraft assets` or `Downloading mod loader files` and hides finished 100% download events from the sidebar progress bar. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "live file download events surface|activity screen loads event log fallback|activity file progress uses mod loader context"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest native task-lock wording cleanup: renderer-facing native lifecycle gate failures no longer expose `another launcher lifecycle operation is already running` or `launcher lifecycle operation lock poisoned`. They now render as `Another launcher task is already running. Try again when it finishes.` or a restart-if-repeated busy message through the shared native-error sanitizer, covering Discover/import/setup/install races without changing the Rust gate behavior. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover provider install failures"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest automatic-Java override guidance: Advanced Settings and Advanced profile customization now explicitly say to leave Java override fields blank for automatic Java selection. Normal profile screens still hide Java controls, while the optional advanced overrides remain available for power users. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "library profile editor saves preview changes|settings global Java override"`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest CurseForge provider-link unlock: pasted plain CurseForge modpack pages such as `https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/modpacks/enigmatica9expert` now route to the native Discover installer instead of asking the user to find a specific file page. Exact `/files/<id>` links still preserve the file id, while plain pages send no version id so launcher-core can resolve the latest downloadable export through the existing CurseForge provider path. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "plain CurseForge pack page|pasted CurseForge provider link|discover shows provider plan"`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm run build`, `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`, and `cargo fmt --all --check`.

Latest profile action status cleanup: Library profile Play buttons no longer collapse active same-profile work into the generic `Busy` label. Updating, automatic setup, deletion, and save states now show `Updating...`, `Setting up...`, `Deleting...`, or `Saving...` directly on the disabled profile action, keeping normal Library feedback aligned with the sidebar/Activity progress language. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pending native install plan surfaces operation progress"`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "home repair action shows pending native repair state"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Home/pack action status cleanup: Home primary pack actions and pack cards/details now use the same specific in-progress labels as Library instead of falling back to generic `Busy` while a pack is updating or being set up. The shared lifecycle fallback is now `Working...`, the main Home button shows `Updating...` or `Setting up...` during pending native work, and regression coverage asserts the generic busy label is absent from those normal surfaces. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "pending native install plan surfaces operation progress|home repair action shows pending native repair state"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest CurseForge plain-page install cleanup: the Prism-style provider matrix remains implemented for Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, FTB, FTB Legacy, FTB Private, and Technic. This pass removed the remaining API-key dependency from pasted plain CurseForge pack-page installs by adding a keyless latest-export download plan for `https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/modpacks/<slug>`; exact `/files/<id>` links still preserve the file id, and full CurseForge catalog search still uses the official keyed API. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "plain CurseForge pack page|pasted CurseForge provider link|CurseForge project file"`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover provider-copy cleanup: Discover preview/provider rows no longer describe CurseForge installs as going through an `automatic zip importer`, and non-installable provider rows now say the launcher cannot prepare the pack automatically instead of talking about a source not providing an automatic install. This is copy-only; native provider routing is unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest CurseForge keyless Discover fallback: CurseForge Discover search now uses the official keyed API when `THEBOYS_CURSEFORGE_API_KEY` is configured, but falls back to keyless public-page results when it is not. Browse/search can surface known installable CurseForge packs, typed slugs are converted into installable public-page suggestions, and pasted CurseForge pack-page URLs in the search box resolve to the same native provider install path. The Discover copy no longer says CurseForge search is only available sometimes. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_keyless -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core curseforge_ -- --test-threads=1`, `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "discover shows provider plan|discover CurseForge search install"`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest profile setup-fallback copy cleanup: after profile creation or version/loader changes, the launcher still automatically prepares game files first, but if that native setup attempt fails the sidebar no longer says `Press Try play again to finish setup.` It now says the profile was created/updated but game setup needs another try, while preserving the existing `Try play again` recovery action. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "profile creator explains setup can finish|library profile editor keeps profile values after native save failure"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Settings storage placeholder cleanup: Settings storage readouts now format web-preview fallback directories as `Available in desktop app` instead of leaking `Native data/log directory unavailable in web preview` into the normal Settings and Advanced status screens. Real native paths are unchanged. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "settings render memory"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest first-run import failure copy cleanup: the native first-run automatic import scan still runs once when there are no saved profiles, but if that background check fails it now says `Could not check for existing profiles automatically` instead of the misleading `Import scan skipped`. The Home empty state stays on `Import profiles` and does not show fake candidates. Verified with `npm exec playwright -- test tests/e2e/launcher-shell.spec.ts --project desktop-chromium --grep "empty native launcher checks|empty native launcher explains"`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.

Latest Discover provider-link search routing: the Prism-style provider methods are still implemented locally, and Discover search now recognizes pasted provider-page/install links directly in the search box instead of treating them as literal search text. The Rust search layer normalizes Modrinth, CurseForge, ATLauncher, current FTB, FTB Legacy CDN zip, Technic page, and `curseforge://install` links into installable native Discover results with preserved version/file ids where present; All sources routes recognized links to the matching provider immediately. Verified with `cargo test -p launcher-core discover_provider_query -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core discover -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core atlauncher -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core ftb -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p launcher-core technic -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo fmt --all --check`, `npm run build`, and `cargo check -p theboyslauncher`.
